# Steps M15-M17: Archwater, the midpoint, home households I, Threemills and the Tower unlock

Issue-ready specs for S-117 to S-151 (plus the splits listed in [Index changes](#index-changes)). Each step below is
written so the plan job can paste it into one `agent:improvement` issue: the title is the step heading after the
id, the six sections map to the issue's `## Why`, `## What`, `## Acceptance criteria`, `## Verification plan`,
`## Out of scope` and `## Risk & rollback` (the `#### Assets` block goes inside `## What`).

Order of authority: `docs/roadmap/CANON-NOW.md` LOCKED > `docs/roadmap/CANON.md` > the reconciliation
rows in `docs/roadmap/steps/*.md` (for the topics they name, until the named close-out edits the losing file) > the
bible (`docs/roadmap/bible/*.md`) > the INDEX row > the issue. Where two bible files disagree, this file picks one and says so in
[Bible reconciliations used by these steps](#reconciliations); an implementer follows the step, and the close-out step
of the milestone copies the reconciliation into the bible file that lost.

Links are relative to `docs/roadmap/steps/`: bible files are `../bible/<file>.md#<anchor>`.

**Conventions every step here uses** (do not repeat them in the issue; link this section):

- **Find earlier-step code by grep, not by guess.** Steps before M15 were specified in parallel; where this file
  names a module from an earlier step (`js/realms/index.js`, `js/realms/coverage.js`, `js/gates.js`, `js/beacons.js`,
  `js/quests.js`, `js/scenes.js`, `js/dialogue.js`, `js/content/looks.js`, `js/boss/defs.js`,
  `js/realms/dungeon-compile.js`, `js/dungeon-mod.js`, `js/travel.js`, `js/content/economy.js`,
  `js/content/flags.js`, `tools/realm-stats.mjs`), the implementer first runs the grep given (for example
  `grep -rn "placeIsleChests" js/`) and uses the real path and export names. If a named helper does not exist,
  the step builds the smallest version that the spec needs and says so in the PR body.
- **Pinewhistle is the template.** M13 (S-095 to S-109) built the land template: the `ridges` recipe, the isle
  finds passes (`placeIsle*`, bible atlas-isles#passes), the coverage test, the realm module that registers music and
  ambience, the people/depths/Warden quest shape (`mq-pinewhistle`), the kindle hand-off from the dungeon's last room
  to the overworld, the plaza visitor, the land's chapter test. Every Archwater and Threemills step copies the
  matching Pinewhistle file's structure and changes only data. "As Pinewhistle did" in a step means: open the
  Pinewhistle file of the same kind and mirror it.
- **Text.** Every new string is a text id in `js/text/en/<realm>.js` (`archwater.*`, `threemills.*`,
  `home.*`, `side.ssNN.*`), at most 220 characters, `{tokens}` from `js/hud/keys.js` only, `~` only in Miso's lines,
  `!!` only in Kinako's lines, and Pip never speaks. Dialogue entries live in `js/content/dialogue/<realm>.js`, quests in
  `js/content/quests/<realm>.js`, scenes in `js/content/scenes/<realm>.js`, NPC looks in `js/content/looks.js`.
- **Save.** No step in M15-M17 changes the save schema. The save stays at **v9** (from S-071). New state goes in
  `S.flags`, `S.items`, `S.quests`, `S.collect`, `S.chests`, `S.secrets`, `S.bosses`, `S.plushies`, `S.rematches`,
  `S.travel`, `S.bestiary`, `S.dungeons`, with the id rules of [../bible/systems.md#ids](../bible/systems.md#ids).
  Every new flag key or family is registered in `js/content/flags.js` in the same PR (the content lint fails
  otherwise).
- **Invariants.** Every step's acceptance criteria include "Invariants: paste the block from
  [../bible/charter.md#invariants-block](../bible/charter.md#invariants-block) and keep the lines named". The
  planner pastes the block; the lines to keep are named per step.
- **Machine.** Before any browser run: `free -h` (want ≥ 6 GB available); one browser at a time; stop every server
  and browser started. In-page and chapter runs are `node tests/inpage.mjs <suite>` and `node tests/chapter.mjs <id>`.
- **Art.** Painted images are generated inside the step by a GPT Sol subagent (T3 orchestrator `delegate_task`,
  Codex `image_gen.imagegen`) or a Gemini model, with the fixed preamble of
  [../bible/art-audio.md#preamble](../bible/art-audio.md#preamble) + the kind suffix of
  [../bible/art-audio.md#kinds](../bible/art-audio.md#kinds) + the subject given in the step. Raw generations are
  never committed; the prompt and the processing command are appended to `docs/art/prompts.md`. Every key gets a
  `realm` tag and a fallback. 3D is code-built (`js/models.js`, `js/props.js`, `js/toon.js`, rig families from
  [../bible/cast.md](../bible/cast.md)) with the ART BRIEF pasted in the PR body; GLB slots are added to
  `assets/manifest.js` **commented out** with their height.

<a id="m15"></a>

## M15 · Archwater and the midpoint

**Goal.** The second Act II isle, built from the Pinewhistle template: the `basalt` recipe with a two-state tide, the
Raft and its otters, three new species, the Tide Bell (and the home and Duskwick finds it opens), the 14-room
Undertow Grotto, Old Gloop, the Archwater main chain, II.M (the Lamp Thief names himself Mote on whichever isle is
relit second) and Archwater's three Hollows.

**Entry criteria.** M14 closed (S-116 merged or stacked); Pinewhistle unhidden with its chapter test green;
`node tests/chapter.mjs ch-pinewhistle` passes; the save is v9.

**Exit criteria.**
- `REALMS.archwater.hidden === false`; its prints (both tides), coverage (both tides), solver and realm-suite views pass.
- `undertow_grotto` and the three `hollow_archwater_*` realms compile, solve and pass the realm suite.
- Chapter tests `ch-archwater`, `iim-pine-arch` and `iim-arch-pine` pass in CI.
- The Tide Bell opens the Storm Cave and two tide caches on home and Duskwick's lagoon chest.

**What the player can do at the end.** Sail to Archwater, find the Raft beached on the mud, climb the Watch Column,
drain the Great Tidepool and thread the Kelp Maze for three pearls, open the Undertow Grotto, flip its basins with
conch shells and then with the Tide Bell, calm Old Gloop, kindle the arch beacon and watch the tide come home. On the
second beacon (Pinewhistle or Archwater, whichever comes second) Mote lands on the beacon, names himself, and every
villager has a new line. At home the Tide Bell opens the Storm Cave and two rock-pool caches.

**Playtime added.** +4.5 h (Archwater 4.0 h main chain including the dungeon, 0.25 h Hollows, 0.25 h home tide
finds and II.M). Cumulative 22.5 h.

**Save-version plan.** None. Save stays v9. New flags: `aw:*` family, `tide:duskwick` (toggle), `gate:home:storm-cave`
and `gate:home:tide-cache-1..2` (R24), `gate:archwater:*`, `moteNamed`, `scene:*`; items `tide_bell`, `pearl`;
`S.dungeons.undertow_grotto`; boss key `undertow_grotto:gloop`; `collect.hollow` keys `archwater:1..3`.

<a id="m16"></a>

## M16 · Archwater side stories and home households I

**Goal.** Make Archwater and the village feel lived in: four Archwater side stories (Clover's Map, the Raft Choir,
Eight Socks, the Moon Pool), Gloop's Juggling and Lagoon Lights, Dash courses on Duskwick and Archwater, Rook's race
part 2, and three home households: Granny Mochi (ss-03 part 1), Hazel Thimble (ss-05 and buns) and the Quackenbushes
(ss-06 and the Duck Regatta).

**Entry criteria.** M15 closed; `beacon:archwater` reachable in `ch-archwater`; S-112 (Dash framework) and S-113 (Rook
framework) merged.

**Exit criteria.** Quests `ss-23`, `ss-24`, `ss-25`, `ss-26`, `ss-03` (steps 1-3), `ss-05` (Duskwick, Pinewhistle and
Archwater deliveries), `ss-06`; activities `act-juggling`, `act-lagoon-lights`, `act-regatta`, Dash courses 3 and 4 and
Rook part 2 complete in in-page tests; `docs/roadmap/briefs/batch-07.md` written.

**What the player can do at the end.** Take Clover exploring and follow her chalk arrows, find the Raft's shanty at low
tide and hear the choir at dusk, carry Gloop's eighth sock back from Frostbun's ear, set three tide stones so the moon
swims in the Moon Pool at midnight, juggle knitted urchins with Gloop, float lanterns on Duskwick's lagoon, race two
new Dash courses and Rook again, meet Granny Mochi and carry the twins' first letter, buy Hazel's buns and deliver one
to three isles, and herd five ducks through the Regatta.

**Playtime added.** +3.5 h. Cumulative 26.0 h.

**Save-version plan.** None (v9). New: `ss23.*` to `ss26.*`, `ss03.*`, `ss05.*`, `ss06.*`, `act.juggle.best`,
`act.lagoon.done`, `act.regatta.best` flags; items `bun`, `bun_parcel`, `clover_map_1`, `kinako_box`, `mochi_letter`,
`sock` and the ingredient key items; `collect.dash` entries `dash:duskwick`, `dash:archwater` (best time in tenths of a second, integer 1-36000; systems#save-plan `dash:<realm>`).

<a id="m17"></a>

## M17 · Threemills; the Tower unlocks

**Goal.** The third Act II isle: the `downs` recipe (hedgerows, fields, millpond), the Hayburys, Goody Gander and
Pennywhistle, three species, the Thistle Hook (and the three home hook finds), the 12-room Granary Vaults, Lady
Honksworth, the Threemills main chain, the third-beacon beat that opens the Lamplighters' Tower, Threemills' Hollows,
the six-order test and performance checkpoint 4.

**Entry criteria.** M16 closed; `act2` orders Pine→Arch and Arch→Pine green.

**Exit criteria.**
- `REALMS.threemills.hidden === false`; prints (both tides), coverage, solver and realm-suite views pass.
- `granary_vaults` and `hollow_threemills_1..3` compile, solve and pass the realm suite.
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch-threemills` and all six `act2-order-*` chapters pass; `towerOpen` is set exactly once, by Granny
  Wick's scene after the third isle beacon (whichever isle is third).
- Perf checkpoint 4 recorded in `docs/roadmap/progress.md`: every shipped realm's 4 views in both cameras under budget;
  a 10× swap soak with no leak.

**What the player can do at the end.** All three Act II isles in any order. On Threemills: hear why the mills stopped,
win the Hoist Key back from the vole gang, creak the Old Sluice's wheels open in order, lift the hay off the Granary door, ride gear-bridges
and fill pits with hay bales in the Vaults, find the Thistle Hook, calm Lady Honksworth, kindle the sun-lamp and watch
the sails turn and the wheat go gold. At home the Hook reaches the Stairfoot route, the Cap Ledge on Grumble's Crown
and the Gull's Nest on the lighthouse. After the third beacon, Granny Wick says the old lamp can wake and the Tower door
is unlocked (she waits at it, "Not yet, dear", until the Tower's floors ship in M19).

**Playtime added.** +4.5 h. Cumulative 30.5 h.

**Save-version plan.** None (v9). New: `tm:*` family, `tide:threemills` (toggle), `towerOpen` (already in the
registry), `gate:threemills:hoist`; items `hook`, `hoist_key`; `S.chests` `home:hook1`, `home:hook2` and
`threemills:c1..c14`; `S.dungeons.granary_vaults`; boss key `granary_vaults:honksworth`; `collect` ids `threemills:*`.
