<a id="s-117"></a>

### S-117 · feat: Archwater basalt recipe with a two-state tide (hidden)

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-096 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.archwater.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-card](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-card),
[#archwater-recipe](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-recipe), [#archwater-raft](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-raft),
[#archwater-regions](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-regions),
[../bible/systems.md#realm-registry](../bible/systems.md#realm-registry),
[../bible/art-audio.md#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes)

#### Why

M15's first brick: the rock isle east-south-east of home, built from the Pinewhistle land template, with the one thing
no land has yet: a **two-state tide**. Archwater's whole puzzle (beached Raft, tide flats, pearl chain, Undertow
Grotto, the Tide Bell) hangs on water that sits at 0 m or −1.4 m. This step gives the World a water level that can
change, generates the land in both states and freezes both prints. Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-117 (M15
Archwater and the midpoint); vision item **Archwater and the midpoint**.

#### What

1. **Water level on every World (generic, home-identical).** `grep -n "hAt(x, z) < -0.4" js/world-collide.js` finds
   `walkable()` and `walkF()`. Replace the literal sea level with an instance field:
   `if (this.hAt(x, z) < this.waterY - 0.4) return false;` and `(this.hAt(x, z) - this.waterY + 0.4) * 2` in `walkF`.
   Set `this.waterY = 0` in the `World` constructor (`js/world.js`). Home never changes it, so
   `tests/fixtures/world-prints.json` and every home test stay identical.
2. **Tide decks.** A bridge record may carry `tide: { low: <deck h>, high: <deck h> }` (and `tilt` for drawing only).
   `bridgeAt()` (find with `grep -n "bridgeAt(x, z)" js/world-collide.js`) returns `b.tide[this.tideState]` for such
   decks; a record without `tide` is unchanged. Add `this.tideState = 'high'` in the constructor.
3. **Pure tide helper `js/realms/tide.js`** (no Three.js; `bun test` imports it):
   - `tideState(S, realmDef)` → `'low' | 'high'`. Reads `S.flags[realmDef.tide.flag]`: `0` → low, `1` → high; absent →
     `realmDef.tide.absent`, which is `'low'`, `'high'`, or `{ until: '<flag>', then: 'high', else: 'low' }`.
     Archwater: `{ flag: 'tide:archwater', low: -1.4, high: 0, absent: { until: 'beacon:archwater', then: 'high', else: 'low' } }`.
   - `applyTide(W, state)`: sets `W.tideState = state`, `W.waterY = W.tide[state]`, recomputes `W.walk` and `W.reach`
     with the shared `postReach()` from S-016 (`grep -rn "postReach" js/`), from the realm's arrival spawn. Returns the
     list of cells that changed walkability (S-122 uses it to lift Pip).
   - On realm entry the realm manager calls `applyTide(W, tideState(S, REALMS[id]))` before placing Pip, and sets the
     water mesh's `position.y = W.waterY` (no tween yet; S-122 adds the tween and the bell).
4. **Registry entry** in `js/realms/index.js` (copy the `pinewhistle` entry's shape):
   `archwater: { id: 'archwater', name: 'Archwater', kind: 'overworld', size: 512, seed: 40231, recipe: 'basalt',
   palette: 'archwater', music: 'archwater', ambience: 'archwater_coast', hidden: true, open: 'flag:ch8',
   arrive: 'jetty', bundle: 'archwater', enemies: 'archwater', dash: true, benches: 5, rootgate: true,
   tide: { … as above … }, views: [ … four below … ] }`. Spawns `jetty` (−206, 12) facing 90°, `grotto` (128, −62)
   facing 270°, `rootgate` (−56, 104) facing 292°.
5. **The recipe `js/realms/recipes/basalt.js`**, a `RealmWorld` subclass like `ridges.js`, every stage on its own
   `new RNG(40231 * k + c)` stream. Build exactly atlas-isles#archwater-recipe items 1-10 with these numbers:
   - Outline: high-water shore radius `176 + 16·fbm(θ; 3 octaves, 4 cycles)`. The Raft bay: ellipse centre
     (−178, 10), rx 46, rz 56, open to the west (the shore line is cut where x < −214), floor h −1.0.
   - Tide flats outside the high-water shore: h −0.2 at the shore to −1.3 at 18 m out, then to −4 beyond. On bearings
     20°-110° the band is 48 m wide (same fall over 48 m).
   - Plateau: centre (−20, −50), `base = 30·(1 − d/150)^1.3 + fbm(4 m)`, quantised `h = 2.4·floor(base/2.4)`; risers
     over 0.85 m are cliffs (mark the cells `W.cliff[i] = 1` for S-118a's column stamps). Top terrace h 30.
   - Column stairs: every path crossing a riser gets a ramp of 0.6 m steps (each step 1.2 m long) cut into the riser.
   - Arch ground: flatten a disc r 6 at the west leg (130, −70) to h 6 (blend 10 m) as the "arch shoulder"; the East
     Stack r 9 at (170, −70), top h 6, with a ramp of fallen column steps (0.6 m steps) on its west side down to the
     flats; the Moon Pool basin r 12 at (150, −70), floor −2.0; the Spire Stack (196, −84) r 4, top h 6, sides slope
     > 1.2 (unclimbable at any tide).
   - The grotto ledge: flatten 4 × 3 m at (134, −64) to h 1.2; the grotto stair: a ramp of 0.6 m steps from the arch
     shoulder's north edge (122, −58) down to the ledge. Both reachable at both tides.
   - Tidepool Shelf, bearings 110°-230°: a low shelf h 0.2-1.6; 12 real pool basins (r 2-4, floor −0.6) placed on stream
     `40231 * 3 + 7`; the Great Tidepool r 7, floor −2.0, at (50, 130) with three rim slots (notches 1.2 m wide at
     bearings 0°, 120°, 240° from its centre); the Spouter dome stamped into H at (90, 120): `12·(1 − (d/10)²)^0.8`
     for d < 10.
   - Kelp Shallows: the Kelp Maze area 36 × 36 m centred (190, 10) on the flats is left flat at h −1.1 (S-118a puts
     the kelp walls on it); sea caves cut into the NE cliff: Gull Cave mouth (110, −140), Echo Cave mouth (60, −170),
     each a 10 m deep, 4 m wide tunnel, floor h 0.6, marked `W.dark` (region "dark zone" for spawns and the Lantern).
   - The Pool Steps: a basin at (−120, −110) r 14, floor −1.6, connected to the sea by a 4 m channel to the north
     shore; the column top at (−128, −118) h 1.6 (a hexagon 2.4 m across, sheer sides) for chest-8.
   - Paths (2.4 m, splat `dirt` slot = `archwater_shingle`), all at `h ≥ 0.2` (the path stamp lifts low cells to 0.2):
     **W1** (−176, −52) → (−130, −40) → (−80, −50) → (−40, −56) → (−20, −50); **W2** (−20, −50) → (40, −50) →
     (100, −50) → (122, −58); **W3** (−10, −40) → (10, 0) → (30, 40) → (40, 80) → (70, 104) → (90, 108) → (52, 120);
     **W4** (−172, 66) → (−110, 80) → (−60, 110); **W5** (−176, −52) → (−150, −90) → (−120, −100) → (−60, −120) →
     (−80, −150).
   - **The Raft and its walks as decks** (visuals come in S-118b; walkability is here so the spawn is real): the
     landing pier, fixed deck h 1.2, from (−222, 12) to (−195, 12), 3 m wide; a 6 m gangway deck from the pier's east
     end to raft 7 whose east end follows the raft (`tide` low −0.2 / high 0.6). Fourteen raft decks (each `tide:
     { low: -0.2, high: 0.6 }`, `tilt` up to 4° at low for drawing only):

     | # | Centre | Size (x × z m) | Role |
     |---|---|---|---|
     | 1 | (−166, 6) | 10 × 8 | main raft: Rafferty's Mast, the cabins door (−162, 2) |
     | 2 | (−180, 0) | 8 × 6 | cabin "tilt" |
     | 3 | (−150, 20) | 8 × 6 | cabin "hammock" |
     | 4 | (−172, 30) | 8 × 6 | cabin "choir note" |
     | 5 | (−146, −6) | 8 × 6 | cabin "smell" |
     | 6 | (−190, 24) | 8 × 6 | cabin "small otter" |
     | 7 | (−188, 12) | 8 × 6 | landing raft (gangway) |
     | 8 | (−178, 18) | 6 × 6 | flowerpot raft (acorn-1 at (−170, 14) sits on raft 1's west edge) |
     | 9 | (−158, 8) | 6 × 6 | open deck |
     | 10 | (−160, 28) | 6 × 6 | drying racks |
     | 11 | (−170, −6) | 6 × 6 | north raft (the North Walk starts here) |
     | 12 | (−156, −2) | 6 × 6 | open deck |
     | 13 | (−186, 34) | 8 × 6 | net-shed raft (chest-3 lies on the mud south of it) |
     | 14 | (−140, 10) | 6 × 6 | east raft |

     Joins: a 1.2 m rope-and-plank deck between rafts along the edges of a minimum spanning tree over raft centres,
     plus one extra join raft 1 ↔ raft 9 (a loop). The **North Walk**: fixed deck h 1.2, 2.4 m wide, from raft 11's
     north edge to the bay north shore (−176, −52), with a tide ramp at the raft end. The **South Walk**: the same from
     raft 4's south edge to the south shore (−172, 66). The **Pool Steps pontoons**: six tide decks, hexagons 2.4 m
     across, `tide: { low: -1.2, high: 0.2 }`, in a line from the basin rim (−112, −104) to the column top (−128, −118)
     with gaps ≤ 1.0 m. The **floating log** at Barnacle Ledge (20, 170): a 6 × 1 m tide deck `{ low: -0.6, high: 0.8 }`
     1.4 m below the ledge top (the ledge top is h 2.2, a rock shelf 3 × 3 m at (20, 172)).
   - Coordinate checks the recipe must satisfy (asserted by the test): the Watch Column site (−20, −60) is on the top
     terrace (h 30) with a clear disc r 6 (reserved for S-118b); the arch shoulder, East Stack and Moon Pool as above;
     `W.reserved` discs for every landmark and the Kelp Maze square.
6. **Regions** (`W.regions`, the S-027 table shape), rule exactly atlas-isles#archwater-regions: `raft` if within 40 m
   of the bay ellipse or the landing; else `basalt` if high-water h ≥ 8; else by bearing `kelp` [20°, 110°),
   `tidepool` [110°, 230°), `basalt` [230°, 20°). Cards: **The Raft** · "Mind the gap", **Basalt Stairs** · "Hexagons
   everywhere", **Tidepool Shelf** · "Low tide, high hopes", **Kelp Shallows** · "Squelchy". Entry points (for card
   triggers and sightlines): jetty spawn, (−176, −52), (40, 80), (100, −50). Fog per region from art-audio#palettes:
   `raft [32, 240, #eef6f4, .12]`, `basalt [30, 240, #dfe6ea, .20]`, `tidepool [34, 255, #e8f4f2, .15]`,
   `kelp [28, 220, #d6efe6, .25]`. Enemies: every region `enemies: [], max: 0` in this step (S-121 fills them).
   The `raft` region has `town: true`.
7. **Palette** `W.palette.archwater`: art-audio#palettes row `archwater` (sky day `#68a9d9`/`#d9f1ee`, gold
   `#f0d6b0`, dusk `#5b4f93`/`#c99ac0`, night `#1d2340`/`#3a3a6b`; clouds `#ffffff`/`#e8c8dc`/`#6e6aa4`; water deep
   `#1d6f86`, shallow `#7fd3d0`, foam `#f4fbfa`), terrain keys `archwater_saltturf`, `archwater_kelpmat`,
   `archwater_thrift`, `archwater_sand`, `archwater_tideflat`, `archwater_shingle`, `archwater_hextop`, cliff
   `archwater_columns` (all fall back to the home layer of the same slot tinted with `#36414f #7fd3d0 #e2c9a0 #6b4a8c`
   until S-119 ships the textures).
8. **Fixed views** (`REALMS.archwater.views`; yaw is the compass bearing in degrees as S-083 encoded it):
   (1) town: (−206, 12) yaw 90 pitch 0 (the Raft and the Watch Column); (2) landmark: (112, −60) yaw 90 pitch 8 (the
   Great Arch); (3) densest: (30, 100) yaw 135 pitch −5 (the Spouter and the pools); (4) widest: (−20, −60) at the
   column-top height 55.5 yaw 100 pitch −10. Each is measured in both cameras by the realm suite.
9. **Debug:** `G.debug.tide(state)` calls `applyTide(G.world, state)` and moves the water mesh (instant); `?tide=low`
   URL flag for tests.

#### Assets

None in this step (fallback tints only). S-118a/b build the props, S-119 the painted textures.

#### Acceptance criteria

- `generateRealm('archwater')` is deterministic; `W.genMs` ≤ 1,200 ms on the laptop (printed by the test).
- Reachable land at low water is 100,000-120,000 m²; at high water 90,000-110,000 m².
- In **both** tide states: `W.unreachable` is empty; spawns `jetty`, `grotto`, `rootgate` and the four region entry
  points are walkable and reachable; every path cell has h ≥ 0.2; every raft deck, the North and South Walks and the
  landing pier are reachable.
- At low water the Kelp Maze square, the bay mud and the flats are walkable; at high water they are not; the six
  pontoons and the floating log are reachable only at high water; the Spire Stack top is unreachable in both.
- Region samples: (−168, 10) → `raft`; (−20, −50) → `basalt`; (190, 10) → `kelp`; (40, 130) → `tidepool`;
  (−150, −100) → `basalt`.
- `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/archwater.json` is written with the placement print plus `walk`/`reach` checksums for
  `low` and `high`.
- `tests/fixtures/world-prints.json` is byte-identical; `bun test tests/world.test.js` passes unchanged.
- `REALMS.archwater.hidden === true`; the chart still shows Archwater as a fog bank.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep frozen home island, frozen realms, hidden until whole, budgets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/realm-archwater.test.js` (new): prints, genMs, land area per state, reachability per state, region
  samples, path heights, maze/pontoon/log walkability per state, `tideState()` truth table (absent + no beacon → low;
  absent + beacon → high; 0 → low; 1 → high), `applyTide()` returns changed cells and restores on toggling back.
- `bun test` (whole suite; world prints untouched: `git diff --stat tests/fixtures/world-prints.json` empty).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` with Archwater added to the suite's realm list (hidden realms are entered with
  `G.debug.realm`): enter/leave ×3, leak check, the four views in both cameras under budget, `?tide=low` and
  `?tide=high` both enter cleanly.

#### Out of scope

Props and landmarks (S-118a, S-118b), textures and music (S-119), people (S-120), enemies (S-121), the Tide Bell, the
water tween and lifting Pip (S-122a), finds (S-123).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the `waterY` change touches the home walk code; the world prints test and `world.test.js` catch any drift.
Rollback: revert the PR; Archwater is hidden so no player sees it.

<a id="s-118a"></a>

### S-118a · feat: Archwater prop kit: basalt columns, kelp, tide pools, floats and the shared kit pieces

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-117, S-082 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.archwater.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-kit](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-kit),
[#brief-archwater-small](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-archwater-small),
[#archwater-poi](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-poi), [#shared](../bible/atlas-isles.md#shared)

#### Why

Half of INDEX S-118 (split, see Index changes): the instanced kit and scatter that make Archwater read as
"hexagons everywhere" from the first second off the boat. The unique landmarks and the Raft come in S-118b.

#### What

1. **Prop builders** in `js/props.js` (or the realm prop module Pinewhistle used: `grep -rn "pinewhistle_" js/props*.js`),
   each a code-built toon model, ink hull where the shipped props have one, `occ` off outdoors:
   - `archwater_column`: hexagonal prism 0.9 m across, heights 2.4 / 4.8 / 7.2 m by scale, top cap uses the rock
     slot texture, ≤ 60 tris; used as **riser stamps** along every `W.cliff` riser (one column per 0.9 m of riser
     edge, height = riser height, jitter ±0.15 m).
   - `archwater_stump`: hexagon stump 0.6-2.0 m tall for the **Hex Maze** at (−40, −120): a 30 × 30 m field of
     stumps laid on a 1.0 m hex grid, maze walls of stumps ≥ 1.2 m (unhoppable: hop apex 0.85 m) and corridors of
     0.6 m stumps (hoppable steps); the maze is generated by a randomized depth-first carve on stream
     `40231 * 5 + 3` over a 10 × 10 cell grid with the entrance on the south side and the exit toward chest-1's
     anchor (−40, −120); colliders per stump ≥ 1.2 m.
   - `archwater_kelp`: 5 ribbon cards, 1.6 m, `#4a6a3a` / `#7a8a3a`; sways in the vertex shader only while its base
     is below `waterY` (uniform `uWaterY` set from `W.waterY`), ≤ 40 tris.
   - `archwater_kelp_wall`: 4 m × 1.6 m kelp hedge with a collider rail; **the Kelp Maze**: a 36 × 36 m maze centred
     (190, 10) on a 6 × 6 cell grid (cells 6 m), carved on stream `40231 * 5 + 5`, entrance on the west side at
     (172, 10), centre cell (190, 10) left open as a 6 × 6 m clearing (the Kelp Pearl, S-120), one dead end at
     (200, 22) kept open as chest-6's cell. Walls are solid at both tides (at high water the whole maze is under
     water and unwalkable anyway).
   - `archwater_urchin` (decor cluster, purple `#6b4a8c`, ≤ 120 tris), `archwater_barnacle` (barnacle rock, reuse
     `rock` + barnacle vertex colours `#e2c9a0`), `archwater_poolrim` (tide-pool rim ring for the 12 basins and 40
     decal pools), `archwater_pumice` (the pontoon visual: pale `#d8d0c0` hexagon slab 2.4 m, drawn at its deck's
     current height), `archwater_float` (green glass ball in a rope net 0.4 m, ≤ 80 tris), `archwater_driftwood`
     (reuse `log` recoloured `#b8a68a`), `archwater_thrift` (flower card `#e89ab0`), `archwater_shell` (deco).
   - Reused: `rock` as basalt boulder tinted `#36414f`; grass card; `kit_bench`, `kit_ruin_pillar`,
     `kit_ruin_lampstump`, `kit_log_niche` from S-082 (bench, ruin and niche positions come with the finds in S-123;
     here only the scatter types are registered).
   - Instanced types after this step plus S-118b must be **≤ 24**: hex column, hex stump, basalt boulder, kelp, kelp
     wall, thrift tuft, urchin cluster, barnacle rock, tide-pool rim, driftwood, raft deck piece, raft cabin,
     barrel-and-crate stack, net rack, pumice pontoon, glass float, grass card, flower card, shell deco, jetty post,
     `kit_bench`, `kit_ruin_pillar`, `kit_ruin_lampstump`, `kit_log_niche` (atlas-isles#archwater-kit). A unit test
     counts `W.scatterTypes` ≤ 24.
2. **Scatter tables** for the `basalt` recipe (the per-region weights the recipe passes to `js/scatter.js`):
   `basalt`: boulder 3, thrift tuft 4, grass card 6, barnacle rock 1; `tidepool`: urchin cluster 2, barnacle rock 3,
   shell deco 4, thrift 2; `kelp`: kelp 6 on flats below −0.4, barnacle rock 2, driftwood 1; `raft`: none (decks
   only). Density 0.30 deco and 0.032 props per m² of land as home. Keep a 6 m tree-and-tall-prop-free corridor along
   each sightline in atlas-isles#sightlines (from each region entry point and each bench position listed in
   atlas-isles#archwater-finds to the region's landmark top).
3. **Glass floats and tide-chalk decals** are not placed here (they are curios in S-123).
4. **Material programs** added: basalt, wet basalt (darkens with a `uWet` uniform below `waterY + 0.3`), kelp
   (sway), barnacle. S-118b adds rope, plank, Moon Pool glow, spout: total **8 ≤ 12** (a unit test reads the realm's
   material list).
5. Refresh `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/archwater.json` (same milestone, realm still hidden).

#### Assets

Code-built only; no images. ART BRIEFs pasted in the PR body from atlas-isles#brief-archwater-small for
`archwater_column`, `archwater_stump`, `archwater_kelp`, `archwater_kelp_wall`, `archwater_urchin`, `archwater_pumice`,
`archwater_float` (fallbacks: the code models above; 3D prompt pattern "stylised <thing>, basalt-and-tide-pool set,
toon shading, game-ready"). Manifest: commented-out `models` slots `prop_archwater_column`, `prop_archwater_kelp`,
`prop_archwater_float` with heights 2.4, 1.6, 0.4.

#### Acceptance criteria

- Every `W.cliff` riser edge carries column stamps; no column blocks a path or a column stair (test: no column
  collider within 1.4 m of a path centreline).
- The Hex Maze and the Kelp Maze are solvable: a BFS on the walk grid at low water reaches the Kelp Maze centre
  (190, 10) and the dead end (200, 22) from the maze entrance; the Hex Maze exit from its south entrance.
- ≤ 24 instanced types and ≤ 12 new material programs for the realm.
- The four realm views stay within budget in both cameras (main ≤ 200 calls, total ≤ 300, ≤ 1.3 M tris).
- Prints refreshed; home prints untouched; Archwater still hidden.
- Invariants: keep frozen home island, frozen realms, hidden until whole, budgets, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/realm-archwater.test.js` (extend: maze solvability, type and material counts, sightline corridors,
  prints).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (Archwater views both cameras; record the numbers in the PR body).
- `bun test`.

#### Out of scope

The Great Arch, the Watch Column, the Spouter dressing, the Raft visuals and Rafferty's Mast (S-118b); finds and
curios (S-123).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: column stamps can blow the triangle budget at the plateau view; fix by the charter's order (LOD proxies past
40 m, merge stamp runs). Rollback: revert; realm hidden.

<a id="s-118b"></a>

### S-118b · feat: Archwater landmarks: the Great Arch, the Watch Column, the Spouter, the Raft and Rafferty's Mast

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-118a · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.archwater.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-landmarks](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-landmarks),
[#brief-archwater-arch](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-archwater-arch),
[#brief-archwater-column](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-archwater-column),
[#brief-archwater-spouter](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-archwater-spouter),
[#brief-archwater-raft](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-archwater-raft), [#kit-beacon](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-beacon)

#### Why

The second half of S-118: the four landmarks a first-person player steers by, the Raft that is Archwater's town, and
the sealed grotto door. Each landmark is visible from its region's entry point (sightline rule).

#### What

1. **`archwater_great_arch`** (landmark, `W.landmarks` entry `arch`, region `kelp`): legs at (130, −70) on the arch
   shoulder (ground 6) and (170, −70) on the East Stack (ground 6); span 40 m; underside crown 22 m; top 26 m; built as
   a half-torus of hexagonal prisms (16 segments) on two prism legs, barnacle band and kelp cards hanging from the
   underside; colliders on the two legs only (box 8 × 8 m each); the **crown deck**: a bridge deck 10 × 3 m at h 26
   from x 145 to 155, z −71.5 to −68.5 (reachable only by the Hook later, S-143a: two hookbloom gates come in S-123).
   A `kit_beacon` at 1.4× bolted to the keystone (150, −70) at h 26.5, state `dark` until `beacon:archwater`
   (S-127 lights it). ≤ 8,000 tris, LOD proxy ≤ 800 beyond 48 m.
2. **`archwater_watch_column`** (landmark `column`, region `basalt`): a hexagonal prism 7 m across, 24 m tall at
   (−20, −60) on the top terrace (base 30, top 54); collider = hexagon prism; **the outer stair**: eight straight
   flights, each a bridge deck 1.2 m wide rising 3 m, wrapping the column (flight k turns 90° from k−1, landings 1.6 ×
   1.6 m at each turn), from the ground at the column's south face (−20, −52) to the **top platform**: deck r 3.5 at
   h 54 with a rope rail (rail collider ring r 3.5, gap none), a `kit_beacon` at 0.8× dark (the Sky Pearl's cage,
   S-120). The fifth landing (h 45) is chest-2's spot. ≤ 3,000 tris.
3. **`archwater_spouter`** dressing on the stamped dome at (90, 120): blowhole decal at the top, the **tide post**
   (cylinder 4 m tall, 0.4 m wide, painted rings `#f4efe2` / `#6b4a8c` every 0.5 m) on the summit; the **spout**: an
   additive card strip `archwater_spout` (6 frames) that plays a 10 m plume every 8 s only while
   `W.tideState === 'high'` and `beacon:archwater` (until then a faint hiss puff every 8 s). Positional sound `spout`
   (a whoosh) audible to 200 m (S-127 enables it). ≤ 1,600 tris for the post and lip.
4. **The Raft visuals**: `archwater_raft` deck pieces drawn on each of S-117's 14 raft decks (logs `#8a6a48`, planks
   `#b89a74`), tilted up to 4° at low water (visual only) and bobbing ±0.05 m at high; seven `archwater_raft_cabin`s
   (5 × 4 × 4 m, roofs `#6b4a8c` / `#3f8a88`, porthole glow `#f2b45a` at night) on rafts 1-6 and 13 (raft 13 is the net
   shed), each with a box collider and a door interactable that S-120 fills with knock lines; **Rafferty's Mast**
   (landmark `mast`, region `raft`): an 18 m mast with a crow's nest on raft 1 at (−166, 6), long pennant `#6b4a8c`,
   bunting strings to five cabins (card `archwater_card_bunting` from S-119, canvas fallback), tilted with the raft at
   low; jetty posts along the landing pier; barrel-and-crate stacks and net racks (≤ 2 per raft).
5. **The pearl door** `archwater_pearl_door` on the grotto ledge (134, −64), facing east over the Moon Pool: a round
   basalt door 2.4 m across with three shell sockets (glow `#bff4f0` when filled); collider box; interactable with no
   prompt yet (S-120 adds `[E] Place · Pearls`); the portal to `undertow_grotto` is registered sealed (`needs:
   'flag:aw:door'`, S-124 builds the realm).
6. **The Hollow doors and root-gate are not placed here** (S-123 finds pass); the shared kit bench/niche types only.
7. Every landmark gets a `W.landmarks` record `{ id, x, z, top, region }` for the fog cap (≤ 0.45) and the compass:
   `arch` (150, −70, top 26), `column` (−20, −60, top 54), `spouter` (90, 120, top 16), `mast` (−166, 6, top 18.6).
8. Refresh `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/archwater.json`.

#### Assets

Code-built only in this PR. ART BRIEFs pasted verbatim from atlas-isles#brief-archwater-arch,
#brief-archwater-column, #brief-archwater-spouter and #brief-archwater-raft (keys `archwater_great_arch`,
`archwater_watch_column`, `archwater_spouter`, `archwater_raft`, `archwater_raft_cabin`, `archwater_mast`,
`archwater_pearl_door`), with commented-out GLB slots in `assets/manifest.js`:
`// archwater_great_arch: { src: 'assets/realms/archwater/models/great_arch.glb', height: 26 },` and likewise for the
column (24), spouter (12), raft cabin (4), mast (18), pearl door (2.4). The spout card `archwater_spout` (6 frames, 256 ×
512, transparent) is generated in S-119; fallback here is the shared `fx_atlas` puff.

#### Acceptance criteria

- From the jetty spawn the Watch Column top is visible (heightfield ray clears the terraces by ≥ 12 m); from (100, −50)
  the arch crown; from (40, 80) the tide post; from the jetty the mast.
- The outer stair's eight flights are walkable from the ground to the h 54 platform (BFS over decks); the fifth landing
  is at h 45 ± 0.3.
- The arch crown deck and the Spire Stack top are unreachable on foot (solver: needs `hook` / `glide`).
- Raft decks draw at −0.2 (tilted) with `?tide=low` and level at 0.6 with `?tide=high`.
- Realm budgets at the four views in both cameras; ≤ 24 instanced types; ≤ 12 material programs (8 used).
- Archwater hidden; home prints untouched.
- Invariants: keep frozen home island, frozen realms, hidden until whole, budgets, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/realm-archwater.test.js` (extend: sightline rays, stair reachability, crown/spire unreachable,
  landmark records, prints).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (both cameras, both tides via `G.debug.tide`); add an in-page check that the spout
  plays only in the high + beacon state (set the flag with `G.debug.flags.set`).
- `bun test`.

#### Out of scope

Painted textures, spout card, loading card (S-119); NPCs and the pearl chain (S-120); finds, curios, benches, Hollow
doors and root-gate (S-123); lighting the arch (S-127).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the arch and stair decks are the realm's heaviest geometry; keep the LOD proxy and the 20 m shadow rule.
Rollback: revert; realm hidden.

<a id="s-119"></a>

### S-119 · art: Archwater look: terrain layers, cards, loading card, map art and music

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-117, S-098 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.archwater.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/art-audio.md#archwater](../bible/art-audio.md#archwater),
[../bible/art-audio.md#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data), [../bible/art-audio.md#ambience](../bible/art-audio.md#ambience),
[../bible/art-audio.md#bundles](../bible/art-audio.md#bundles), [../bible/art-audio.md#loading-cards](../bible/art-audio.md#loading-cards)

#### Why

The painted look and the sound of Archwater: seven terrain layers plus the column cliff, billboard cards, the
loading card (which also shows while sailing in), the map, and the `archwater` track that starts without its harp
(dim) and gains it at the beacon.

#### What

1. **Terrain** (kind `tile`, 1024² generate, ship `_hd` 1024² + `_lo` 512² webp; process with
   `python3 -I tools/make_seamless.py <in> <out> --blend 64`, then webp q 82): keys and subjects verbatim from
   art-audio#archwater: `archwater_saltturf`, `archwater_kelpmat`, `archwater_thrift`, `archwater_sand`,
   `archwater_tideflat`, `archwater_shingle`, `archwater_hextop`, and the cliff `archwater_columns`. Each subject ends
   "Palette: …" exactly as written there. Files `assets/realms/archwater/terrain/<key>_hd.webp` / `_lo.webp`; manifest
   entries `realm: 'archwater'`. Size limits (tests/assets.test.js from S-029): hd ≤ 110 KB, lo ≤ 40 KB.
2. **Cards** (kind `card`, white keyed to alpha with the art-audio one-liner): `archwater_card_ground` (sheet-items
   N = 4, 1024 × 512, cells `thrift`, `kelpfrond`, `anemone`, `seaholly`, sliced with `tools/slice_sheet.py`),
   `archwater_card_spray` (512² 4-frame strip, `tools/process_strip.py`), `archwater_card_moonpool` (512² alpha),
   `archwater_card_bubbles` (128²), `archwater_card_bunting` (1024 × 128), and `archwater_spout` (6 frames, 256 × 512
   each, "a burst of white sea spray shooting upward from a blowhole, painterly, six frames"). Each ≤ 150 KB.
3. **Loading card** `load_archwater` (kind `loading`, 1792 × 1024 → 1600 × 900 and a 960 × 540 `_lo`, ≤ 200 KB):
   subject verbatim from art-audio#archwater. **Map art** `map_archwater` (kind `map`, 1024²): subject verbatim.
   The chart's sailing card for Archwater uses `load_archwater` (S-081 rule).
4. **Music** (`js/realms/archwater.js`, the realm module; mirror `js/realms/pinewhistle.js`): register the
   `archwater` track by pasting the `archwater: { … }` block from art-audio#track-data into
   `G.audio.addTrack('archwater', def)` in the module's `onRealm` hook (bpm 100, G mixolydian, `lead2: 'harp'`,
   `layers: { lead2: 1 }`, `startLayer: 0`). On entry: `G.audio.layer(S.flags['beacon:archwater'] ? 1 : 0)`.
   Register the `undertow` dungeon variant (`{ from: 'depths', transpose: -3, voices: { lead: 'pluck' }, delay: {
   beats: 0.75, fb: 0.5, mix: 0.45 } }`) and `warden_gloop` (`{ from: 'warden', voices: { lead: 'pluck' }, swing: 0.15 }`)
   if S-103/S-105 did not already add them to `js/audio.js`.
5. **Ambience**: `G.audio.addAmbience('archwater_raft', …)` and `('archwater_coast', …)` with the data from
   art-audio#ambience (`archwater_raft` for region `raft`; `archwater_coast` for `basalt`, `tidepool`, `kelp`), both
   with their `dim` multipliers applied while `!flag:beacon:archwater`.
6. **Bundle**: everything under `assets/realms/archwater/`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs` lists it as bundle
   `archwater`; total ≤ 2.5 MB (target ≈ 2,440 KB from art-audio#bundles: terrain 1,050, cards 450, portraits 165 later,
   beast cards 140 later, loading 260, map 180, sketches 135 later, other 60). This step's share must leave ≥ 650 KB
   for S-120/S-121/S-123/S-126's portraits, cards and sketches.
7. Log every prompt and command in `docs/art/prompts.md`.

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Processing | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| 8 terrain keys above | tile · 1024 + 512 webp | make_seamless, webp q 82 | home layer of the same slot tinted with the atlas fallback hexes |
| 5 cards + `archwater_spout` | card · as listed | white→alpha, slice_sheet / process_strip, webp | `fx_atlas` puff, plain tinted quads, canvas bunting |
| `load_archwater` | loading · 1600 × 900 + 960 × 540 | webp q 80 | the shipped loading colour with the region card text |
| `map_archwater` | map · 1024² | webp q 80 | the canvas map from `buildMapCanvas(W)` |

#### Acceptance criteria

- Every key exists in `assets/manifest.js` with `realm: 'archwater'`, within its size limit (`tests/assets.test.js`).
- `?noassets=all&realm=archwater` renders (fallbacks) with no console error.
- The `archwater` track validates (`G.audio.addTrack` validator: every bar sums to `sp`; chord qualities allowed); it
  plays without the harp at layer 0 and with it at layer 1.
- Bundle `archwater` ≤ 2.5 MB; core precache unchanged by more than 20 KB; `node tools/build-pages.mjs` passes.
- Invariants: keep hidden until whole, precache, assets, IP, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (assets test, audio track validator test over the new track).
- `node tools/build-pages.mjs` then `bun run test:pwa`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (Archwater entry with assets; and once with `?noassets=all` through the smoke variant
  `bun run test:smoke` if it has the flag).
- Manual-free check of the look: an in-page screenshot from each of the four views saved to `test-results/` and linked
  in the PR body.

#### Out of scope

Portraits (S-120, S-126), bestiary cards (S-121, S-126), vista sketches (S-123).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: bundle size creep; check the build output before committing images. Rollback: revert; fallbacks remain.

<a id="s-120"></a>
<a id="s-120a"></a>

### S-120a · feat: the Raft's people I: the otter rig, seven looks, spots and Rafferty's portrait

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-118b, S-029b, S-068a · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.archwater.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/cast.md#skipper-rafferty](../bible/cast.md#skipper-rafferty),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-poi](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-poi),
[../bible/art-audio.md#npc-voices](../bible/art-audio.md#npc-voices)

#### Why

The Raft is Archwater's town and the start of its chain: a beached floating village of otters with a loud captain who
cannot sing. This step builds the `otter` rig family (reused later by Nana Kelp and Bosun Snore's seals) and puts
seven otters on the Raft at their spots with schedules, a portrait and voice blips; they have no lines yet (S-120b).
Split from INDEX S-120 ([Index changes](#index-changes)) to keep each PR under ~500 lines. Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-120 (M15 Archwater and the midpoint); vision item **Archwater and the midpoint**.

#### What

1. **`otter` rig family** in `js/models.js` `npcDef` (one new case, read from a look record; mirror the `rodent` case
   S-099 added: `grep -n "case 'rodent'" js/models.js`): long lathe body (radius profile 0.20 / 0.26 / 0.24 / 0.16 of
   `h`), small round ears (two `sphere` r 0.05 h), a thick tapered tail `tube` (length 0.55 h, radius 0.09 → 0.03 h),
   four whisker `box`es (0.12 × 0.005 × 0.005 h) each side of the nose, `face()` with open eyes, parts `body`, `head`,
   `armL`, `armR`, `extra`. Look fields: `{ rig: 'otter', h, pal: { fur, belly, nose, coat, hat, acc }, acc: [...] }`
   where `acc` items are `hat:bobble`, `coat`, `scarf`, `apron`, `accordion`, `pot`. Options `ears: false`,
   `arms: 'flipper'` are accepted but unused until Bosun Snore's seals.
2. **Look records** in `js/content/looks.js` (`NPC_LOOKS`):

   | id | h (m) | Palette | acc |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `rafferty` | 1.15 | fur `#6b4a33`, belly `#ead7b8`, nose `#2a1a12`, coat `#36414f` (buttons `#c99a4a`, patches `#7fd3d0` / `#e2c9a0`), hat `#c2563f` | `hat:bobble`, `coat`, `accordion` (`#6b4a8c` / `#f3ead2`: two `rbox` ends + a ribbed `cyl`) |
   | `marina` | 0.95 | fur `#7a5a3e`, belly `#e8d4b4`, scarf `#7fd3d0` | `scarf` |
   | `spratt` | 0.60 | fur `#8a6a48`, belly `#f0dcbc` | none |
   | `old_bilge` | 1.05 | fur `#5e4430`, belly `#dcc8a6`, apron `#e2c9a0` | `apron`, `pot` (a `cyl` ladle) |
   | `rafter_a` | 0.90 | fur `#6e5038`, coat `#3f8a88` | `coat` |
   | `rafter_b` | 0.85 | fur `#7e6044`, hat `#6b4a8c` | `hat:bobble` |
   | `rafter_c` | 0.95 | fur `#6a4c36`, scarf `#e2c9a0` | `scarf` (holds a rod: `extra` thin `cyl` 1.4 m) |

   Rafferty's bunting twin: while he talks or sings, the bunting strings on raft 1 jiggle (±3 cm, 4 Hz).
3. **NPC records** (`js/content/npcs/archwater.js`, the shape S-099 used for Pinewhistle: `grep -rn "nutmeg" js/content/`)
   with spots written to `W.npcSpots` by the realm module (not a post-pass: these are the land's own people and ship
   before the realm is unhidden, so `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/archwater.json` is refreshed in this PR). Spots are the
   nearest walkable deck cell within 3 m of the anchor and ≥ 2.5 m from every door interactable:

   | id | Name label | Spot id | Anchor | Schedule `[fromHour, spot, activity]` |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | `rafferty` | Skipper Rafferty | `archwater.raft_deck` | (−164, 10) | `[[7,'archwater.raft_deck','work'],[19,'archwater.raft_deck','sing'],[22,'archwater.raft_deck','doze']]` (the cabin row waits for S-131b) |
   | `marina` | Marina | `archwater.net_rack` | (−180, 3) | `[[6,'archwater.net_rack','work'],[21,'archwater.net_rack','doze']]` |
   | `old_bilge` | Old Bilge | `archwater.cook_pot` | (−146, −3) | `[[6,'archwater.cook_pot','work']]` |
   | `spratt` | Spratt | `archwater.spratt_edge` | (−196, 18) | `[[7,'archwater.spratt_edge','wander:3'],[20,'archwater.spratt_edge','sleep']]` |
   | `rafter_a` | Rafter | `archwater.raft_south` | (−172, 27) | `[[6,'archwater.raft_south','stand']]` |
   | `rafter_b` | Rafter | `archwater.raft_east` | (−150, 17) | `[[6,'archwater.raft_east','wander:2']]` |
   | `rafter_c` | Rafter | `archwater.landing_end` | (−216, 8) on the pier | `[[5,'archwater.landing_end','work']]` (fishing pose) |

   If S-174 schedules are not merged yet, every NPC stands at its first row's spot (the S-099 fallback).
   Until S-120b lands the otters have no Talk prompt: the records carry no `talk` field and no `lines`, and
   `interact.js` offers Talk only to NPCs with one of them (add that guard if it is missing, with a unit test).
   S-120b adds `talk: 'archwater'`.
4. **Portrait** `portrait_rafferty` (realm `archwater`), generated and processed exactly as
   [../bible/cast.md](../bible/cast.md) "Portraits" says (house preamble + Rafferty's prompt + portrait suffix;
   `tools/export_sprites.py` then a 512² webp q 82, ≤ 55 KB). Saved `assets/realms/archwater/portraits/rafferty.webp`.
   The other rafters show name only (bundle budget, [../bible/art-audio.md#bundles](../bible/art-audio.md#bundles):
   three Archwater portrait slots go to Rafferty, Old Gloop and Nana Kelp). The turnaround reference
   `docs/art/refs/rafferty_turnaround.webp` is generated too (docs only).
5. **Voice blips**: otter voice blip per [../bible/art-audio.md#npc-voices](../bible/art-audio.md#npc-voices) (grep
   `otter`); Rafferty's sound cue is an accordion wheeze every 20-30 s within 30 m (`sfx.accordion`, a 0.6 s
   `T({f: 196, f1: 220, d: 0.6, v: 0.06, type: 'sawtooth', lp: 1200})` chord of three such tones a fifth apart).

#### Assets

#### Assets

- `portrait_rafferty` (generated, above). Fallback: name only.
- Code-built otters (above). ART BRIEFs pasted in the PR body verbatim from
  [../bible/cast.md#skipper-rafferty](../bible/cast.md#skipper-rafferty): `npc_rafferty` (1.15 m, ≤ 6,000 tris,
  3D prompt "[3D preamble] a sturdy otter sea captain, patched slate sea coat with brass buttons, red bobble hat,
  accordion on a strap, 1.15 m tall.") and `npc_rafter` (≤ 3,500 tris, three textures). Manifest: commented-out
  `// npc_rafferty: { src: 'assets/realms/archwater/models/npc_rafferty.glb', height: 1.15 },` and
  `// npc_rafter: { ..., height: 0.95 },`.

#### Acceptance criteria

- `npcDef('rafferty')` and the six rafters build with the `otter` rig, ≤ 1,800 tris each, ≤ 6 materials; the rig
  family adds one `npcDef` case and no per-character branches (unit test in `tests/models.test.js`).
- In Archwater (via `G.debug.realm('archwater')`) seven otters stand on walkable deck cells at both tides (they ride
  the Raft's deck height: their y follows `bridgeAt` at their spot), none within 2.5 m of a door.
- `portrait_rafferty` ≤ 55 KB, realm-tagged; `?noassets=all` shows the name only.
- Archwater prints refreshed; home prints untouched; Archwater still hidden.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep frozen home island, frozen realms, hidden until whole, assets, tone, IP, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (`tests/models.test.js` otter cases; `tests/realm-archwater.test.js` extended: every NPC spot walkable
  and reachable in both tide states, ≥ 2.5 m from doors; prints; `tests/assets.test.js` for the portrait size).
- `free -h`, then `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (Archwater views still under budget with the seven otters).

#### Out of scope

Dialogue, cabin knock lines, the quest data and flags (S-120b); the pearl interactables, hints and door (S-127a);
the Raft cabins interior (S-131b); Nana Kelp (S-132).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: NPCs on tide decks can sink into the deck when the tide moves; the realm test covers both states. Rollback:
revert; the realm is hidden.

<a id="s-120b"></a>

### S-120b · feat: the Raft's people II: Rafferty's chain lines, the rafters' talk, knock lines and the mq-archwater quest data

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-120a · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.archwater.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/cast.md#skipper-rafferty](../bible/cast.md#skipper-rafferty),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-interiors](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-interiors),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-chain](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-chain),
[../bible/story.md#ch9-11](../bible/story.md#ch9-11), [../bible/charter.md#reserved-markers](../bible/charter.md#reserved-markers)

#### Why

The otters stand on the Raft (S-120a); this step gives them their words. Rafferty's chain lines are gated on the
`aw:*` flags, the five cabin doors get their knock lines, and the `mq-archwater` quest is written as data so S-127a
only has to make it playable. Part of **Archwater and the midpoint**.

#### What

0. **Talk on.** Each of the seven otter records in `js/content/npcs/archwater.js` gains `talk: 'archwater'`.
1. **Dialogue** in `js/content/dialogue/archwater.js`, text in `js/text/en/archwater.js` (ids `archwater.npc.<id>.<key>.<n>`).
   Rafferty's chain lines are written here and gated on the `aw:*` flags so S-127a only adds the quest:

   | Entry id | `when` | Priority | Lines |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `archwater.rafferty.intro` | `!flag:aw:step1` | 80 | 1 "(sings) Oh the Raft, the Raft, she floats when she oughtn't... except she doesn't. Ahoy, matey! Mind the tilt." · 2 "The tide's stuck low. It follows the arch's light, see, and the arch's light is gone. No light, no tide. No tide, no float." · 3 "There's a door under the Great Arch. The Undertow door. Three pearl sockets, and we've not had a pearl between us since my granddad." · 4 "One pearl's up the Watch Column in the old lamp cage. One's at the bottom of the Great Tidepool. One's in the middle of the Kelp Maze. Off you go, matey!" · then `set:aw:step1` |
   | `archwater.rafferty.pearls` | `flag:aw:step1`, `!flag:aw:door` | 50 | "Column, tidepool, kelp, matey. (sings) Three little pearls in a row, and where they've got to, nobody knows..." |
   | `archwater.rafferty.door` | `flag:aw:door`, `!flag:beacon:archwater` | 50 | "You opened the Undertow door?! (sings) Down, down, down she goes... Mind yourself in there, matey. It's damp. Everything's damp." |
   | `archwater.rafferty.after` | `flag:beacon:archwater` | 50 | 1 "WE'RE FLOATING! (sings) Oh the Raft, the Raft, she floats like she oughta! ...I'll work on the rhyme." · 2 (once) "Level decks, matey. I'd forgotten what level felt like." |
   | `archwater.marina.idle` | before / after `flag:beacon:archwater` | 30 | "Mending nets for a sea we can't reach. Nets don't mend themselves." / "First catch in a month! It's a boot. I'm keeping it." |
   | `archwater.old_bilge.idle` | before / after | 30 | "Soup's on! It's kelp soup. Kelp soup is soup that has given up." / "Soup's on! Real fish soup! The sea came back, and so did the fish." |
   | `archwater.spratt.idle` | before / after | 30 | "I dive for shells! Well. I dive into mud. It's mostly mud." / "The water's back! I found a shell! I found eleven shells!" |
   | `archwater.rafter.idle` (a, b, c rotate by day index) | before / after | 30 | "Beached, matey. Like a big wooden whale." · "The Raft's the Raft. Wet or dry, we're home." · "Can't fish off a raft on mud. Tried. Caught mud." / after: "Bob, bob, bob. Lovely." · "Hear that? Water slapping the logs. Best sound there is." · "Caught a fish! Put it back. It looked busy." |

   First Talk with each sets `met:<id>`. `(sings)` is Rafferty's reserved marker: add it to
   [../bible/charter.md#reserved-markers](../bible/charter.md#reserved-markers)'s table (speaker `rafferty`) in this PR
   and to the content lint's speaker check.
2. **Cabin doors** (S-118b built them as interactables with no text): `[E] Knock · Cabin` on the five doors, first knock
   the "Before" line and after `flag:beacon:archwater` the "After" line, both verbatim from
   [atlas-isles#archwater-interiors](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-interiors) (ids `archwater.door.<cabin>.0/1`).
   The main raft door (−162, 2): `[E] Knock · Cabin` "A note on the door: 'At the mast. Always at the mast. — R.'"
   until S-131b turns it into the `raft_cabins` portal.
3. **Quest data** `js/content/quests/archwater.js` (main quest, shape of [../bible/systems.md#quests](../bible/systems.md#quests)),
   with `start: 'realm:archwater.visited'` so it can never show while the realm is hidden (S-127b changes it to
   `flag:ch8`):

   ```js
   { id: 'mq-archwater', n: 10, kind: 'main', realm: 'archwater', title: 'qt.archwater.title', owner: 'rafferty',
     start: 'realm:archwater.visited',
     steps: [
       { id: 'arrive',   text: 'qt.archwater.s0', target: { realm: 'archwater', spawn: 'jetty' }, done: 'realm:archwater.visited', on: 'REALM' },
       { id: 'rafferty', text: 'qt.archwater.s1', target: { npc: 'rafferty' }, done: 'flag:aw:step1', on: 'TALK_END' },
       { id: 'pearls',   text: 'qt.archwater.s2', target: { pearls: true }, done: ['flag:aw:pearl-sky', 'flag:aw:pearl-pool', 'flag:aw:pearl-kelp'], on: 'ITEM_GET', parallel: true },
       { id: 'door',     text: 'qt.archwater.s3', target: { xz: [134, -64] }, done: 'flag:aw:door', on: 'PUZZLE' },
       { id: 'depths',   text: 'qt.archwater.s4', target: { realm: 'undertow_grotto' }, done: 'item:tide_bell', on: 'ITEM_GET' },
       { id: 'warden',   text: 'qt.archwater.s5', target: { realm: 'undertow_grotto' }, done: 'boss:undertow_grotto:gloop', on: 'BOSS_DEFEATED' },
       { id: 'kindle',   text: 'qt.archwater.s6', target: { realm: 'undertow_grotto' }, done: 'flag:beacon:archwater', on: 'BEACON' } ],
     reward: [], set: [], journal: 'qj.archwater' }
   ```

   Texts: title "The Great Arch"; s0 "Sail to Archwater"; s1 "Find out why the tide won't turn"; s2 "Find the three
   pearls ({n}/3)"; s3 "Open the Undertow door"; s4 "Explore the Undertow Grotto"; s5 "Calm the Warden of the Great
   Arch"; s6 "Kindle the arch's lamp". Short labels (`.short`, ≤ 32 characters): "Archwater", "Talk to Rafferty",
   "Pearls {n}/3", "The Undertow door", "The Undertow Grotto", "The Warden", "Kindle the lamp". Journal open (Hoot):
   "The otters' raft is sitting on mud. Rafferty says the sea is 'stuck'. I did not know the sea could stick." Done:
   "The tide turned. The Raft floats. Rafferty sang. The sea seemed to take it well." Hints (nudge, answer) are added
   with the interactables in S-127a.
4. **Flags registered** in `js/content/flags.js` this step: `aw:step1`, `aw:pearl-sky`, `aw:pearl-pool`, `aw:pearl-kelp`,
   `aw:door` (kind `story`, family `aw:*` documented), `met:rafferty`, `met:marina`, `met:old_bilge`, `met:spratt`.
#### Assets

None (the models and portrait shipped in S-120a).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Talking to Rafferty with no `aw:*` flags shows the four intro boxes in order and sets `aw:step1` and `met:rafferty`;
  later talks show the pearls line; with `aw:door` the door line; with `beacon:archwater` the after lines.
- Marina, Old Bilge, Spratt and the three rafters show their before / after lines by `beacon:archwater`; the rafters
  rotate by day index.
- The five cabin doors show their two knock lines by `beacon:archwater`; the main door shows the note.
- `mq-archwater` validates in the quest lint (every step has `text`, `done`; hints are allowed empty only for this
  step's draft, marked `draft: true`, and the lint test checks `draft` quests are not `start`-able while the realm is
  hidden).
- Content lint: every line ≤ 220 characters; `(sings)` appears only in `rafferty` lines; no `~` or `!!`.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep frozen home island, frozen realms, hidden until whole, tone, IP, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (content lint, quest lint, flag registry).
- `free -h`, then a new in-page check in the suite S-099 used for Pinewhistle's people
  (`grep -rln "nutmeg" js/debug/tests/`): enter Archwater, `G.debug.warpTo('archwater.raft_deck')`, talk to Rafferty,
  assert `S.flags['aw:step1'] === true` and the four text ids shown in order; set `beacon:archwater` with
  `G.debug.flags.set` and assert the after line; knock on one cabin door before and after.

#### Out of scope

The pearl interactables, hints and door (S-127a); the Raft cabins interior (S-131b); the plaza visit (S-127b).

#### Risk & rollback

Data only; revert. The realm is hidden.

<a id="s-121"></a>

### S-121 · feat: Archwater enemies: Tidepool Jelly, Squirt Urchin and Sand Snapper

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-042, S-118b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.archwater.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/creatures.md#enemy-tidepool-jelly](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-tidepool-jelly),
[../bible/creatures.md#enemy-squirt-urchin](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-squirt-urchin),
[../bible/creatures.md#enemy-sand-snapper](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-sand-snapper),
[../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables](../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables), [../bible/creatures.md#model-rules](../bible/creatures.md#model-rules)

#### Why

Archwater's three species: a hopper, a turret and a burrower, all data on the archetype framework (S-040 to S-042).
Each teaches its counter in the open before the Undertow Grotto mixes them. Roadmap: INDEX S-121 (M15).

#### What

1. **Species data** in `js/realms/archwater/enemies.js` (shape of [../bible/creatures.md#archetypes](../bible/creatures.md#archetypes);
   the file S-100 made for Pinewhistle is the template: `grep -rn "pinecone_puff" js/realms/`), registered into
   `ENEMY` when the realm module loads:

   | id | name | archetype | hp | r | emerge | gems | poof | params |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `tidepool_jelly` | Tidepool Jelly | `hopper` | 3 | 0.42 | `water` | 1 | `0xbfeee6` | `{ crouch: 0.45, harmGate: 0.15, hopSpeed: 4.4, hopT: 0.46, reach: 2.8, rest: 1.0, tier: 1 }` |
   | `squirt_urchin` | Squirt Urchin | `turret` | 4 | 0.40 | `still` | 1 | `0xf2e6ff` | `{ turn: 90, aim: 0.80, lock: 0.30, shot: 10, burst: 1, reload: 1.2, range: 14, tier: 1 }` |
   | `sand_snapper` | Sand Snapper | `burrower` | 5 | 0.46 | `sand` | 1 | `0xf29a9a` | `{ moundSpeed: 3.0, ringR: 1.3, tell: 0.75, lock: 0.25, exposed: 1.4, dive: 0.4, reach: 8, tier: 1 }` |

   Each has `realm: 'archwater'`, `model: '<id>'`, `card: 'card_<id>'`, and `lore` / `tip` exactly as creatures.md:
   Jelly "Tidepool Jellies are what the sea makes when it is bored." / "Same as a slime. Squish, then hop!"; Urchin
   "Squirt Urchins are the sea's way of saying 'no, thank you'." / "After it squirts it has to refill. Run in then!";
   Snapper "The Sand Snapper is a clam who wanted to be a crab. It is working on it." / "Keep walking when you hear
   the rumble, then bonk it when it pops up!". The Jelly's landing in water spawns a cosmetic splash (`fx.splashSmall`);
   it wobbles and bloops for its 0.45 s crouch (rim flash to `#7fd3d0`). The Urchin puffs its spines (scale 1.0 →
   1.25) through `aim`. The Snapper surfaces only on cells with sand splat ≥ 0.5 or `W.ground.soft` (burrower rule):
   on Archwater that is the `archwater_sand` and `archwater_tideflat` slots.
2. **Rigs** in `js/models.js` `enemyDef` (≤ 1,200 tris, ≤ 6 materials, built through `instRig`):
   - `tidepool_jelly`: a dome (`sphere` half, r 0.42, scale y 0.75) `#7fd3d0` with fresnel-faked translucency (toon
     material `opacity 0.8`, `transparent`, `depthWrite: true`), a darker core sphere r 0.2 `#4fb0b0`, a five-arm coral
     starfish on top (`#f29a7a`, five `cone`s flattened), eyes `#2a2440`.
   - `squirt_urchin`: body `sphere` r 0.25 `#8f6ab0`, 26 spine `cone`s (h 0.18) `#6b4a8c` with tip colour `#f2e6ff`
     via vertex colour, a little spout `cyl` on top, eyes.
   - `sand_snapper`: two ridged clam shells (`lathe`, 0.8 m wide, `#e2c9a0`, ridges `#c9a878` as vertex bands), pink
     inside `#f29a9a`, two stalk eyes (`cyl` + `sphere`). Underground it is drawn only as the mound (0.8 m dirt hump
     on `archwater_sand` colour `#e2c9a0`).
3. **Spawn tables** ([../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables](../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables) is the definition, R7):
   `W.regions` of the `basalt` recipe get `basalt: enemies [['sand_snapper', 2], ['tidepool_jelly', 2]], max 5`;
   `tidepool: [['tidepool_jelly', 4]], max 5`; `kelp: [['tidepool_jelly', 3], ['sand_snapper', 3]], max 6`; `raft:
   [], max 0` (town). Dark zones (Gull Cave, Echo Cave `W.dark` cells): `[['dimling', 1]]`, max 2. Spawns pick only
   cells walkable **in the current tide state**, ≥ 12 m from the Raft region's edge (safe-zone rule). Night rule: none.
4. **Placed turrets**: new post-pass `placeIsleTurrets` on `new RNG(40231 * 71 + 25)` (add the row to the passes
   table in `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` in the close-out), 10 Squirt Urchins, each the nearest valid cell within 8 m of its
   anchor (valid = walkable at **both** tides, slope < 0.5, 4-8 m from a path centreline, ≥ 6 m from every find,
   NPC spot and gate, outside the town region + 12 m). Basalt Stairs (6): (−120, −60), (−90, −90), (−50, −20),
   (−10, −100), (20, −70), (60, −40). Tidepool Shelf (4): (30, 110), (−30, 150), (80, 95), (−95, 140). Turrets never
   respawn within a visit; they reappear on the next realm entry.
5. **Bestiary cards** `card_tidepool_jelly`, `card_squirt_urchin`, `card_sand_snapper`: 384² webp q 82, ≤ 45 KB,
   `realm: 'archwater'`, generated by a GPT Sol subagent with the creatures.md card pattern:
   `[STYLE] A bestiary card illustration on cream paper with a soft torn edge, a single <description>, three-quarter
   view, centred, gentle rim light, no text, no border lines, square.` where `<description>` is verbatim: Jelly "a
   translucent teal jelly blob with a small coral starfish on its head, sitting in a rock pool with sunlight dancing
   through it"; Urchin "a round purple sea urchin with soft pale-tipped spines and a tiny spout, squirting a droplet
   from a basalt rock pool"; Snapper "a sandy ridged clam with two curious stalk eyes popping up out of wet sand, shell
   open to show a soft pink inside". Process with `tools/export_sprites.py` to 384² and save under
   `assets/realms/archwater/cards/`. Fallback: the canvas blob card from S-046.
6. **Wind-up sounds** with a pitch rise per creatures.md first-person rules: Jelly `bloop` (`T({f: 300, f1: 520, d:
   0.45, v: 0.08})`), Urchin spine-puff rattle (`N({d: 0.8, v: 0.05, type: 'bandpass', f: 2400, f1: 3600})`), Snapper
   rumble from 14 m (shipped burrower rumble) and a sand-bubble fizz during its 0.75 s tell.

#### Assets

| Key | Kind | Build / generate | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tidepool_jelly`, `squirt_urchin`, `sand_snapper` | models | code rigs above; ART BRIEFs pasted from creatures.md (hex lists, poly 600 / 1,100 / 900 code, ≤ 4,000 owner GLB) with the 3D prompt pattern of [../bible/creatures.md#model-rules](../bible/creatures.md#model-rules); manifest slots commented out with heights 0.55, 0.5, 0.5 | the rig |
| `card_tidepool_jelly`, `card_squirt_urchin`, `card_sand_snapper` | bestiary cards | generated as above | canvas blob card |

#### Acceptance criteria

- Each species, spawned by `G.debug.actors.spawn('<id>', x, z)` on Archwater, draws its telegraph ≥ 0.55 s before its
  first damage frame (Jelly 0.45 s crouch + 0.15 s harmGate = 0.60 s; Urchin 0.80 s stripe; Snapper 0.75 s ring), deals
  exactly 1 HP per hit, poofs at 0 HP with its poof colour, and adds `S.bestiary[id].poofed`.
- The Urchin's stripe matches its bead's path; walking in during its 1.2 s reload shows the gold opening.
- The Snapper never surfaces on a raft deck, a path stamp, rock or water; its ring does not follow Pip.
- 10 placed Urchins at the listed anchors (± 8 m), all on cells walkable at both tides; none in the Raft region.
- No enemy spawns on the Raft or within 12 m of it; no enemy spawns on a cell that is water in the current tide state.
- Region `max` and `CFG.maxEnemies` (8) hold during a 5-minute soak in each region.
- Cards ≤ 45 KB each; Archwater bundle still ≤ 2.5 MB.
- Archwater prints refreshed (turrets are in the realm's own print); still hidden.
- Invariants: keep frozen home island, frozen realms, hidden until whole, budgets, assets, tone (poof, 1 HP, ≥ 0.55 s), IP, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (species data validates against the archetype param ranges; spawn table equals creatures.md; turret
  anchors test in `tests/realm-archwater.test.js`; telegraph-lead unit test from S-040 includes the three new species).
- In-page: the per-species test in the suite S-100 used (`grep -rln "needle_wasp" js/debug/tests/`), run with
  `node tests/inpage.mjs <that suite>`: spawn, measure lead, poof, bestiary.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (Archwater four views with enemies alive: still ≤ 200 / 300 calls).

#### Out of scope

Dungeon waves (S-124, S-125); Old Gloop (S-126); Moonmere silver variants (later).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the burrower's sand test can be wrong on tide-flat cells that flood; spawns are filtered by the current tide
state. Rollback: revert; realm hidden.

<a id="s-122a"></a>

### S-122a · feat: the Tide Bell rings tide stones; the water moves and lifts Pip; Archwater's and Duskwick's tide stones

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-048, S-117, S-037b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none (the tool is only reachable through
`undertow_grotto`, built in S-125; tests grant it with `G.debug.give`) · Bible:
[../bible/systems.md#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools), [../bible/systems.md#tool-tide](../bible/systems.md#tool-tide),
[../bible/systems.md#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates), [../bible/systems.md#brief-tools](../bible/systems.md#brief-tools),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-finds](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-finds),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-gates](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-gates)

#### Why

Tool 3 of 10 (pacing rule 1: a tool at least every 5 h of story). The Tide Bell lets the player choose the sea:
it flips a realm's or a room's water between two levels, so the same coast is two places. This half of S-122 builds
the tool, the tide-stone gate, the water tween, the never-strand lift, and the tide stones on Archwater (5) and
Duskwick (1); S-122b adds the home finds. Roadmap: INDEX S-122 (M15).

#### What

1. **Item and ability.** `ITEMS.tide_bell = { name: 'Tide Bell', kind: 'tool', icon: 'icons_tools:tide_bell', desc:
   'Ring it by a tide stone. Low water, high water: your choice.', stack: 1, beat: true }`; `ABILITIES.tide = { name:
   'Tide Bell', from: 'tide_bell' }`. The tool ring (S-035) gets its slot (second after the Lantern). Item-get line:
   "You got the Tide Bell! Press {tool} beside a tide stone to ring it. Low water, high water: your choice."
2. **Held model** `tool_tide_bell` (code-built, [../bible/systems.md#brief-tools](../bible/systems.md#brief-tools)): a
   lathe bell 0.18 m, glass `#7fd3d0` (toon, rim light), a sphere-ish scallop handle `#ff9f8f`, clapper `#e2c9a0`;
   held in the left hand in the FP viewmodel (the Lantern's slot code: `grep -rn "lantern" js/vm/`), on the belt in TP.
   Ringing swings it 25° and back over 0.4 s. ≤ 400 tris.
3. **Gate type `tide_stone`** in `js/gates.js` (if S-086 already registered a look-only `tide_stone` for Duskwick's
   Mouth Rocks, extend it; `grep -n "tide_stone" js/gates.js`): look `gate_tide_stone` (code-built: hexagonal basalt
   standing stone 0.8 × 1.6 m `#36414f`, a wave-spiral decal `#7fd3d0` that glows 1.0 s when rung; ≤ 300 tris),
   `needs: 'ability:tide'`, prompt `[T] Ring · Tide stone` (context-first: the tap uses the Tide Bell whatever is
   equipped), radius 3 m; without the tool `[E] Look · Tide stone` "Water's too high. Or too low."; FP cue: a lapping
   water loop within 8 m (`ambLap`, positional). Record shape: `{ type: 'tide_stone', id, x, z, realm: true }` (flips
   the realm toggle) or `{ ..., local: '<flagKey>' }` (flips only that flag: used by home caches and ss-26).
4. **Ringing** (`js/tide-ring.js`, runtime; pure parts in `js/realms/tide.js` from S-117):
   - t 0: `sfx.tideRing` (`bell(t, 392, 2.4, 0.7)` + `splashBig` at 0.4 s), the decal glows, apply
     `toggle:tide:<realm>` through `cond.apply` (the flag is registered `kind: 'toggle', rewritable: true`).
   - t 0-2.5 s: `tideTween(W, newState, 2.5)` lerps the water mesh `position.y`, every tide deck's height
     (`bridge.tide`), and the kelp shader uniform `uWaterY`. Wet-basalt uniform `uWet` follows.
   - t 1.25 s: `applyTide(W, newState)` swaps walkability; then **never strand**: if Pip's cell is not walkable, call
     `liftToNearest(W, pip)`: BFS over `W.walk` from his cell to the nearest walkable cell reachable from the
     arrival spawn, 0.4 s fade out, place, 0.4 s fade in, no damage, no toast. NPCs on newly flooded cells snap to
     their spot; enemies on them poof quietly (no drop, no bestiary count, no `EV.KILL`).
   - Ringing with no tide stone within 3 m plays the chime only ("Nothing answers."). A ring during a fight is allowed;
     during a scene, the tool input is locked as all inputs.
   - Export `tideTween` and `liftToNearest` for S-124 (rooms) and S-127b (the beacon's 8 s turn, R20).
5. **Archwater's tide stones** (atlas `tide-1..5`) as the first entries of Archwater's `placeIsleGates` pass
   (`new RNG(40231 * 71 + 23)`; S-123 appends its gates after these, so their positions never move), each `realm:
   true`, nearest cell within 2 m walkable at **both** tides: `tide-1` (−176, −52), `tide-2` (−112, −104), `tide-3`
   (124, −60), `tide-4` (100, 108), `tide-5` (120, 20). Gate ids `archwater:tide-1..5`.
6. **Duskwick's tide** ([../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-gates](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-gates)):
   registry entry gains `tide: { flag: 'tide:duskwick', low: -1.4, high: 0, absent: 'high' }`; `bridgeAt` already
   handles the lagoon decks (fixed decks stay put). Its tide stone `duskwick:tide-1` at the Mouth Rocks' west row
   (−24, 156), appended to Duskwick's `placeIsleGates` (`new RNG(40211 * 71 + 23)`; append after any S-086 entries).
   `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/duskwick.json` gains `walk`/`reach` checksums for `low` (the existing `high` entries
   are byte-identical); register `tide:duskwick` in `js/content/flags.js`. Duskwick `chest-5` (−46, 112), placed in
   S-086, becomes reachable at low tide.
7. **Progression solver** (`js/progress-graph.js`): the `tide` ability adds an edge between a land's `high` and `low`
   zones wherever that land has a `realm: true` tide stone reachable in both states; before the tool, Archwater is in
   its absent state (low until `beacon:archwater`). The solver report lists `tide` → Duskwick `chest-5` (Threemills'
   finds join in S-144).
8. **Icon**: generate the `tide_bell` cell for the Tools I icon atlas
   ([../bible/art-audio.md#ui](../bible/art-audio.md#ui): "a small sea-green bell `#3fa79a` with a shell clapper",
   128² cell, transparent) and repack the atlas with `python3 -I tools/pack_atlas.py` exactly as S-057 did for the
   Lantern (`grep -rn "pack_atlas" docs/art/prompts.md`). Fallback: the canvas glyph.
9. **Debug**: `G.debug.ring(gateId)` rings a stone as if Pip stood beside it; `G.debug.tide(state)` (S-117) now uses
   `tideTween` with `dur 0` by default.

#### Assets

| Key | Build | Fallback |
|---|---|---|
| `tool_tide_bell` | code-built held bell; brief from [../bible/systems.md#brief-tools](../bible/systems.md#brief-tools) (image prompt "a small sea-green glass bell with a pink scallop-shell handle, wet sparkle, isolated, transparent background"; 3D "low-poly toon glass hand bell with a scallop shell handle, 400 tris"); manifest slot commented out, height 0.3 | lathe + sphere |
| `gate_tide_stone` | code-built stone; brief from [../bible/systems.md#brief-gates](../bible/systems.md#brief-gates) (300 tris; 3D "low-poly toon basalt standing stone with a carved wave spiral, 300 tris") | hex prism + glow decal |
| `icons_tools` cell `tide_bell` | generated 128² cell, packed | canvas glyph |

#### Acceptance criteria

- With `G.debug.give('tide_bell')`, beside `archwater:tide-1` the pill reads `[T] Ring · Tide stone`; one tap flips
  `S.flags['tide:archwater']` 0 ↔ 1; the water reaches the new level in 2.5 s ± 0.1; tide decks and kelp follow.
- A Pip placed on a Kelp flats cell at low water is lifted to a walkable cell within 0.8 s of the walkability swap,
  with no HP change; standing on the Raft or a path never lifts him.
- Ringing away from a stone changes nothing.
- Duskwick: ringing `duskwick:tide-1` lowers its water by 1.4 m; `chest-5` is reachable at low and not at high;
  `W.unreachable` is empty in both states; the existing high-state prints are byte-identical.
- Every Archwater tide stone is reachable at both tides.
- The solver passes and its report lists `tide → duskwick:chest-5`.
- Flags `tide:archwater` and `tide:duskwick` are `toggle` + `rewritable` in the registry; the content lint passes.
- Invariants: keep frozen home island, frozen realms (Duskwick's change is additive: a new state, no moved
  placement), one save slot (no bump), budgets, tone (no damage on a lift), assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/tide.test.js` (new): toggle effect, `liftToNearest` on a synthetic 20 × 20 grid (nearest by BFS,
  ties by index), `tideTween` schedule (walk swap at the midpoint), the gate record's `realm` / `local` behaviour.
- `bun test tests/realm-archwater.test.js tests/realm-duskwick.test.js` (stone reachability at both tides,
  Duskwick low-state checksums, coverage in both states).
- In-page (the tools suite S-102 used for the Glider: `grep -rln "glide" js/debug/tests/`): give the bell, ring
  Archwater `tide-1`, measure the water y over time, place Pip on (190, 10) at low then ring to high, assert the lift
  and HP; ring Duskwick's stone and walk to `chest-5`.
- `bun test` (solver, lint) and `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

Home tide finds (S-122b); dungeon tide rooms and stones (S-124, S-125); the beacon turning the tide (S-127b);
Threemills' stone (S-144).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: changing Duskwick's tide could expose an unreachable lagoon cell; the low-state reach test catches it, and the
fix is a recipe-free post-deck in Duskwick's gate pass. Rollback: revert; no save change, the toggle flags are inert
without the tool.

<a id="s-122b"></a>

### S-122b · feat: the Storm Cave and two rock-pool caches open on home with the Tide Bell

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-122a · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-home.md#tide-finds](../bible/atlas-home.md#tide-finds), [../bible/atlas-home.md#passes](../bible/atlas-home.md#passes),
[../bible/atlas-home.md#placePortals](../bible/atlas-home.md#placePortals), [../bible/systems.md#realm-registry](../bible/systems.md#realm-registry)

#### Why

Pacing rule 5: every tool re-opens finds at home. The Tide Bell opens three: a sea cave between the Shores islets and
two clam caches in rock pools. Roadmap: INDEX S-122 (M15).

#### What

1. **Post-pass `placeTideFinds`** (atlas-home row 13, `new RNG(7151 * 113 + 59)`, guarded `o.placeTideFinds !==
   false`, runs after row 12 `placeGrove`; if S-114 has not added row 12 yet, it still runs in table order). It writes
   only `W.tideFinds` and follows every rule of [../bible/atlas-home.md#passes](../bible/atlas-home.md#passes) 1.1:
   - `home:storm-cave`: outcrop centre `near(midpoint of the islet-2 bridge + 5 m on its south side, 0, 4)` (in water;
     the `ok()` here inverts the dry-ground test: `hAt < −0.2`), 7 × 5 m `solid` box rotated to face the bridge, mouth
     within 1.5 m of the deck edge; its tide stone `near(islet 1 centre (−40, 139), 2, 6)` with the default `ok()`,
     record `{ type: 'tide_stone', local: 'gate:home:storm-cave' }`.
   - `home:tide-cache-1`: basin centre `near((−30, 110), 0, 12)` on sand in the Shores; a ring of 9 rocks r 2.6 m
     (`solid: { r: 2.9 }`, one rock's collider `active` so it can roll aside); tide stone 3 m from the ring on the side
     nearest the path; `local: 'gate:home:tide-cache-1'`.
   - `home:tide-cache-2`: the same at `near((60, 100), 0, 12)`, ≥ 6 m from the river; `local: 'gate:home:tide-cache-2'`.
   Solids attach through `attachPost(W)` after generation (rule 7). Portal record on the storm-cave item:
   `portal: { id: 'home:portal-storm-cave', to: { realm: 'storm_cave', spawn: 'entry' }, how: 'use', needs:
   'flag:gate:home:storm-cave' }`, return spawn `storm_cave` 1.5 m out on the bridge deck.
2. **Behaviour.** Ringing a home tide stone plays the low-tide chord (`sfx.tideRing`), sets its `gate:` flag for good
   (R24; `local` stones on home are one-way: ringing again does nothing but the chime), then:
   - cave: the sea plug in the mouth (a dark `cyl` water card) sinks 1.2 s; `[E] Enter · The Storm Cave` appears on the
     bridge. Before: `[E] Look · Cave mouth` "The cave mouth is full of sea. The tide is in."
   - caches: the pool water drops in 1.2 s, the marked rock rolls 1.5 m aside (its collider turns inactive), and a clam
     cache shows `[E] Open · Clam cache` (+25 gems, `S.chests['home:tide1']` / `['home:tide2']`).
3. **Interior `storm_cave`** (realm `kind: 'interior'`, parent `home`, grid 48 m, compiled from the tile format; map
   verbatim from atlas-home):

   ```
   ####X####
   #...@...#
   #.......#
   #.__.__.#
   #.__.__.#
   #a..c..f#
   #########
   ```

   `a` = the anchor of Sleeping Acorn `home:a08` (stored only; S-161 draws and wakes it), `c` = clam chest 30 gems
   (`S.chests['home:storm1']`, item-get "+30 gems!"), `f` = fish-spot anchor `home:fish-cave` (stored; S-207). Two pools
   `_` (deep water: falling in respawns at the last safe tile, no damage). Walls hung with storm-washed things; one is
   the old oar: `[E] Look · Old oar` "An old oar with a spiral on the blade. Washed in by some storm or other." Light:
   ambient 0.45 tinted `#7be0c8` (bioluminescent pools), no enemies (`max 0`), card "The Storm Cave" / "Only at low
   tide". Prints fixture `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/storm_cave.json`.
4. **Flags** `gate:home:storm-cave`, `gate:home:tide-cache-1`, `gate:home:tide-cache-2` (family `gate:*`, already
   registered by S-013; add the ids to the doc list). `tide:home` stays in the registry unused (R24; note it there).
5. **Solver**: the three finds become items needing `ability:tide`; the report shows `tide → home: storm-cave chest,
   tide-cache-1, tide-cache-2` (pacing rule 5).
6. **Completion**: the cave chest and the two caches count in home's "chests" row
   ([../bible/systems.md#completion](../bible/systems.md#completion)); grep `COMPLETION_ROWS` in `js/completion.js` and
   add the three ids to the home chest list the row reads.

#### Assets

Code-built: `storm_cave_mouth` (grey-green rock outcrop `#6a7a76` / `#8a9a8a` with kelp `#3a6a4a`, an arched mouth,
≤ 1,000 tris) and `tide_pool_cache` (ring of `ico` rocks `#a8a096`, water disc `#7fd3d0`, clam box `#ff9f8f`, ≤ 700
tris). ART BRIEFs pasted verbatim from [../bible/atlas-home.md#tide-finds](../bible/atlas-home.md#tide-finds) with
commented-out manifest slots (heights 5.0 and 0.8). No images.

#### Acceptance criteria

- `tests/fixtures/world-prints.json` is byte-identical; `generateWorld(seed)` and `generateWorld(seed, {
  placeTideFinds: false })` have equal prints, walk, reach and unreachable on seeds 1-40.
- On seed 7151: the outcrop's mouth is within 1.5 m of the islet-2 bridge deck; the two caches are on sand in the
  Shores, ≥ 6 m from the river; every tide stone is reachable on foot.
- Ringing the islet-1 stone sets `gate:home:storm-cave`; the portal appears; entering and leaving lands Pip on the
  bridge; the clam chest gives 30 gems once; nothing resets on reload.
- Each cache gives 25 gems once after its stone is rung; without the Tide Bell the stones say "Water's too high. Or too
  low." and the caches are closed.
- `storm_cave` compiles; its prints fixture is written; the realm suite enters and leaves it ×3 with no leak.
- Invariants: keep frozen home island (post-pass rules), one save slot, budgets, assets, tone, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/world.test.js` (SLOW group: the new pass on seeds 1-40, rules (a)-(d) of atlas-home 1.1).
- `bun test` (solver, completion, interior compile, `storm_cave` prints).
- In-page (the tools suite): give the bell on home, `G.debug.warpTo` islet 1, ring, enter the cave, open the chest;
  ring both caches; reload the save and assert all three still open.
- `bun run test:playthrough` (Act I untouched) and `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

The Sleeping Acorn a08 (S-161), the fish spot (S-207), Threemills' tide stone (S-144).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: an `ok()` mistake can move nothing in home prints (passes never touch generator lists) but can put the cave
mouth off the deck; the seed-7151 assertion catches it. Rollback: revert; the `gate:` flags stay harmless in saves.

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-118b, S-122a, S-086b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.archwater.hidden: true` · Bible:

[../bible/atlas-isles.md#passes](../bible/atlas-isles.md#passes), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#coverage-rule](../bible/atlas-isles.md#coverage-rule),
[../bible/lore.md#bottle-3](../bible/lore.md#bottle-3), [../bible/lore.md#log-6](../bible/lore.md#log-6), [../bible/lore.md#log-12](../bible/lore.md#log-12)

five benches and the root-gate stub, placed by post-passes so later tools re-open them, and the 40 × 40 m coverage
proof in **both** tide states. Roadmap: INDEX S-123 (M15). Note the dependency on S-122a (added here): the
tide stones are the first entries of the gate pass.

#### What

1. **Passes** (copy Pinewhistle's finds module, `grep -rn "placeIsleChests" js/`, and change only data; streams
   `new RNG(40231 * 71 + c)`, run in this order after the recipe and S-121's turrets, each guarded `o.<pass> !== false`).
   Anchor rule: [../bible/atlas-isles.md#conventions](../bible/atlas-isles.md#conventions) ("nearest valid 1 m cell
   within r", default r 12, valid in the **tide state named**).

   | Pass (c) | Writes | Content (ids per R2) |
   |---|---|---|
   | `placeIsleBenches` (1) | `W.waystones` | `bench:archwater:b1` Landing Bench (−204, 20) on the pier · `b2` Stair Bench (−6, −44) · `b3` Arch Bench (112, −60) · `b4` Spout Bench (70, 104) · `b5` North Bench (−80, −150) (`kit_bench`, S-082 behaviour: sit heals, saves, in-land travel) |
   | `placeIsleChests` (3) | `W.chests` | `archwater:c1..c12` at the atlas anchors, each 30 gems; c3 (−190, 40) r 6 low water; c6 (200, 22) r 4 low water (the Kelp Maze dead end S-118a kept open); c7 (20, 170) r 4 on the Barnacle Ledge (rock shelf deck 3 × 3 m top h 2.2, reached at high water from the floating log); c8 (−128, −118) on the Pool Steps column; c9 (196, −84) Spire Stack top; c10 (150, −70) on the arch crown deck; c11 (−40, 160) and c12 (10, −20) buried under soft earth (`buried: true`, drawn only when dug) |
   | `placeIsleKeepsakes` (5) | `W.secrets` | `archwater:k1` keep-ring (172, −72) r 3 · `k2` keep-lens (−20, −68) r 4 · `k3` keep-shanty (90, 134) r 4 · `k4` keep-oar (−166, 0) r 2 on deck; prompts and both lines verbatim from the atlas table; 25 gems each; fifth line (all four): "A tide ring, a lens, a song and a little oar. Archwater keeps time by the tide, and the tide keeps time by the light." |
   | `placeIsleAcorns` (7) | `W.acorns` | `archwater:a1..a9` at the atlas anchors (a1 in a flowerpot on raft 1 (−170, 14), a4 low water, a8 and a9 behind blockers, item 3) |
   | `placeIsleVistas` (9) | `W.vistas` | `archwater:v1` Five Isles and One in the Sky (Watch Column top, h 54) · `v2` Under the Arch (136, −66) · `v3` The Raft at Sunset (−110, 10) r 6; `kit_vista` posts; Hoot's captions verbatim |
   | `placeIsleBottle` (11) | `W.bottles` | one bottle, collect key `archwater:3` (R4: Bottle 3, Fen; text verbatim from [../bible/lore.md#bottle-3](../bible/lore.md#bottle-3), `hand_fen` style if S-078 defined writer hands, else the default letter frame), Tidepool Shelf south-west beach (−80, 160) r 10, above high water |
   | `placeIsleLogs` (13) | `W.logs` | `kit_log_niche` at the Watch Column top (−18, −62) h 54 = Lamp Log 6 "The Catchers' Rule" (`archwater:6`); at the back of Echo Cave (60, −170) r 4 = Lamp Log 12 "The Moon Pool" (`archwater:12`) (R4; texts verbatim from lore.md) |
   | `placeIsleHollowDoors` (15) | `W.portals` (kind `hollow`) | `kit_hollow_door` at (90, −120) r 8 (ledge h 0.6, both tides), (−80, 20) r 8, (0, 120) r 8; until S-129 each is `[E] Look · Spiral door` "A round door with a spiral carved across it. Something inside is humming." |
   | `placeIsleRootGate` (17) | `W.rootGates` | `archwater:rootgate` (−60, 110) r 6, root arch on bearing 292.5° (pointing home), sealed: `[E] Look · Root-gate` "Sealed with roots." |
   | `placeIsleSites` (19) | `W.sites` | the 16 sites of [../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-poi](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-poi), plus two quest-prop sites: `archwater:plugs` (three 1 m basalt plugs on the Great Tidepool's rim, 2.5 m outward from the drain notches at bearings 0°, 120°, 240° about (50, 130); inert until S-127a) and `archwater:cage` (S-118b's lamp cage on the column top). The Urchin Garden site has its Look line |
   | `placeIsleCurios` (21) | `W.curios` | 17 curios, lines verbatim from the atlas: 4 tide chalk, 4 empty lamp-stands (`kit_ruin_lampstump`, shared line), 3 counted hexagons, 4 glass floats, 2 gull signs |
   | `placeIsleGates` (23) | `W.gates` | after S-122a's five tide stones: `hookbloom` `archwater:hook-1` on the arch's west shoulder (140, −70) at h 22 (a short stalk out of the arch's flank) and `archwater:hook-2` on the keystone (150, −70) h 27; `soft_earth` `archwater:dig-1` (−40, 160) and `dig-2` (10, −20); `gap` record `archwater:gap-1` arch crown → Spire Stack top (for the solver); blockers of item 3 |
   | `placeIsleFill` (29) | `W.curios` | coverage fill from the pool (glass float, counted hexagons), last |

2. **Gate types not yet built.** `hookbloom` (S-143a) and `soft_earth` (S-181) are placed **look-only**: if `js/gates.js`
   has no entry yet (`grep -n "hookbloom\|soft_earth" js/gates.js`; S-086/S-101 may have added them), register
   `{ type, look, needs, prompt, without }` from [../bible/systems.md#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates) with no use
   behaviour. Without the tool: hookbloom shows no prompt (a chime when in range comes with S-143a), soft earth `[E]
   Look · Soft earth` "Soft earth. Something's down there."
3. **Blockers for later gates** (Duskwick's chest-10 precedent): acorn a8 (−150, −70) sits in a 3 m basalt nook (two
   column-stamp walls); its mouth is closed by a fallen-column collider `archwater:block-thorn` (a 2.4 m prism lying
   across it, `[E] Look · Fallen column` "A basalt column has toppled across the gap. Something glassy glints behind
   it.") that S-233 replaces with thornglass. Acorn a9 sits in a 2 × 2 m alcove behind a plain carved slab
   `archwater:block-star` (`[E] Look · Carved slab` "A stone slab carved with a moon. It feels like it's waiting for
   night.") on a new **west-leg ledge**: a 4 × 3 m deck at h 14 centred (132, −73), reached from the arch shoulder by a
   column stair (14 steps of 0.6 m, each a 1.2 m bridge deck, wrapping the leg's west face from (124, −66)); S-208
   turns the slab into a star door. The ledge is also the launch point for hook-1 (R in atlas: "hooked from the west
   leg top (132, −70) h 14"). These decks are part of the realm build (prints refreshed; the realm is still hidden).
4. **Vista sketches** `sketch_archwater_1..3` (kind `sketch`, 1024 × 576 → 512 × 288 webp q 80, ≤ 45 KB, `realm:
