   'archwater'`), preamble + `sketch` suffix + subject: v1 "the view from the top of a tall basalt column: a ring of
   five small islands on a calm sea and one floating island high in the sky"; v2 "a huge natural sea arch of dark
   hexagonal basalt seen from underneath, a round pale-teal pool below it"; v3 "a cosy village of lashed log rafts in
   a rocky bay at sunset, little round-roofed cabins, a tall mast with bunting". Fallback: the minimap crop canvas.
5. **Coverage** (`js/realms/coverage.js`, S-101's module): run for both tide states; record `landCells, passFind,
   passView, passPath, filled` per state in the PR body and in `docs/roadmap/progress.md` at S-130. Expected ≈ 76
   (high) / 86 (low) land cells, fill ≤ 4. If the outer east flats fail at low, add glass-float curios there (atlas
   note), never raise the fill cap.
6. **POI count**: a unit assertion that Archwater's POI anchors ≥ 88 (finds 45 + sites 16 + curios 17 + gates 8 +
   quest props 2 = 88; people from S-120 bring it to 95+).
7. **Completion and collect wiring**: `stats.js` / `completion.js` land table gains `archwater: { chests: [c1..c12],
   keepsakes: [k1..k4], acorns: [a1..a9], hollows: [1..3], vistas: [v1..v3], bottles: ['archwater:3'], logs:
   ['archwater:6', 'archwater:12'], benches: [b1..b5] }` (the shape S-101 used). The Archwater group stays hidden in
   the Completion page while the realm is hidden.

**Prize chests** (CANON §10.4; rows already in `js/content/prizes.js`, opened by S-086a's chest path): this land's
three prize chests give their item instead of 30 gems: `c2` Raft Hammock, `c10` Tidepool Teal trim, `c7` "Sea Arch" sticker. Add one unit test that each listed chest resolves to its
prize, and keep `STATS` chest counts unchanged.

#### Assets

`sketch_archwater_1..3` (generated, above). Everything else reuses the shared kit (`kit_bench`, `kit_vista`,
`kit_log_niche`, `kit_hollow_door`, `kit_root_gate`, `kit_ruin_lampstump`, `sign`) and S-118a's props; the fallen
column and carved slab are code-built (prism `#36414f`, slab with a canvas moon decal `#e2c9a0`), ≤ 200 tris each,
no brief needed (temporary until S-208 / S-233).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Counts: 12 chests, 4 keepsakes, 9 acorns, 3 vistas, 1 bottle, 2 Lamp Logs, 3 Hollow doors, 5 benches, 1 root-gate;
  every find within its anchor radius (unit test per id).
- Reachability by state: c3, c6, a4 and k1 (East Stack) reachable at low only; c7 and c8 at high only; c5 and log 12
  inside dark zones (solver: `lantern`); c9 needs `glide` from the crown deck, c10 needs `hook`; c11/c12 need `dig`;
  a8 needs `sword3`; a9 needs `starsong` at night; everything else on foot in both states.
- The progression solver passes with these gates (finds behind unshipped tools are listed as "awaits <tool>").
- Coverage passes in both tide states with fill ≤ 12 % of land cells; the numbers are in the PR body.
- POI anchors ≥ 88.
- Sketches ≤ 45 KB, realm-tagged; Archwater bundle ≤ 2.5 MB.
- Prints refreshed; home prints untouched; realm still hidden.
- Invariants: keep frozen home island, frozen realms (prints refreshed in the build milestone only), hidden until
  whole, budgets, assets, IP, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/realm-archwater.test.js tests/realm-coverage.test.js` (counts, anchors, per-state reach, coverage
  both states, POI count).
- `bun test` (solver report lists Archwater's finds per tool; completion table; content lint for every new text).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (four views with finds: still within budget; enter/leave ×3).
- In-page spot check in the S-101 finds suite: open c1 (+30 gems), read the bottle (collect `bottle:'archwater:3'`),
  read Lamp Log 6 at the column top, sit on b2.

#### Out of scope

Pearl interactables (S-127a); Hollows (S-129); Hook behaviour (S-143a); digging (S-181); star doors (S-208); thornglass
(S-233); fishing spots (S-206); story marks (S-131, S-132).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the coverage test in two states doubles the chance of a thin cell; the atlas's fill-pool plan covers the flats.
Rollback: revert; the realm is hidden and no save holds Archwater ids yet.

<a id="s-124"></a>

### S-124 · feat: the Undertow Grotto rooms 1-7: rooms that flip between two water levels

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-122a, S-053 · Size: M · Save: none (uses the v8 `dungeons {}` container) · Flag:
`REALMS.undertow_grotto.hidden: true` · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#d3](../bible/dungeons.md#d3),
[../bible/dungeons.md#format](../bible/dungeons.md#format), [../bible/dungeons.md#entities](../bible/dungeons.md#entities),
[../bible/dungeons.md#state](../bible/dungeons.md#state), [../bible/dungeons.md#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules)

#### Why

Dungeon 3, first half: the Undertow Grotto's signature mechanic, rooms whose water is low or high. Conch shells flip a
**basin** (a group of rooms) and rafts float when it is high. Roadmap: INDEX S-124 (M15).

#### What

1. **Data file** `js/realms/dungeons/undertow_grotto.js` (R1: realm id `undertow_grotto`; music variant `undertow`),
   shape of [../bible/dungeons.md#format](../bible/dungeons.md#format): `id: 'undertow_grotto', name: 'The Undertow
   Grotto', kind: 'dungeon', tile: 2, palette: 'undertow', music: 'undertow', light: { ambient: 0.6, torches: true,
   sun: false, tod: 0.5 }, parent: 'archwater', door: 'grotto'`. The composite map `undertow L1` is pasted **verbatim
   and whole** (all 14 rooms) from dungeons.md#d3 so `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` (S-049) matches; `rooms` lists r1-r14
   with the boxes from the room table; this step fills `entities`, `chests` and `spawns` for r1-r7 only.
2. **Sealed half.** Door (5,16) r7 → r8 gets `cond: 'sealed'` and door (35,8) r4 ↔ r12 keeps `switch:r12:s1` (r12 is
   unreachable this step). `sealed` is a new door condition meaning "not built yet": never opens; the compiler test
   fails if a realm with `hidden: false` contains one. S-125 replaces it with `always`.
3. **Tide entities** in `js/dungeon-mod.js` (legend extension `,` `;` `o`; `Q` arrives in S-125):
   - Basins from the bible: **A** = r2, r3 (starts `low`), **B** = r5, r6, r7 (starts `high`); state saved in
     `S.dungeons.undertow_grotto.state['tide:A' | 'tide:B']`.
   - Compile `,` and `;` as pit tiles (heightfield −4) covered by bridge decks with an `active` flag: `,` → a "tide
     floor" deck at h 0, active at **low**; `;` → a raft deck at h 0 (code-built `int_raft`, five round logs bound with
     rope, 2 × 2 m), active at **high**. Each basin has one water plane over its rooms' `,`/`;` tiles: y −1.2 at low
     (visible only in the pits) and −0.02 at high; the tide-floor deck mesh drops from y 0 to −1.0 under the water at
     high (wet sand look `#c9b89a`), the rafts rise from −1.0 to 0 and bob ±0.05 m.
   - A flip takes **1.5 s** (water plane, floor and rafts tween; `sfx.floodRise` / `floodFall`); walkability swaps at
     0.75 s; **never strand**: S-122a's `liftToNearest` within the room (0.4 s fade, no damage). Pits and deep water
     elsewhere follow R4 (respawn at the last safe tile, no damage).
   - Conch `o` (`int_conch`: 0.9 m pearl spiral conch `#e2c9a0` with a pink lip `#f4a6a0` on a rock; glows aqua
     `#7fd3d0` 0.5 s when it flips): a sword hit (or beam / ember later) flips its basin; 0.5 s cooldown; conches:
     `r2:o1` (12,6) and `r3:o1` (21,1) flip A; `r5:o1` (29,9) and `r7:o1` (9,10) flip B.
   - Enemies standing on tiles that flood poof quietly (no drop); enemy waves on `,` floors start only once the floor
     is dry (`spawns[].when: 'tide:A=low'`).
4. **Rooms r1-r7** exactly as the room notes: r1 The Sea Door (`X` (0,4) to Archwater spawn `grotto` (128, −62), R6;
   sign `?` (3,3) "When the arch lamp is lit, the sea comes and goes on time. When it isn't, ring for it."); r2 The
   Conch Hall (map chest `&` (14,2) at low; rafts to the east door (20,4) `always` at high); r3 The Tidepool Room
   (wave 1 Tidepool Jelly ×3 at (23,3), (27,3), (25,6) at low; `clear` → small key `r3:k1` (25,4); east door (30,4)
   `always`; south `L` (25,8)); r4 The Urchin Gallery (no tide; four pillars; wave Squirt Urchin ×3 at (35,3), (33,5),
   (37,5); gem chest `r4:c1` (39,1)); r5 The Kelp Pass (B: dry stone (25,9), rafts to `r5:o1`; door (20,12) `always`);
   r6 The Snapper Sands (B: wave Sand Snapper ×3 at (13,14), (17,14), (17,10) at low, the `,` floor counts as sand;
   door (10,12) `clear`; gem chest `r6:c1` (14,10) reached only at high from the raft ring); r7 The Drowned Stair (B:
   small key `r7:k1` (5,12) and Chime Stone `%` (5,9) at low; conch (9,10)). Gem chests give 40 gems. Torches are
   sea-glass lamps; `fx_caustics` cards scroll on the walls of tide rooms.
5. **Realm registry** `undertow_grotto: { kind: 'dungeon', parent: 'archwater', size: 102, hidden: true, palette:
   'undertow', music: 'undertow', bundle: 'archwater', tod: 0.5, needsOnEntry: ['lantern'] }` (no spawns roll: waves
   only). Name card on first entry: "The Undertow Grotto" / "beneath the Great Arch". The Archwater portal
   `archwater:grotto` (S-118b, sealed) now points here (still needs `flag:aw:door`).
6. **Solver** (`bun test`): tide states join the BFS state (`tide:A`, `tide:B`); the bible's solver notes for r1-r7
   replay as a script up to door (5,16); proves map, Chime Stone, `r4:c1`, `r6:c1`, `r3:k1`, `r7:k1` reachable and no
   soft-lock (a conch is always reachable from both sides of its basin, so no state traps Pip).
7. **Hints** (Ask the Tree, R10): 1 "Shells by the water remember the tide." / "Swing at the conch shell. The water in
   this part of the grotto goes up or down, and rafts float when it is high." 2 "The chest in the Snapper Sands floats
   on its own little ring." / "Flip the Kelp Pass conch to high water, then walk the rafts from the east side of the
   Snapper Sands to the chest."
8. **Prints** `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/undertow_grotto.json` (both basin states recorded as walk checksums for
   the four A/B combinations).

#### Assets

| Key | Build / generate | Processing | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tex_basalt` (`realm: archwater`) | kind `tile`: "<style line> Seamless texture of hexagonal basalt columns seen from the side, cool blue-grey, soft sea-glass highlights, a little barnacle and kelp, tileable" | `python3 -I tools/make_seamless.py --blend 64`, 1024/512 webp q 82 | hex canvas `#36414f` / `#4b5768` |
| `fx_caustics` | card 512², transparent painted water-caustic pattern, soft aqua, loops when scrolled | webp | off |
| `int_conch`, `int_raft` | code-built (above); ART BRIEFs from dungeons.md d3 "Art" table in the PR body; manifest slots commented out (0.9, 0.4) | - | kit switch tinted pearl; plank deck |

#### Acceptance criteria

- The map in the data file equals dungeons.md's `undertow L1` byte for byte (`tests/dungeon-maps.test.js`).
- Compiler and solver pass for r1-r7 (exit, every chest and key reachable, no soft-lock, the solver-notes script
  replays) and report door (5,16) as `sealed`.
- A basin flip takes 1.5 s; a Pip standing on a `,` tile when it floods is lifted within 0.4 s with no HP change;
  rafts carry Pip at high; falling into a pit respawns with no damage.
- State survives leaving and re-entering (tides, chests, keys, opened doors).
- Dungeon budgets: ≤ 120 draw calls, ≤ 150 k tris in every room view, genMs ≤ 150 ms.
- Realm hidden; reachable only by `G.debug.realm('undertow_grotto')`.
- Invariants: keep frozen realms, hidden until whole, one save slot, budgets, tone (no damage from water), assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/dungeon-undertow.test.js` (new: compile, solver with basin states,
  replay of the notes to (5,16), `sealed` door count).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (enter/leave ×3, room views under budget) and the dungeon suite S-103 used
  (`grep -rln "whistlehollow" js/debug/tests/`): hit `r2:o1`, assert water y and raft walkability over 1.5 s, stand on
  (14,2) at low then flip and assert the lift.

#### Out of scope

Rooms r8-r14, tide stones, the Tide Bell chest (S-125); Old Gloop (S-126).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: deck-based tide floors can fight the heightfield shadow; keep `occ: true` only on walls. Rollback: revert; hidden.

<a id="s-125"></a>

### S-125 · feat: the Undertow Grotto rooms 8-14: the Tide Bell, tide stones and the way to Old Gloop

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-124 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.undertow_grotto.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/dungeons.md#d3](../bible/dungeons.md#d3), [../bible/systems.md#tool-tide](../bible/systems.md#tool-tide)

#### Why

The second half of dungeon 3: the Tide Bell is found in the Bell Chamber and immediately rung at tide stones to drain
and fill basin C, a push-block becomes a stepping stone at high water, the Root Key sits on a shelf only high water
reaches, and the boss room waits under the arch. Roadmap: INDEX S-125 (M15).

#### What

1. **Unseal**: door (5,16) `cond: 'always'`. Fill `entities`, `chests`, `spawns` for r8-r14 exactly as
   [../bible/dungeons.md#d3](../bible/dungeons.md#d3):
   - r8 The Bell Chamber: treasure chest `$` (5,20) on a rock in a shallow pool (`w` tiles): the Tide Bell (`give:tide_bell`,
     `ability:tide`; item-get line from S-122a). East door (10,20) `cond: 'chest:r8:$1'` (opens when the bell is taken).
   - r9 The Stone Basin (basin **C**, starts `high`): rafts (12-14,20) to tide stone `r9:Q1` (15,20); ring it → C low;
     locked door (20,20) uses `r7:k1`; wave at low: Squirt Urchin ×2 at (13,18), (17,22).
   - r10 The Pearl Hall (C): push-block `r10:b1` (23,18) E ×5 to (28,18), S ×2 onto hold plate `r10:P1` (28,20); door
     (30,20) `plate:r10:P1`; reset lever `r10:r1` (22,22). **Block on a `,` tile at high water**: it lowers 1.3 m with
     the tide floor so its top sits 0.6 m above the water and becomes a deck (a stepping stone, hop up); it cannot be
     pushed until low (pushing it plays a soft "bonk").
   - r11 The Echo Cave (C): Root Key chest `K` (38,18) on a digit-2 shelf reached at high by the row-20 rafts and raft
     columns (35-36, 17-20) then a hop; at low the shelf is cut off and doors north (33,16) to r12 and south (35,24) to
     r13 are walkable; tide stone `r11:Q1` (31,22). Every sound in r11 repeats at 0.3 s and 0.6 s (a two-tap delay on
     the room's audio send, −6 dB and −12 dB).
   - r12 The Kelp Curtain: kelp curtain cards (`card_kelp`, 512 × 1024 alpha, hung from the 5 m ceiling); wave Tidepool
     Jelly ×2 at (37,12), (35,13); gem chest `r12:c1` (39,10); crystal switch `r12:s1` (35,9) opens door (35,8) to r4
     for good (the shortcut).
   - r13 Gloop's Larder: decor `int_sock_line` (rope between two posts with eight small knitted socks in the cast.md
     sock colours `#ff8f7a #f2c94c #7fd3d0 #8fb8ff #f59aa8 #9bd46a #c9b6ec` and, in slot 8, the grey-blue dish-cloth
     `#9aa7b4` (R8)), four barrels of pickled kelp; gem chest `r13:c1` (38,30); boss door `B` (30,28).
   - r14 The Moon Pool Hollow (ceiling 14; arena 19 × 17 tiles, R9): the deep pool `~` (cols 15-25, rows 25-28); boss
     spawn `M` (20,27) holds, this step, an inert placeholder: a large purple rounded rock in the pool with `[E] Look ·
     Something in the pool` "Something big is asleep in the pool. It is wearing socks."; the arch beacon `!` (20,39)
     (S-095's beacon entity, `isle: 'archwater'`; kindle needs `boss:undertow_grotto:gloop`, so it stays dark until
     S-126); a shaft of daylight card from above lights it; `X` (20,42) to Archwater spawn `grotto` (R6).
2. **Tide stones in rooms** `Q`: S-122a's `tide_stone` gate with `basin: 'C'` (flips the basin, 1.5 s, not the realm);
   prompt `[T] Ring · Tide stone` (the tool key; the dungeon legend's `[E]` is replaced by the context-first tool pill
   of [../bible/systems.md#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools)). Without the bell: "Water's too high. Or too low."
3. **Keys and chests ledger** (bible): `r3:k1` → L (25,8); `r7:k1` → L (20,20); `K` (38,18) → B (30,28); gem chests
   `r4:c1`, `r6:c1`, `r12:c1`, `r13:c1` = 160 gems; map r2, Chime Stone r7, Tide Bell r8.
4. **Solver**: the full solver notes of d3 replay from `@` to the kindle tile and back to `X` (the boss step is skipped
   by a solver flag `bossCalmed: true` until S-126), "every chest" includes the optional `r6:c1` detour; the Tide Bell
   joins the solver's tools when `$` opens.
5. **Hint 3**: "A Root Key on a shelf above the rafts." / "In the Echo Cave, ring the tide stone for high water, ride the
   rafts to the shelf and hop up. Ring it again for low water to leave."
6. **Map card** (R8 of the dungeon rules): with the map every room shows; with the Chime Stone unopened chests are stars,
   the boss room a spiral, the Root Key a gold dot.
7. Refresh `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/undertow_grotto.json` (the completing PR for the layout).

#### Assets

| Key | Build / generate | Fallback |
|---|---|---|
| `card_kelp` | card 512 × 1024 alpha: "hanging strands of green-gold kelp, painterly, isolated" (white keyed) | green quads |
| `int_tide_stone` (dungeon skin of `gate_tide_stone`: 1.6 × 0.9 × 0.6 m with a bronze bell cup `#b0793a`) | code-built; ART BRIEF from dungeons.md d3 pasted verbatim | grey box + spiral decal |
| `int_sock_line` | code-built rope + eight sock tubes | - |

#### Acceptance criteria

- Compiler passes with no `sealed` door left; the full solver passes: exit and boss door reachable, all 4 gem chests,
  map, Chime Stone, Root Key and the Tide Bell reachable, no soft-lock; the notes replay.
- Taking the Tide Bell opens door (10,20) and gives `ability:tide`; ringing `r9:Q1` drains basin C in 1.5 s; r10's
  block becomes a stepping stone at high and is pushable at low.
- The r12 switch opens door (35,8) for good; reload keeps it open.
- Dungeon budgets hold in every room view (r14 at full size included): ≤ 120 calls, ≤ 150 k tris.
- Hidden; prints refreshed.
- Invariants: keep frozen realms, hidden until whole, one save slot, budgets, tone, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/dungeon-undertow.test.js` (full solver, ledger counts, no `sealed`).
- In-page dungeon suite: from `@` with `G.debug.give('lantern')`, warp to r8, open `$`, ring `r9:Q1`, push the block,
  assert door (30,20) opens; ring `r11:Q1` and stand on the shelf.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

Old Gloop and the handover (S-126); the overworld tide turn on kindling (S-127b).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the "block becomes a stepping stone" state can trap the block on a pit tile; the reset lever and the solver's
soft-lock check cover it. Rollback: revert; hidden.

<a id="s-125b"></a>

### S-125b · feat: Undertow Grotto grows the Sluice Hall and the Sluice-Keeper's Cell: basins B and C become one (depth rule R14)

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-125, S-060b, S-104b (shared annex pieces and the latch) · Size: M (200-500; most of it map data and entity tables) · Save: none · Flag: the dungeon realm stays `hidden: true` · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#d3-depth](../bible/dungeons.md#d3-depth), [../bible/dungeons.md#depth-common](../bible/dungeons.md#depth-common), [../bible/dungeons.md#depth-counts](../bible/dungeons.md#depth-counts), [../bible/dungeons.md#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules), [../bible/dungeons.md#d3](../bible/dungeons.md#d3)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md R14, CANON §11.1 A2; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after
S-125 and before S-126, which now depends on it.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M15 · Archwater and the midpoint**. Every story dungeon had about one simple beat per room and almost no room where two
mechanics meet, so Undertow Grotto played in well under its budget and taught one idea without building on it. Rule R14
asks for ≥ 1.6 beats per counted room, ≥ 4 compound rooms, one mid-dungeon state flip that sends the player back
through rooms already solved, and a last room before the boss that uses everything. This step builds
Undertow Grotto's annex, flip, latch and synthesis from the bible, so the dungeon meets the rule before its boss lands.

#### What
1. **Annex levels.** Copy the map blocks of [dungeons.md#d3-depth](../bible/dungeons.md#d3-depth) verbatim
   into the dungeon's data module (`js/realms/dungeons/<id>.js`, the file its room steps created): `undertow LA` (`at` [43,0]): r15 The Sluice Hall (basin D); `undertow LB` (`at` [43,10]): r16 The Sluice-Keeper's Cell (basin E); extent 54 × 43, realm grid 124 m. Room
   table rows, entity rows and the room-by-room conditions exactly as the bible gives them. Annex levels join the §5
   rooms only through stair warps.
2. **Stairs.** r15 `^`(1,7) ↔ r13 (35,26) (the larder hatch, worked by the new tide stone `r13:Q1`); r16 `^`(1,7) ↔ r7 (5,13), a hatch in the pool floor, sealed until the flip and usable only at low water.
3. **Tile edits to the §5 map** (apply to the bible's map block and to the data in this PR; the map test compares
   against the edited block): r13 (35,26) `.` → `^`; r13 (31,26) `.` → `Q` (`r13:Q1`); r7 (5,13) `,` → `^`. Update the bible's room table, keys-and-chests ledger and solver notes to
   match, in the same PR.
4. **The flip.** **the Moon Sluice** `r15:s1` (9,1), flag `ug.sluice`, line `dg.ug.flip` "The sea pulls at the sluice. Two basins sigh and become one.". From then on: basins B and C become one basin **BC** (state `tide:BC`, starting at C's level): every conch in r5/r7 and tide stone in r9/r11 flips r5, r6, r7, r9, r10, r11 together. **Q = r10**: the surge washes `r10:b1` back to (23,18) and door (30,20) closes; push it again (E5, S2) at low. **P = r7**: the pool hatch (5,13) unseals; it opens only at low water. Saved as `S.dungeons[<id>].state.flip = true` (v8
   container, no bump); the dungeon compiler applies the listed entity-state changes when it builds Q and P; the first
   entry into each after the flip plays the 0.4 s result cue (R7).
5. **The latch.** `B` (30,28) opens on `key:K && latch:r16:s1 && clear:r13`. Hitting the latch plays the toast `dg.latch` and a positional clunk at `B`.
6. **Synthesis in the room before the boss** (R14 item 4): r13 gets a wave (Sand Snapper ×2, Tidepool Jelly ×1; `clear` for `B`) and the tide stone `r13:Q1` (31,26) that opens the larder hatch at its "low" and shuts it at "high".
7. **Ask the Tree** gains two hint pairs for this dungeon (systems#hints): "Gloop's larder has a cellar, if the tide lets you in." / "Ring the larder's tide stone, go down the hatch to the Sluice Hall, push the block across at low water and turn the Moon Sluice." · "Two basins are one now. Choose your tide carefully." /
   "Set the joined basin to low water, push the Pearl Hall block back onto its plate, and open the hatch in the Drowned Stair's pool." (text ids `dg.undertow.hint.4`, `.5`).
8. **Solver and depth test.** The flip is one more state bit in the lock-and-key graph (S-049); the solver proves the
   whole path (§5 rooms → annex → flip → Q and P re-solved → latch → `B`), every chest, and no soft-lock whether the
   player flips early or late. Extend `tests/dungeon-depth.test.js` (S-060b) with this dungeon's row: 16 rooms, 14 counted, ≥ 23 beats (planned 27), ≥ 4 compound rooms (planned 6); the test prints the per-room table
   for the PR body.

#### Assets
- The annex rooms reuse this dungeon's kit, textures and palette (no new generated images; the annex rooms are within
  the realm bundle already budgeted).
- **`int_moon_sluice`** (code-built, ≤ 160 tris; the flip prop): a small stone sluice gate with a pearl-inlaid wheel on top, sea-glass rivets. ART BRIEF: silhouette as described;
  size 1.4 m; palette `#36414f` `#e2c9a0` `#7fd3d0`; toon materials; 160 / 600 tris (code / painted GLB); pose static, it
  turns or swings once when used; image prompt "<style line> a small stone sluice gate with a pearl-inlaid wheel on top, sea-glass rivets, cosy storybook dungeon prop, three-quarter
  view"; 3D prompt "stylised low-poly a small stone sluice gate with a pearl-inlaid wheel on top, sea-glass rivets, hand-painted texture, game-ready, under 600
  triangles, 1.4 m". Manifest key `int_moon_sluice` (commented-out GLB slot). Fallback: the code-built model.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (this dungeon's prints are refreshed in this PR, inside its own build milestone,
  before the land is unhidden), *Hidden until whole*, *One save slot* (no bump), *Budgets* (R11: every annex room view
  ≤ 120 draw calls and ≤ 150 k triangles), *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the annex blocks and the edited §5 block; data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves the full path, every chest, the flip in both orders, and no soft-lock; the depth test row passes
  the thresholds above.
- In page: hitting the flip changes Q and P exactly as listed; `B` stays shut until the latch (or d1's plate) and the
  Root Key; the hints show in Ask the Tree.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/dungeon-depth.test.js` and the
  dungeon's solver test.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `undertow_depth`: `G.debug.realm('<id>')`,
  play the intended path from the annex stair with `G.debug.dungeon.solve('<id>', { from: 'annex' })`, assert the flip's
  changes in Q and P, the latch, and `B`).
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (prints refreshed, budgets).

#### Out of scope
- The boss (S-126); any new chest, key or reward (the 48-chest ledger is fixed); the land overworld.

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the flip makes a room unsolvable from the side the player returns by; the solver run in both flip orders is the
guard, and the bible's fallback (§5A.1) adds a block and plate. Rollback: revert the PR; the §5 rooms work alone
(stair warps to a missing annex show as sealed hatches) and the realm stays hidden.

<a id="s-126"></a>

### S-126 · feat: Old Gloop, the octopus holding the sea still

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-125, S-045, S-125b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.undertow_grotto.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/creatures.md#boss-gloop](../bible/creatures.md#boss-gloop), [../bible/creatures.md#boss-def](../bible/creatures.md#boss-def),
[../bible/creatures.md#hp-formula](../bible/creatures.md#hp-formula), [../bible/creatures.md#intro-handover](../bible/creatures.md#intro-handover),
[../bible/cast.md#old-gloop](../bible/cast.md#old-gloop)

#### Why

The Warden of the Great Arch: a grandmotherly octopus in eight knitted socks who has held the sea still so the beached
Raft would not tip. Act II's only **puzzle-boss** (the other three Act II encounters are bouts): each arm grips one of
eight tide stones, the lit stone says which arm will let go, and Pip wins by reading the arena ("Arms let go 0/8"), not
by wearing down a number. Roadmap: INDEX S-126 (M15).

#### What

1. **Def** `js/boss/gloop.js` (one file per boss, registered in `BOSS_DEFS`; mirror S-105's `mossmantle.js`):

   ```js
   BOSS_DEFS['archwater.gloop'] = {
     id: 'archwater.gloop', key: 'undertow_grotto:gloop',       // key = S.bosses key (systems.md#ids); use S-105's field name
     name: 'Old Gloop', title: 'Eight-Socked Warden of the Great Arch', kind: 'puzzle',
     model: 'boss_gloop', height: 4.0, r: 1.6, keepOut: 2.2, room: 3.2, talkAt: 3.0,
     hp: null, arms: 8, bar: 'Arms let go {n}/8', group: 'act2Wardens',     // counts as a Warden for the others' HP
     window: [2.5, 2.2, 1.9],          // Tide-Bell gold window (s) by Wardens calmed before (0 / 1 / 2)
     stones: [[14,25],[14,27],[15,29],[18,29],[22,29],[25,29],[26,27],[26,25]],   // rim tiles, stone n = arm n
     enrageAt: 4, cool: [1.6, 2.6],    // P2 when 4 arms have let go (a count, not an HP fraction)
     arena: { realm: 'undertow_grotto', room: 'r14', pool: { tiles: [15, 25, 25, 28] }, seal: 'vines', wallBonk: false },
     phases: [ { letGo: 0, lit: 1, table: [['sweep', 45], ['ink', 20], ['juggle', 35]] },
               { letGo: 4, lit: 2, table: [['sweep', 35], ['ink', 20], ['juggle', 30], ['double', 15]] } ],
     moves: {
       sweep:  { telegraph: 'stripe', wind: 0.8, act: 0.5, recover: 0.4, open: 1.5, dmg: 1, threats: 1 },
       ink:    { telegraph: 'disc',   wind: 1.2, act: 0.2, recover: 0.6, open: 0,   dmg: 1, threats: 1, linger: 4.0 },
       juggle: { telegraph: 'reticle', wind: 0.8, act: 0.9, recover: 0.4, open: 2.0, dmg: 1, threats: 1, count: 3, gap: 1.0 },
       double: { telegraph: 'stripe', wind: 0.8, act: 0.5, recover: 0.6, open: 1.5, dmg: 1, threats: 2, gap: 0.6 } },
     ... };
   ```

   **The puzzle runner** (`kind: 'puzzle'`, extending S-061a's puzzle state): at the intro it draws a permutation of
   arms 1-8 from `new RNG(fightSeed)`; the next un-released arm's stone is **lit** (glow 0 → 0.8 in the sock colour, its
   carved pattern shining, its pitch chiming every 2 s; HUD: a 24 px swatch of the sock beside the bar text). From 4/8
   two stones are lit at once (either order). An **opening** on a lit arm comes three ways: (a) `[E] Ring · Tide stone`
   on the lit stone (3 m) lifts that arm onto the rim, wiggling, gold for `window[calmedBefore]` s; (b) the lit arm's
   missed `sweep` sticks 1.5 s gold; (c) a batted-back urchin dazes Gloop 2.0 s and the lit arm flops onto the rim,
   gold. One sword hit on the lit arm's sock (hurtbox r 0.6) in an opening **releases** it: the arm uncurls from its stone
   (`sfx.gloopPlop`), the stone's light goes out, the bar reads n + 1/8, and the next stone lights after 1.5 s. Any other
   hit on an arm: "Tickles, dearie!" (text `archwater.boss.gloop.tickle`, once per 6 s), the shrug shove, nothing
   else. Ringing an unlit stone only chimes. 8/8 → the handover. Dizzy (rule 7): the fight restarts from 0/8 with a new
   permutation. `hp(def, S)` is unchanged for Mossmantle and Honksworth: Gloop counts as a calmed Warden for them.
   Debug: `G.debug.actors.boss('gloop').lit()`, `.letGo(n)`.

   Move details (numbers from creatures.md; arena from the solved map, R9): Gloop sits in the north pool at `M` (20,27).
   - `sweep`: arm k (1-8, picked so consecutive sweeps differ) rises from the pool rim nearest Pip, its sock glows and
     wiggles, a wet slap sound; a radial **stripe 9 m × 1.6 m** from the pool rim toward Pip's position at wind start,
     coral with the sock's colour as its edge line. If Pip is not in it, the arm slaps the floor at its end and
     **sticks 1.5 s gold** ("Now!") if it is a lit stone's arm (other arms recoil at once). Sweeps come only from arms
     that have not let go; after 6/8 only lit arms sweep; a sweep never crosses a lit stone's tile.
   - `ink`: `sfx.inkGulp` (the gulp), then she swells for **1.2 s**; a disc r 4 m on Pip's spot at wind start; lands
     0.2 s; leaves a dark ink zone for 4 s that does no damage after landing; no sweep stripe may cross a live ink zone
     (the picker rejects such an arm).
   - `juggle`: she juggles three knitted urchins, humming; three reticles 1.0 s apart (wind 0.8 each, flight 0.9);
     each urchin glints `#fff3a0` in its last 0.3 s: a Swing inside 2 m bats it back; a returned urchin bonks her →
     she is dazed and the lit arm flops onto the rim beside its stone, **2.0 s gold**.
   - `double` (P2 only): two sweeps on different arms 0.6 s apart (two threats; nothing else live).
   - **Tide stones** (boss-def props, not map glyphs, R9): eight `int_tide_stone_sock` at the `stones` rim tiles above
     (all floor tiles of r14's map); there is no P2 flood (the two lit stones are P2's change).
   - Grammar: telegraph ≥ 0.55 s everywhere; ≤ 2 threats; every hit on Pip is 1 HP; passive contact never hurts; at
     4/8 `speedK` 1.4, cool 1.2-2.0 s.
2. **Socks** (R8, cast.md): arm 1 coral `#ff8f7a` stripes, 2 sunflower `#f2c94c` dots, 3 mint `#7fd3d0` zigzag, 4 sky
   `#8fb8ff` hearts, 5 rose `#f59aa8` stars, 6 lime `#9bd46a` checks, 7 lilac `#c9b6ec` plain, 8 grey-blue dish-cloth
   `#9aa7b4` (Fair Isle look on the dish-cloth's edge) until `ss25.done`, then orange stripes `#f2994a` / `#fff3e0`
   (S-132). Patterns are canvas textures so colour is never the only cue; each arm's wind-up and its stone's chime
   share its pitch (C4 D4 E4 F4 G4 A4 B4 C5 for arms 1-8, a soft `pluck`); each stone carries the same pattern.
3. **Model** `boss_gloop` (code-built one-off case in `js/models.js`): mantle `sphere` r 1.2 `#6b4a8c` with suckers
   `#c9b6ec` (vertex dots), kind gold eyes `#f2c94c` with lids, pink blush `#f59aa8`, eight `tube` arms (6 segments,
   length 2.6 m) as child nodes `arm1`..`arm8` with sock `tube` sleeves (canvas sock textures above), so a tell can tint
   one arm. 4.0 m with arms raised; ≤ 6,000 tris, ≤ 6 materials. Animations by transform: idle curl, arm raise, slap,
   stuck wiggle, gulp swell (scale 1.0 → 1.18), juggle, sit-down dizzy (lean back 20°, three stars 1.5 s).
4. **Intro and handover** (S-045 flow): entering r14 seals `B` with glowing vines; 2.4 s intro; name card "Old Gloop" /
   "Eight-Socked Warden of the Great Arch". Handover lines (each with her tic; ids `archwater.boss.gloop.ho.1-3`):
   "Blub! Oh my. Eight arms, dearie, and not one of them could catch you." / "Blub. When the arch light went out the
   tide stopped where it was. If I let go, the sea would rush back all at once and tip the Raft right over." / "Arm
   three agrees and arm five isn't sure, but... you light the arch, and I'll let the sea go gently. One arm at a time."
   Reward: the beacon tile `!` (20,39) becomes `[E] Kindle · Beacon` (S-095 flow; the overworld change is S-127b).
   `S.bosses['undertow_grotto:gloop'] = true`; `S.bestiary['archwater.gloop'].calmed += 1`.
5. **After** (creatures.md "after spot"): from the first time Pip leaves the realm after the handover, Gloop lives in
   the Moon Pool on Archwater: model at (150, −70) in the pool (only head and four arms above water), talk point on the
   west rim (138, −70) (`talkAt`), `[E] Talk · Old Gloop` "Eight socks, dearie. Well. Seven and a dish-cloth. Blub."
   (after `ss25.done`: "Eight socks, dearie. All eight. Arm eight is very pleased. Blub."). `[E] Wake · Old Gloop` at
   the same spot plays the rematch card "Old Gloop · Rematch" and loads r14 (S-045 flow), returning Pip to the rim.
6. **Rematch**: "Arms let go 0/8" with two stones lit from the start, a 1.6 s Tide-Bell window and the P2 table
   throughout; first rematch win 60 gems once
   (`S.rematches['undertow_grotto:gloop']`). **Plushie** `plush_gloop` (key `undertow_grotto:gloop`), 100 gems at
   Miso's once Gloop is calmed and the shop is bought out (S-045 rule): name "Old Gloop plushie", desc and got line
   from [../bible/side-content.md#plushies](../bible/side-content.md#plushies) (side-content owns plushies): "Eight tiny
   socks, all different. Granny Mochi knitted them. She insisted." / "An Old Gloop plushie! Eight socks, no cold
   arms~" (the key is systems.md's `undertow_grotto:gloop`, not side-content's `archwater:gloop`). **Dream**: data only
   `dream: { id: 'dream_gloop', twist: 'eightArms' }` (S-294 builds it).
7. **Bestiary**: card `card_boss_gloop` (384², ≤ 45 KB, realm `archwater`, card-beast prompt: "a big kindly purple
   octopus grandmother with sleepy gold eyes wearing eight mismatched hand-knitted socks on her arms, one a grey-blue
   dish-cloth, juggling three knitted sea urchins in a moonlit sea cave pool"); Hoot "Old Gloop is the only octopus I
   know who darns." · Clover "Watch which stone glows. That sock's arm lets go when you bonk it!"
8. **Portrait** `portrait_gloop` (realm `archwater`, ≤ 55 KB), cast.md prompt verbatim through the portrait pipeline.
9. **Music**: `warden_gloop` (S-119) during the bout; the calm layer of `undertow` at the handover.
10. **Balance test** (S-045 bots, [../bible/creatures.md#balance-targets](../bible/creatures.md#balance-targets)): a
    careful bot (reads the lit stone, rings it or waits for its sweep or urchin, hits only the lit arm in openings,
    dodges every drawn shape) wins ≥ 90 % of 50 seeded runs in **60-120 s** losing ≤ 3 hearts at each window (2.5, 2.2,
    1.9 s); a never-dodging bot wins ≤ 50 %; a **random-arm** bot (hits whichever arm is nearest) never finishes inside
    240 s (the puzzle is the fight). Pip's hearts in the test: 3 + the petals reachable by then (`maxHp` 8 HP, the S-072
    fixture).
11. Marker "Blub." is Gloop's reserved tic: add it to the charter's reserved-markers table (speaker `gloop`).

#### Assets

| Key | Build / generate | Fallback |
|---|---|---|
| `boss_gloop` | code-built (above); ART BRIEF from [../bible/cast.md#old-gloop](../bible/cast.md#old-gloop) (owner GLB ≤ 12,000 tris, nodes `arm1..arm8`, 3D prompt verbatim) and the creatures.md brief, pasted in the PR body; manifest `// boss_gloop: { src: 'assets/realms/archwater/models/boss_gloop.glb', height: 4.0 },` | code rig |
| `portrait_gloop`, `card_boss_gloop` | generated as above | name only; canvas blob card |
| sock textures | canvas-generated at runtime (stripes, dots, zigzag, hearts, stars, checks, plain, Fair Isle) | - |
| `int_tide_stone_sock` ×8 | code-built 1.4 m rounded stone `#7a8a96`, ≤ 120 tris, a carved-pattern decal per sock (generated: "<style line> A carved sock-pattern relief on a smooth sea stone, eight variants ... glowing softly in its colour", 8 × 256² webp ≤ 10 KB each, realm `archwater`, via `export_sprites.py`); ART BRIEF in [../bible/creatures.md#boss-gloop](../bible/creatures.md#boss-gloop) | the same canvas sock pattern drawn on the stone |

#### Acceptance criteria

- Gloop's def has `kind: 'puzzle'` and no HP; the bar reads "Arms let go n/8"; Mossmantle's `hp()` still returns 74 /
  82 / 90 with Gloop counted as a calmed Warden (unit test with fixtures).
- Only the lit stone's arm releases on a hit in an opening; any other arm tickles; ringing the lit stone opens it for
  2.5 / 2.2 / 1.9 s by Wardens calmed; from 4/8 two stones are lit; a dizzy restarts at 0/8 with a new order.
- Every move's telegraph appears ≥ 0.55 s before damage and matches its damage volume; never more than two threats
  live; every hit on Pip is exactly 1 HP (in-page move test via `G.debug.actors.boss('sweep' | 'ink' | 'juggle' |
  'double')`).
- A batted urchin flops the lit arm 2.0 s gold; a missed lit-arm sweep sticks 1.5 s; no sweep crosses a lit stone.
- Dizzy: Pip wakes at the grotto entrance `@` with puzzle state kept; Gloop naps at full HP.
- Handover lines play with the portrait; `S.bosses['undertow_grotto:gloop']`; the beacon tile becomes kindle-able.
- After leaving once, Gloop is in the Moon Pool with Talk and Wake; the rematch (two lit, 1.6 s window) pays 60 gems
  once.
- Balance test passes; content lint (lines ≤ 220, `Blub.` only for `gloop`, `~` only in Miso's got line).
- Invariants: keep hidden until whole, one save slot, budgets (arena ≤ 120 calls), tone, assets, IP, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (def schema, HP formula, balance suite as S-105 extended it: `grep -rln "mossmantle" tests/`).
- In-page boss suite (the one S-105 added): `G.debug.realm('undertow_grotto')`, warp to r14, each move by name,
  opening timings, `lit()` then ring that stone and hit its arm (bar 1/8), hit a wrong arm (bar unchanged, tickle line),
  `letGo` to 4/8 and assert two lit stones, dizzy reset (0/8), `G.debug.killBoss('undertow_grotto:gloop')` then leave and
  check the Moon Pool after spot and Wake.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

The kindle scene and the tide turning (S-127b); Gloop's Juggling (S-133a); Eight Socks (S-132); the Dream (S-294).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the pool sits at the arena's north edge, not its centre (R9), so the eight stones sit on its rim tiles and long
sweeps toward the south wall could leave no safe ground; sweeps are radial from the nearest rim point and 9 m long,
leaving rows 34-41 (≥ 12 m) outside every stripe. A player who never reads the stones can stall: after 60 s without a
release, Clover's tip appears as a toast once ("Watch which stone glows!"). Rollback: revert; hidden.

<a id="s-127a"></a>

### S-127a · feat: Archwater's chain on the ground: Rafferty, three pearls and the Undertow door

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-120b, S-123, S-125, S-037b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.archwater.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-chain](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-chain), [../bible/story.md#ch9-11](../bible/story.md#ch9-11),
[../bible/systems.md#quests](../bible/systems.md#quests), [../bible/systems.md#hints](../bible/systems.md#hints)

#### Why

First half of INDEX S-127 (split, R30): the "people" part of the land loop becomes playable. Rafferty explains
the stuck tide, the player climbs the Watch Column, drains the Great Tidepool and threads the Kelp Maze for three
pearls, and the pearls open the Undertow door. Everything happens at low water, because the tide is stuck (R5, R28).

#### What

1. **Pearl item** `ITEMS.pearl = { name: 'Pearl', kind: 'delivery', icon: 'icons_items:pearl', stack: 3, desc: 'A
   round sea-pearl. The Undertow door has three sockets.' }`. Picking one up: `give:pearl`, `set:aw:pearl-<which>`,
   item-get beat line "You found a pearl! ({n}/3)".
2. **The Sky Pearl**: in S-118b's dark lamp cage on the Watch Column's top platform (−20, −60) h 54: `[E] Pick up · Sky
   Pearl`. In first person the pearl glints in the cage from the fifth landing up (a `glint` sprite, pulse 1.2 s).
3. **The Pool Pearl** (Great Tidepool (50, 130)): the three plugs placed in S-123 (`archwater:plugs`) become
   interactables `[E] Use · Basalt plug`: each rolls 2.5 m into its notch in 0.6 s (`sfx.landHeavy` thud) and stays
   (`gate:archwater:plug-1..3`). When all three are in, the pool drains in 3 s: the pool's own water disc (a local
   water mesh at y 0.2 inside the rim, separate from the realm water) lowers to −2.1; two post-decks turn active (the
   S-123 `attachPost` mechanism for realms, or the realm module's deck list): a floor disc r 6 at h −2.0 and a ramp
   1.6 m wide from the rim's south notch (50, 137) down to (50, 132) (slope 0.43); `gate:archwater:pool` is set for
   good (the pool stays drained). The pearl lies at the centre: `[E] Pick up · Pool Pearl`. Before draining: `[E]
   Look · Great Tidepool` "Something round and pale glows at the bottom. The water's much too deep to reach it."
4. **The Kelp Pearl**: at the Kelp Maze's centre clearing (190, 10), `[E] Pick up · Kelp Pearl`, sitting in an open
   clam shell. Two Sand Snappers spawn in the maze while Pip is inside it (`kelp` region spawns, low water only).
5. **The pearl door** (S-118b, (134, −64)): with fewer than three pearls `[E] Look · Pearl door` "Three round sockets,
   like three empty eyes. Pearl-sized. ({n}/3)"; with three: `[E] Place · Pearls` → `take:pearl*3`, the sockets glow
   `#bff4f0` one by one (0.3 s apart, `sfx.chimeUp`), the door rolls aside in 1.2 s (`sfx.door_open`), `set:aw:door`;
   the portal `archwater:grotto` becomes `[E] Enter · The Undertow Grotto`.
6. **Quest hints** (two parts each, systems.md#hints; Wenna's voice is applied by the hint UI): s0 "Nell can sail you
   anywhere on the chart." / "Use the mooring post on Nell's Jetty and pick Archwater on the chart." · s1 "Someone on
   the Raft knows why the sea stopped." / "Talk to Skipper Rafferty by the mast on the main raft." · s2 (the answer
   names the first missing pearl) "Three pearls: one high, one deep, one lost in the kelp." / "Climb the stair round
   the Watch Column to the lamp cage at the top." · "Push the three stone plugs on the Great Tidepool's rim into their
   notches, then walk down to the pearl." · "Walk into the Kelp Maze on the east flats at low water and find its
   middle." · s3 "The pearls fit a round door under the Great Arch." / "Take the stair down from the arch's west
   shoulder to the ledge, and place the pearls." · s4 "The grotto's water goes up and down." / "Hit the conch shells to
   change the water, and ride the rafts when it's high." · s5 "Watch which sock wiggles." / "Step out of the arm's
   stripe, then hit the stuck sock. Swing at the urchins to bat them back." · s6 "Old Gloop is waiting for the arch's
   light." / "Walk to the lamp on the south floor of the Moon Pool Hollow and press {interact}." Remove `draft: true`.
7. **Compass targets**: `pearls` targets the nearest missing pearl; `door` the pearl door; dungeon steps the portal.
8. **Flags registered**: `gate:archwater:plug-1..3`, `gate:archwater:pool` (family `gate:*`).
9. **Mote's trace** (one row, S-090b's runner; story.md#motes-traces): append to `placeIsleStories`
   (`new RNG(40231 * 73 + 7)`) the record `archwater:mote-trace` at `near((126, −58), 0, 6)` on the plateau beside the
   pearl door under the Great Arch's west leg: a `decal_scorch_spiral` and a `prop_scrap_lamp`. The note it shows is
   chosen by the beacon count, not by the isle.

#### Assets

`icons_items` cell `pearl` (kind `icon`, "a single round pale sea pearl with a soft pink sheen", 128² cell; repacked
with `tools/pack_atlas.py`); pearl model code-built (`sphere` r 0.12 `#f6f0e6`, emissive 0.2); open clam shell reused
from `sand_snapper`'s shell halves (static). Fallback: canvas glyph.

#### Acceptance criteria

- From a fresh Archwater visit (debug realm, quest started by `realm:archwater.visited`), the three pearls can be
  taken in any of the 6 orders; each sets its flag and adds one `pearl`; the door takes all three and sets `aw:door`.
- The plugs, the drained pool and the opened door stay so after a reload; nothing can be lost (pearls are only taken
  by the door).
- The objective line and compass follow the steps; each step has a two-part hint; `{interact}` resolves.
- The solver's Archwater critical path needs only `lantern` and `tide` (pacing rule 6); the pearls need nothing.
- Invariants: keep frozen realms (drain decks are realm-module decks, not recipe changes; prints untouched), hidden
  until whole, one save slot, tone, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (quest lint with hints, flag registry, solver run).
- In-page (the S-106 Pinewhistle chain suite, `grep -rln "mq-pinewhistle" js/debug/tests/`): realm Archwater, talk to
  Rafferty, warp to each pearl, use, wait 3 s at the pool, warp to the door, place; assert flags and the portal.

#### Out of scope

The kindle, the tide turning, unhiding and the chapter test (S-127b).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the drained-pool decks must not change prints: they are realm-module decks added at load, like S-117's tide
decks. Rollback: revert; hidden.

<a id="s-127c"></a>

### S-127c · feat: Floating the Raft: three winch points round the beached Raft, worked under Squirt Urchin fire

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-127a, S-121, S-091b · Size: M (about 380 lines) · Save: none · Flag:
`REALMS.archwater.hidden: true` · Bible: [../bible/story.md#set-pieces](../bible/story.md#set-pieces),
[../bible/story.md#ch9-11](../bible/story.md#ch9-11), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater),
[../bible/creatures.md#enemy-squirt-urchin](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-squirt-urchin)

Added in review (see [Index changes](#index-changes)); **filed between S-127a and S-127b** (INDEX §1.8), while
Archwater is still hidden.

#### Why

Part of **Archwater and the midpoint**. Archwater's main-path set piece (15-25 minutes, no fail): the Raft sits
crooked on the mud, and Rafferty is sure it will crack in two when the tide comes back. Pip works three winches round
the bay to haul it level onto log rollers while Squirt Urchins on the basalt stacks keep up a telegraphed spray. The
payoff comes later: when the tide turns at the beacon (S-127b), the whole Raft floats level instead of groaning.

#### What

1. **Data** (`mq-archwater`, step `pearls`): `before: [{ id: 'raft', until: 'flag:aw:raftLevel', minutes: 20, text:
   'qt.archwater.raft', short: 'qt.archwater.raft.short', target: 'w:archwater.winch-next', hint: ['ht.aw.raft.n',
   'ht.aw.raft.a'] }]`. Journal "Haul the Raft level before the tide comes back ({n}/3)." · compass "Work the winches"
   · nudge "Rafferty's ropes run to three winches round the bay." · answer "Turn each winch with {interact} until its
   flag goes up; keep moving when the urchins squirt."
2. **Rafferty's ask** (talk after `aw:step1`, priority 85, once): "(sings) Oh the Raft is on the mud, on the mud, on
   the... sorry. Habit. When the sea comes back she'll come up crooked and split her lashings. Three winches round the
   bay. Haul her level, would you? I'd do it, but I'm the singer." Sets `aw:raftAsked`.
3. **Three winches** (`placeIsleStories`, `new RNG(40231 * 73 + 7)`, appended rows `archwater:winch-1..3`, each at the
   nearest walkable land cell within 10 m of: north shore (−166, −18), east walk end (−128, 8), south shore (−170, 46)).
   Model `prop_raft_winch` (code-built: a 1.1 m timber drum on an A-frame, a crank handle, a rope `tube` running to the
   nearest raft deck's edge, a pennant flag on a 1.6 m pole that rises as it is wound). `[E] Use · Winch` starts a hold:
   Pip cranks while `{interact}` is held, 6 s of holding per winch (progress kept if he lets go; the pennant shows it).
   While cranking, Pip can still roll out of a stripe (rolling pauses the crank). Each winch done: the raft decks on
   that side lift 0.25 m and level (their `tilt` lerps to 0 over 2 s), a creak and a cheer from the nearest otter, and
   `set:aw:winch<k>`, `inc:aw:winches`.
4. **Squirt Urchins** (S-121's `squirt_urchin`, turret archetype; only during the set piece): three placed on basalt
   stacks, one 10-14 m from each winch (nearest walkable rock cell in a ring r 10-14 m around it, facing the winch),
   spawned when Pip first comes within 20 m of that winch and despawned (poofing harmlessly) when its winch is done.
   Their shipped numbers hold (aim 0.80 s with a drawn stripe, lock 0.30, reload 1.2): a squirt that lands costs half
   a heart. They can be poofed (4 HP) and respawn after 20 s while their winch is unfinished. Dizzy wakes Pip at the
   nearest finished winch (or the pier) with progress kept: **no fail**.
5. **All three** (scene `aw-raft-level`, 14 s, replayable): the camera crane over the bay; the whole Raft settles level
   on log rollers (new `log_roller` instances under raft decks 1-7); Rafferty: "(sings) She's level! She's LEVEL! When
   the tide comes she'll float like a duck. A big wooden duck. With cabins." `after: ['set:aw:raftLevel']`.
6. **Payoff hook for S-127b**: the tide turn's Raft float (S-127b's rhythm change) lifts the decks from the level
   rollers; without `aw:raftLevel` (impossible on the main path, but kept for debug saves) it floats as before.
7. **Chapter test** (S-127b's `ch-archwater` spec): after `talk rafferty`, `G.debug.aw.winchAll()` (real interaction,
   with the urchins active); expect `aw:raftLevel`, `aw:winches = 3`. **Debug:** `G.debug.aw.winchAll()`.
8. **Flags**: `aw:raftAsked`, `aw:winch1..3`, `aw:winches` (counter), `aw:raftLevel` (story).

#### Assets

- `prop_raft_winch` code-built (≤ 400 tris; palette timber `#8a6a4a`, rope `#d9c49a`, pennant `#7fd3d0`). **ART BRIEF**:
  silhouette an A-frame timber winch with a fat rope drum and a crank, 1.1 m, a tall pennant pole; materials painted
  wood, rope; ≤ 1,500 tris GLB; image prompt "[style preamble] a small wooden rope winch on an A-frame by a muddy shore,
  thick rope wound on a drum, a sea-glass green pennant on a pole, soft painterly storybook style, transparent
  background, 1024 × 1024"; 3D prompt "[3D preamble] a wooden A-frame rope winch with a crank and a pennant pole, 1.1
  m tall, hand-painted toon storybook prop, under 1,500 triangles, origin at the base centre".
- `log_roller`: a `cyl` r 0.25 × 6 m, bark `#7a5a3e`, instanced.

#### Acceptance criteria

- From `aw:step1`, the journal shows the raft text before the pearls; three winches, each 6 s of holding, level the
  Raft side by side; the scene plays once on the third.
- Each winch's urchin draws every squirt ≥ 0.55 s ahead; urchins exist only near unfinished winches.
- The bay view stays ≤ 200 main-pass calls with urchins and rollers (instanced).
- Invariants: *Hidden until whole*, *Frozen realms* (appended `storyMarks` only), *Tone* (no fail; enemies poof),
  *Budgets per view*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

```sh
bun test tests/quests.test.js tests/content.test.js tests/realm-archwater.test.js && bun test
free -h && node tests/inpage.mjs archwater
```

#### Out of scope

The Undertow's early rooms (S-127d); the beacon, the tide turn and unhide (S-127b).

#### Risk & rollback

Additive rows, one prop and a set-piece spawner. Rollback: revert; the pearls step shows its own text.

<a id="s-127d"></a>

### S-127d · feat: the Undertow's upper rooms open early and exit onto the Tidepool Shelf

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-127c, S-124 · Size: M (about 300 lines) · Save: none · Flag:
`REALMS.archwater.hidden: true` · Bible: [../bible/story.md#loop-variations](../bible/story.md#loop-variations),
[../bible/dungeons.md#d3](../bible/dungeons.md#d3),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater)

Added in review (see [Index changes](#index-changes)); **filed between S-127c and S-127b**.

#### Why

Part of **Archwater and the midpoint**. Six land loops in a row had one shape (people → dungeon → Warden). Archwater
varies it: the dungeon starts during the people steps. A small wicket in the pearl door lets Pip into the Undertow's
first four caves early; the fourth opens onto the Tidepool Shelf, which becomes the main way to the Pool Pearl. The
player meets the tide-room mechanic before the dungeon proper, and the Shelf (which the old template left optional)
is on the path.

#### What

1. **The wicket** (S-118b's pearl door (134, −64)): from `aw:raftLevel`, a 0.9 m wicket in the door's lower left
   shows `[E] Open · Wicket` (before: `[E] Look · Pearl door` as S-127a). Opening it (0.6 s) adds portal
   `archwater:wicket` → `undertow_grotto` spawn `entry` (the same `X` tile at (0, 4)); the big door stays shut until
   three pearls (S-127a's rule unchanged).
2. **Map edit** (`js/realms/dungeons/undertow.js` and **dungeons.md#d3** in the same PR, so S-049's
   map test keeps them equal): row 4's east wall tile (40, 4) becomes `X` with exit `shelf`. The door at tile (35, 8)
   (r4 to the room below) gets `seal: 'flag:aw:door'`: before the three pearls it shows `[E] Look · Sealed door`
   "Sealed with pearl-coloured wax. The big door must open first." Nothing else in r1-r4 changes; basin A and its
   two conches (`r2:o1`, `r3:o1`) are the early puzzle. The solver gains a start state "wicket": exits `X (0,4)` and
   `X (40,4)` reachable, every r1-r4 chest reachable, no route past (35, 8), no soft-lock.
3. **The Shelf Mouth** (Archwater post-pass `placeIsleStories`, appended row `archwater:shelf-mouth`): a sea-cave
   mouth prop (reuse S-118b's grotto-mouth arch scaled 0.6) at the nearest walkable cell within 10 m of (62, 118) on
   the Tidepool Shelf, facing the Great Tidepool (50, 130); spawn `shelf` 1.5 m in front; portal back in to
   `undertow_grotto` spawn `shelf` (the new `X`). Toast on first exit: "✿ The Tidepool Shelf".
4. **Main path** (data, `mq-archwater` step `pearls`): the step's hint answers and compass order become Sky Pearl →
   the wicket → the four caves → the Shelf Mouth → the Pool Pearl → the Kelp Pearl. The hint answer for the Pool Pearl
   is "Go through the wicket in the pearl door, through the first caves, and out onto the Tidepool Shelf; push the three
   plugs on the Great Tidepool's rim." The overland way to the Shelf stays open (nothing is forced); the guidance leads
   through the caves.
5. **Dungeon state**: puzzle state in r1-r4 is saved in `S.dungeons.undertow_grotto` as usual, so entering by the big
   door later finds basin A as the player left it.
6. **Chapter test**: S-127b's `ch-archwater` spec takes the wicket → caves → Shelf route
   (`G.debug.dungeon.solveRooms('undertow_grotto', ['r1','r2','r3','r4'])`, exit `shelf`).

#### Assets

None new (reused arch and door).

#### Acceptance criteria

- After the Raft is level, the wicket opens r1-r4 only; tile (35, 8) is sealed until `aw:door`; r4's new exit leads to
  the Shelf Mouth and back.
- `bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes (repo map equals dungeons.md); the solver passes the "wicket" start
  state and the full dungeon from the big door.
- Invariants: *Hidden until whole*, *Frozen realms*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

```sh
bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/solver.test.js tests/quests.test.js && bun test
free -h && node tests/inpage.mjs archwater
```

#### Out of scope

The rest of the dungeon (S-124, S-125), the beacon (S-127b).

#### Risk & rollback

One map tile, a seal and a portal. Rollback: revert; the dungeon opens only by the big door again.

<a id="s-127b"></a>

### S-127b · feat: Archwater's beacon: the tide turns; Archwater opens

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-127a, S-127c, S-127d, S-126, S-095, S-030, S-119 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: unhides `archwater` · Bible:
[../bible/story.md#kindle](../bible/story.md#kindle), [../bible/story.md#ch9-11](../bible/story.md#ch9-11),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-beacon](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-beacon), [../bible/systems.md#beacons](../bible/systems.md#beacons),
[../bible/atlas-home.md#spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#spots), [../bible/atlas-home.md#sky](../bible/atlas-home.md#sky),
[../bible/systems.md#test-harness](../bible/systems.md#test-harness)

#### Why

The isle's payoff (pacing rule 4: within 10 s and for good): kindling the arch lamp turns the tide; the Raft floats, the
Spouter spouts, the crown gains a lantern, and Archwater opens on the chart. Roadmap: INDEX S-127 (M15); vision
item **Archwater and the midpoint**.

#### What

1. **Kindle scene** `kindle-archwater` (30 s, `replay: true`, in `js/content/scenes/archwater.js`, the story.md
   template with S-106's conversion: `grep -rn "kindle-pinewhistle" js/content/`): pov kindle at the r14 beacon tile
   (`fx.kindle`, `sfx.kindle`, `light beacon on`), `flag { 'beacon:archwater': 'next' }`, then a 0.35 s fade and Pip
   is placed at Archwater spawn `grotto` (the kindle exit, as S-106 did for the pine); `shot crane from
   w:landmark:arch at w:moonPool dur 3.0`; `emit rhythm.archwater`; `wait 2.5`; Gloop as a scene actor in the Moon Pool;
   `say gloop archwater.kindle.1` "Eight arms, nine days. I can let go now? ...Oh, that's lovely. Oh, my elbows." ·
   `say gloop archwater.kindle.2` "The Raft's afloat. Rafferty will sing about it. I'm so sorry in advance." ·
   `sfx.crown` · the crown card and toast "✿ A lantern lit in the Great Tree's crown! ({n}/8)". `after:
   ['quest:mq-archwater.done', 'set:visit:archwater']`.
2. **`rhythm.archwater`** (the isle's change, within 10 s of the flag, for good; `js/realms/archwater.js`):
   t 0 the keystone `kit_beacon` lights and a soft beam (additive cylinder 0.8 m, `#bff4f0`, 0.25 alpha) falls onto the
   Moon Pool (1 s); t 0-8 s `tideTween(W, 'high', 8)` (R20) with `set:tide:archwater=1` at the end; raft decks lift
   from −0.2 to 0.6 and level (tilt 4° → 0°) with the water; t 6 s the first Spouter plume (S-118b's spout plays
   every 8 s from now on); kelp sways (`uWaterY`); gulls join the ambience (`dim` multipliers off); music layer 1
   (`G.audio.layer(1)`: the harp enters); the Moon Pool glows `#bff4f0` at night; positional sounds: the Spouter's
   whoosh (audible 200 m) and a low hum from the lit arch (60 Hz-ish `drone`, 40 m). On realm entry with the flag set,
   the realm builds in this state directly (no tween).
3. **Home**: the crown lantern (S-095 counts it), horizon card `b`'s night window on (`sky.windowsOn { isle:
   'archwater' }`, the hook S-106 used), the distant `beaconLit` bell played at Archwater's bearing (115°) on the next
   home arrival; **plaza visitor** (R26) Skipper Rafferty at `home:spot-visit-archwater` (bearing 120°, r 12-15),
   08:00-20:00, from the next home arrival, idle lines rotating by day index: "(sings) Ohhh the tide comes in and the
   tide goes out... Your Tree's a fine audience, matey. Very still." · "Came to see where you live, matey! Very green.
   Not enough boats." · "Every time the sea comes in I think of you, matey. And then I sing. Sorry, everyone."
4. **Count beats**: after the scene, the beat for the new `beacons` value runs: `beacons==1` → S-106's beacon-1 beat
   (Granny Wick's Lamp Log 2 and Hoot's star count) must also fire when Archwater is the first isle; `beacons==2` →
   II.M (S-128 adds it); `beacons==3` → S-148b.
5. **Unhide**: `REALMS.archwater.hidden = false`; the chart lists Archwater (open `flag:ch8`) with its sailing card
   `load_archwater`; `mq-archwater.start` becomes `flag:ch8` (so the journal lists all three isles after ch8); the
   chapter banner on first arrival: "Chapter {9 + number of isles visited before}" / "Archwater" (S-106's banner
   helper). The Completion page shows the Archwater group.
6. **Chapter test** `ch-archwater` (spec next to S-106's, `grep -rln "ch-pinewhistle" tests/`): start fixture
   `tests/fixtures/saves/ch-archwater-start.json` = `ch-pinewhistle`'s end fixture (Archwater second in the canonical
   chain); steps: `sail archwater` · `talk rafferty` · `scene.skip` · `warpTo archwater:cage` · `use pearl-sky` ·
   `warpTo archwater:plugs` · `use plug-1` · `use plug-2` · `use plug-3` · `wait 3` · `use pearl-pool` · `warpTo
   archwater:kelp-centre` · `use pearl-kelp` · `warpTo archwater:pearl-door` · `use pearl-door` · `use grotto` ·
   `give tide_bell` · `killBoss undertow_grotto:gloop` · `warpTo undertow_grotto:beacon` · `use kindle` · `scene.skip`.
   Expect: `flags { 'beacon:archwater': 2, 'tide:archwater': 1, 'aw:door': true, 'visit:archwater': true }`, `items
   { tide_bell: 1 }`, `quests { 'mq-archwater': 'done' }`, `lanterns: 2`. Write the end fixture
   `tests/fixtures/chapters/ch-archwater.json`; add the CI matrix job `chapter:ch-archwater`. (S-128 adds `moteNamed`
   to this expectation.)

#### Assets

None new (the beam is an additive cylinder; the spout card and plume are S-118b / S-119's).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Kindling sets `beacon:archwater` to the order number, and within 10 s on Archwater: the lamp is lit, the water is at
  0 (high), the Raft is level, a plume has spouted, the harp layer plays; all stay on reload.
- The crown lantern count rises by one; horizon card b's window glows at night on home; Rafferty stands at the plaza
  spot 08:00-20:00 with his three lines.
- With Archwater kindled first (fixture `ch8` end + Archwater), the beacon-1 beat still plays (Wick's reading waits
  for the next Duskwick visit, as S-106 built it).
- `REALMS.archwater.hidden === false`; the chart sails there; the chapter banner shows once.
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch-archwater` passes in CI; the chain check passes.
- Invariants: keep frozen home island (the visitor spot is S-068's `placeSpots` entry; no new pass), frozen realms
  (unhiding freezes Archwater's prints from now on), one save slot, budgets, tone, IP, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (registry, quest lint, chain check).
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch-archwater` (and the existing `ch-pinewhistle`, `ch8`).
- In-page: the kindle rhythm timing (water y at 0, 4 and 8 s; first plume by 6.5 s); home visitor at 12:00 and absent
  at 21:00.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`, `bun run test:smoke`, `bun run test:playthrough`.

#### Out of scope

II.M (S-128); Hollows (S-129); Archwater's side stories (M16).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: unhiding freezes the realm: any later Archwater change must be a post-pass. Rollback: revert sets `hidden` back;
saves with `beacon:archwater` keep the flag harmlessly (the realm stays reachable only by debug).

<a id="s-128"></a>

### S-128 · feat: II.M: the Lamp Thief lands on the second beacon and names himself Mote

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-127b, S-106, S-090, S-030, S-033a · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/story.md#II.M](../bible/story.md#II.M), [../bible/story.md#act-II-reactions](../bible/story.md#act-II-reactions),
[../bible/cast.md#mote](../bible/cast.md#mote), [../bible/atlas-home.md#sky](../bible/atlas-home.md#sky),
[../bible/charter.md#tone-mote](../bible/charter.md#tone-mote), [../bible/lore.md#twists](../bible/lore.md#twists)

#### Why

The midpoint and twist T4: on whichever isle is relit second, the Lamp Thief lands on the beacon, says the Tree is his
sister and gives his name. From here the village talks about him and his light drifts on the sky isle. It is keyed to
the count (`beacons == 2`), never to an isle (CANON §12 rule 7). Roadmap: INDEX S-128 (M15).

#### What

1. **Scene** `iim-mote-named` (80 s, `replay: true`, title "The Lamp Thief Has a Name"; data in
   `js/content/scenes/act2.js`, text in `js/text/en/act2.js` ids `act2.scene.iim.01..07`), trigger `{ on: 'count',
   what: 'beacons', n: 2 }` queued after the kindle scene ends; `when: ['calm']`; it plays on that isle at its beacon
   (`w:beacon`, the overworld anchor S-095 gives every isle: the pine's crown, the arch's keystone, the granary
   cupola). Steps (story.md table converted by its rule 9):
   - `emit sfx.mote`; `emit spawn { actor: 'mote', model: 'npc_mote', at: 'w:beacon.rim' }` (S-090's starkin rig,
     state `cloaked`); the beacon's light leans toward him (`emit fx.lightLean { at: 'w:beacon', to: 'actor:mote' }`,
     a 1.5 s bend of the beam card) and he pushes it away with both hands (`pose mote anim: 'push'`).
   - `shot two at actor:mote from pip`; say `The Lamp Thief` 01 "You. Green hood. You keep *lighting* them." (his glow
     pulses on every word: `emit fx.pulse` per word, 0.15 s) · 02 "Do you know how long it took me to gather that one?
     Do you know how far up I live?" · 03 "...Fine. Fine. My name is Mote. She's my sister. Your Tree." (this box's
     frame label switches from "The Lamp Thief" to "Mote": the say step carries `label: 'mote'`).
   - `shot look at sky:skyIsle dur 1.2`; say `mote` 04 "She fell. I came to bring her home. I have been stuck on a rock
     in the sky for a hundred years, counting lights."
   - `shot two at actor:mote`; `emit face { who: 'mote', state: 'open' }` (he lifts the hood: two dot eyes, a small
     worried mouth); say 05 "I'm not stealing. I'm *borrowing*. Like your guardians did." · 06 "She's burning herself
     out down there, and none of you can see it. That bloom was the brightest thing in a hundred years. Bright things
     burn." · 07 "Keep your light. I'll find another. ...I always do."
   - `pose mote anim: 'rise'` (straight up 12 m in 1.5 s); `emit sky.streak { from: 'w:beacon', to: 'sky:skyIsle' }`;
     `emit despawn { actor: 'mote' }`; `sfx.mote` fades over 2 s.
   - `after: ['set:moteNamed', 'journal:mote']`.
   The `*italics*` markers render as italic in the dialogue box (the shipped `ui.say` markup, or strip them if it has
   none). Mote is built `hittable: false`, `lockable: false` (charter decision 21); Pip's attack input is locked for
   the whole scene anyway.
2. **Journal**: the People entry `thief` (from S-090, "the Lamp Thief") is renamed **Mote** (`journal:mote` effect sets
   the entry's name id to `people.mote.name`), with Hoot's line "Mote. A small star who borrows lights. Says the Great
   Tree is his sister. I have underlined this twice." Frame labels after `moteNamed`: Mote's dialogue frame always
   reads "Mote". Spoiler rule: the name "Mote" appears in no text gated before `moteNamed` (content lint check: every
   text containing "Mote" has a condition implying `flag:moteNamed`, charter decision 6).
3. **Reactions** (`js/content/dialogue/home.js` and `duskwick.js`, priority 50, `when: ['flag:moteNamed',
   '!beacons>=3']`, one new line each, verbatim from story.md's IIM column): Shellby "...Mote. Oh, dear." · Ribbit "A
   sky boy taking our lights? Not on my watch. I don't have a watch. I'll get one." · Miso "A little star, stealing
   lamps... Hmph. Even I give receipts~" · Clover "A star with a name? That's the best thing I've ever heard. And also
   the worst?" · Hoot "Mote. A Starkin, then. Fascinating. Distressing. Mostly fascinating. I'll put it in the Field
   Notes." · Digby "A brother. Huh. Never could see family resemblances. Can hear 'em, though." · Nell "A star that
   borrows lights. I've known sailors like that. Borrow a rope, never give it back." · Granny Wick "Mote. So it is him.
   I was twelve the night he came down the Ladder. He was the brightest thing I ever saw."
4. **Mote's light on the sky isle** (R14, atlas-home#sky): with `moteNamed` and not `mainEnding`, at night (21:00-
   04:00), on nights where `(dayIndex * 7 + 3) % 3 !== 0` (two nights in three), a 6 m additive glow (the
   `horizon_window` sprite tinted `#fff0b0`) drifts along three waypoints on the sky isle card's rim at 0.03
   sprite-widths per second; drawn in the sky sprite buffer (no extra draw call) in every realm that draws the sky isle.
   **S-011b already draws this light** (one night in three, `dayIndex % 3 === 0`, from the first night, via
   `moteLightOn` in `js/sky-home.js`): this step changes only the frequency rule after `moteNamed` (the branch S-011b
   leaves false) and extends it to every realm's sky; it does not add a second quad.
5. **Tests both orders** (the scene names no isle):
   - `iim-pine-arch`: start = `ch-pinewhistle` end; play Archwater to the kindle (as `ch-archwater`), then
     `scene.skip` ×2; expect `moteNamed`, `scene:iim-mote-named`, journal entry `mote`.
   - `iim-arch-pine`: start = a new fixture `ch8` end + Archwater done first (`beacon:archwater: 1`); play Pinewhistle
     to the kindle; expect the same, and that the scene played on Pinewhistle at the pine's crown anchor.
   - Update `ch-archwater`'s expectation with `moteNamed: true`.
   - In-page: during the scene, a scripted swing at Mote does nothing (`hittable: false`), the lock-on skips him.

#### Assets

None new: Mote's rig, portrait sheet and states come from S-090 (`grep -rn "npc_mote" js/`). If S-090 built no `push`
or `rise` pose, add them as transform animations (arms forward 0.4 s; body up 12 m over 1.5 s with the glow trail).

#### Acceptance criteria

- With `beacons` going from 1 to 2 on any isle, the scene plays once at that isle's beacon after the kindle scene, sets
  `moteNamed`, and never plays again except from the Lore tab (replay runs with flags off).
- Skipping at any point leaves the same save as watching (`moteNamed`, `scene:iim-mote-named`, journal renamed).
- All eight reaction lines show as top lines after `moteNamed` and are replaced at `beacons >= 3`.
- The sky isle shows Mote's light on two nights in three after `moteNamed` (unit test of the day rule; in-page check
  at 22:00 on day indices 0, 1, 2).
- `iim-pine-arch` and `iim-arch-pine` pass in CI; `ch-archwater` passes with the new expectation.
- Content lint: "Mote" only behind `moteNamed`; lines ≤ 220.
- Invariants: keep one save slot, tone (Mote never hit, no fight, no attack prompt), budgets (no extra draw call for
  the light), IP, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (scene lint: skip-safety per S-034, spoiler lint, sky day rule).
- `node tests/chapter.mjs iim-pine-arch`, `node tests/chapter.mjs iim-arch-pine`, `node tests/chapter.mjs ch-archwater`.
- In-page scene check (the scenes suite from S-034): play `iim-mote-named` on Archwater via `G.debug.scene.play`,
  swing at Mote, skip mid-way, assert flags.

#### Out of scope

The Catchers' plaza evenings after `moteNamed` (S-170); the third beacon (S-148b); Mote's later states (Acts III-IV).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: if Threemills (S-148) is the second isle later, its beacon anchor must exist; S-148b's test covers the Pine →
Three order and S-150 all six. Rollback: revert; `moteNamed` stays a harmless flag in saves.

<a id="s-129"></a>

### S-129 · feat: Archwater's three Hollows

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-123, S-073, S-107 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none (doors live in a shipped realm from S-127b;
if this lands first, the Hollows stay sealed by the `Look` line until the realm unhides) · Bible:
[../bible/dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [../bible/dungeons.md#h08](../bible/dungeons.md#h08),
[../bible/dungeons.md#h09](../bible/dungeons.md#h09), [../bible/dungeons.md#h10](../bible/dungeons.md#h10)

#### Why

Three one-room puzzles on Archwater: a tide Hollow, a reflection puzzle and a block puzzle; two heart petals and a gem
bag toward the 24 petals and 8 bags of CANON §10.3. Roadmap: INDEX S-129 (M15).

#### What

1. **Realms** `hollow_archwater_1..3` (kind `hollow`, grid 48 m, parent `archwater`), files
   `js/realms/hollows/hollow_archwater_<n>.js`, maps pasted verbatim from dungeons.md (they are checked by
   `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js`). Doors (S-123's `kit_hollow_door`s) become portals `[E] Enter · Spiral door` (systems#portal-table): door at
   (90, −120) → `hollow_archwater_1`, (−80, 20) → `_2`, (0, 120) → `_3`; return spawns 1.5 m out. Toast on entry: the
   Hollow's name. Solved: the lamp above the door lights `#ffd27a` and the map shows a gold spiral.
2. **H08 The Rockpool Hollow** (Tide Bell, petal): one basin covering every `,` and `;` tile, starts `low`; the tide
   stone `Q` at (1,1) uses S-124's tide entities and S-125's room stone (basin flip, 1.5 s, never strand). Pip rings
   from (1,2); at high the rafts (2,1)-(6,1) and (2,2) reach the raft ring round the petal `+`
   (5,2). Hint "The petal floats on a raft ring." / "Ring the tide stone in the corner for high water and walk the
   rafts to the petal."
3. **H09 The Mirror Pool Hollow** (no tool, petal): four plates `p` (2,4), (8,4), (2,8), (8,8) round a still pool `w`
   (4-6, 5-7). A new door/bridge condition `seq: ['r1:p4', 'r1:p1', 'r1:p2', 'r1:p3']` (plates numbered in reading
   order: p1 (2,4) NW, p2 (8,4) NE, p3 (2,8) SW, p4 (8,8) SE; the answer is SE, NW, NE, SW). A plate pressed out of
   order resets the count with a soft `bloop` (`T({f: 220, f1: 140, d: 0.25, v: 0.08})`); nothing else happens. The
   **reflection**: a decal under the pool surface shows four stars that light in order SE, NW, NE, SW (1 s each, 1 s
   dark, loop), with the explanation that they are painted in a ceiling hole only the pool shows (the ceiling there is
   drawn plain). In first person the pool is in view from `@`. Solved: the hidden bridge `=` (5,2)-(5,3) rises (1.0 s,
   `sfx.bridge_rise`) and stays. Hint "The pool remembers something the ceiling hides." / "Look into the pool: step on
   the plates south-east, north-west, north-east, south-west."
4. **H10 The Barnacle Block Hollow** (no tool, gem bag): blocks (3,5), (5,5), (7,5) with the barnacle skin
   (`int_block` tinted `#36414f` with barnacle dots `#e2c9a0`); hold plates `P` (2,7), (8,7); pillar `I` (5,7); lever
   `r` (1,9). Both plates held → the hidden bridge `=` rises. Solution: (3,5) W ×1, S ×2; (7,5) E ×1, S ×2; the middle
   block is spare. Hint "Only two plates." / "Push the outer blocks out one step and down two onto the plates; leave
   the middle one."
5. **Prizes**: H08 petal `collect.petal['hollow_archwater_1:p1']`, H09 petal `collect.petal['hollow_archwater_2:p1']` (item-get "You found a heart petal!
   Six petals make a whole heart." and the S-072 count line), H10 gem bag "+60 gems!". All three set
   `collect.hollow['hollow_archwater_1']` … `['hollow_archwater_3']` (the Hollow's realm id, the only key S-071's
   `normalize` keeps); puzzle state in `S.dungeons['hollow_archwater_<n>']` until solved, then dropped.
6. **Solver**: each Hollow passes the lock-and-key solver (prize reachable, no soft-lock: H10's lever covers the
   corner case) with its tool from `needsOnEntry` (H08: `tide`); the progression solver lists H08 under `tide`.

#### Assets

None new beyond skins: the reflection stars decal (canvas: four 0.4 m five-point stars `#fff3c4` on transparent,
animated by emissive per star) and the barnacle block tint. No images.

#### Acceptance criteria

- The three maps equal dungeons.md; each compiles, solves and has a prints fixture.
- H09: any wrong plate resets; the right order raises the bridge; reload keeps it.
- Prizes once each; Completion's Archwater Hollows row reads 3/3 and the hearts row counts the two petals.
- Realm suite: enter/leave each ×3, no leak, ≤ 60 draw calls in each.
- Invariants: keep frozen realms (the doors were already placed), one save slot, budgets, tone, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/hollows.test.js` (S-073's Hollow test extended with the three ids).
- In-page Hollow suite (S-073's): solve each via scripted moves; assert collect keys and the petal count.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

Threemills' Hollows (S-149).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: `seq` is a new condition; keep it pure in `js/dungeon-mod.js` with a unit test. Rollback: revert; doors fall back
to the Look line.

<a id="s-129b"></a>

### S-129b · feat: Archwater's Hollows get their second stage, and The Mirror Pool Hollow becomes a Deep Hollow

Milestone M15 · Depends on: S-129, S-090b · Size: M (200-500; mostly map data) · Save: none · Flag: the land's Hollow realms keep the land's hidden flag · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [../bible/dungeons.md#h09](../bible/dungeons.md#h09), [../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs](../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs), [../bible/story.md#motes-traces](../bible/story.md#motes-traces)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md §7 and R15, CANON §11.1 A3; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after S-129.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M15 · Archwater and the midpoint**. Every Hollow was a one-step room that played in 2-3 minutes and all 24 shared one
shape. Now every Hollow has two stages with the prize behind the second, and one Hollow per land is a three-room
**Deep Hollow** (15-20 min) that ends in a reading nook with a Lamp Log and a note from the Lamp Thief, so the
optional bucket of CANON §11 is real play and each land has one hidden place worth remembering.

#### What
1. **The reliquary** (if S-093b has not added it yet, this step does; later steps reuse it): entity `reliquary: { when:
   [...] }` on a Hollow's prize tile; a code-built cage of four curved roots `#6f5a44` with gold buds `#ffe9a8`, 1.0 m,
   ≤ 120 tris; it opens in 1.0 s with `chime_up` when its condition holds; its state lives in
   `S.dungeons['<hollow>'].stage` until the prize is taken, then is dropped (the shipped Hollow rule). The solver proves
   stage 1, then stage 2, then the prize.
2. **Stage 2 for H08 The Rockpool Hollow, H10 The Barnacle Block Hollow** (copy each Hollow's **Stage 2** line from [dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows)):
   - **H08 The Rockpool Hollow** (`hollow_archwater_1`): a crystal switch `s` on the far tide-floor corner (9,1), reachable only at low water; hit it first, then ring for high and ride the rafts; condition `sw`.
   - **H10 The Barnacle Block Hollow** (`hollow_archwater_3`): two crystal switches `s` (1,1) and (9,1) hit within 2.0 s of each other (a ticking ring shows the window); condition `sw:both`.
3. **The Deep Hollow H09 The Mirror Pool Hollow** (`hollow_archwater_2`): replace its one-room data with the 31 × 11 composite map
   from [dungeons.md#h09](../bible/dungeons.md#h09) (copy, never paraphrase; realm grid 78 m). Room 1 is the old
   puzzle (stage 1) with a door (10,1) where its prize stood. **Room 2 The Moon Basin**: (its own tide, starts high) ring tide stone (11,3) for low water; push block (12,5) east ×1 onto the dry tide floor (13,5); ring again for high: the block is a stepping stone onto the raft band (cols 14-16) and the dry east strip; exit door (20,9). **Room 3 The Reading
   Nook**: the prize on its pedestal (25,1); **Lamp Log 9** on a page pinned to stone (23,3) (`Read · Lamp Log`;
