   it moves here from its atlas slot in Archwater: edit that land's finds data so the slot is skipped, update the
   atlas row and run `node tools/realm-stats.mjs`; land and game totals unchanged); a scrap jam-jar lamp (`prop_scrap_lamp`
   from S-090b, or its fallback) holding a folded note (27,3), `[E] Read · Note`: "The pool shows the stars the ceiling hides. I came to look at them. Only to look. — M" (text id
   `lore.hollow.archwater`, ≤ 220 characters; it never names him, the charter spoiler rule; reading it adds the Lore
   entry "A note in a Hollow" and sets `trace:hollow:archwater`; if story.md's traces define this land's note, its
   text wins); and its own exit `X` (25,10) back to the spiral door.
4. **Hints** (Ask the Tree): one pair per stage-2 line (nudge = the condition's object, plain = the line), and for the
   Deep Hollow's room 2: "A stepping stone, if the water is up." / "At low water push the block into the channel, ring for high water and hop from the block onto the rafts.".
5. `STATS` is regenerated (`node tools/realm-stats.mjs`): same Hollow ids, same prizes, the log's new position.

#### Assets
- `int_reliquary` (code-built as above; ART BRIEF: a small cage of four curved roots with gold buds over a stone
  pedestal; 1.0 m; palette `#6f5a44` `#8a5a3c` `#ffe9a8`; toon bark; 120 / 500 tris; it opens like a flower; image prompt
  "<style line> a small cage of four curved tree roots with tiny gold buds over a mossy stone pedestal, cosy"; 3D prompt
  "stylised low-poly root cage over a stone pedestal, hand-painted, under 500 triangles, 1.0 m"). Fallback: four bent
  cylinders. The Deep Hollow reuses the land's Hollow kit and the scrap lamp; no new images.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the edited Hollow blocks (16 `+` and 8 `*` still counted across the Hollows);
  data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves each Hollow's two stages and the Deep Hollow's three rooms in order, with no soft-lock, using only
  the tools listed in the Hollow table.
- In page: a bot solves each stage-2 Hollow and the Deep Hollow; reading the note sets `trace:hollow:archwater`; the
  Lamp Log counts once; prizes and `collect.hollow` keys are unchanged.
- Content lint (≤ 220 characters, no spoiler name). Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (Hollow realms only; the land's
  overworld prints are untouched except the log slot, refreshed in this PR before the land is unhidden or, if it is
  already unhidden, by a finds-data change that the prints test lists), *One save slot*, *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/stats.test.js tests/content.test.js`.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `hollows_archwater`).

#### Out of scope

back.

<a id="s-131a"></a>

### S-131a · feat: side story 23 Clover's Map: chalk arrows on Archwater

Milestone M16 · Depends on: S-127b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/side-content.md#ss-23](../bible/side-content.md#ss-23), [../bible/side-content.md#rules](../bible/side-content.md#rules),
[../bible/side-content.md#data-shape](../bible/side-content.md#data-shape), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-poi](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-poi),
[../bible/cast.md#clover](../bible/cast.md#clover)

#### Why

Half of INDEX S-131 (split, R30). Clover wants to be an explorer: she comes to Archwater, runs ahead and leaves
chalk arrows, and is never lost at all. It gives the Hex Maze a reason and leaves a navigation aid behind for good.
Roadmap: M16 Archwater side stories and home households I; vision item **Archwater side stories and home households I**.

#### What

1. **Quest** `ss-23` in `js/content/quests/home.js` (it starts at home; shape of side-content §2): `n: 123, kind:
   'side', owner: 'clover', title: 'side.ss23.title'` ("Clover's Map"), `unlock: ['realm:archwater.visited']`, offered
   by a priority-40 dialogue entry on Clover (`quest:ss-23.start`), `minutes: 20`, reward `['gems:+35',
   'set:ss23.done']`, `mark: 'ss23.mark'`. Steps:

   | Step | Text (`side.ss23.sN`) | Done | On |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `ask` | "Clover wants to come exploring" | `quest:ss-23.active` (the start) | `TALK_END` |
   | `sail` | "Sail to Archwater with Clover" | `flag:ss23.landed` | `REALM` |
   | `arrows` | "Follow Clover's chalk arrows ({n}/12)" | `{ any: ['flag:ss23.arrows>=12', 'flag:ss23.found'] }` | `PUZZLE` |
   | `find` | "Find Clover" | `flag:ss23.found` | `TALK_END` |
   | `map` | "Bring Clover's map home" | `flag:ss23.given` | `TALK_END` |

2. **Sail with Clover**: while `ss-23` is at `sail`, the sailing card shows (canvas overlay) "Clover is aboard!"; on
   arrival at Archwater's jetty a Clover scene actor stands 2 m from Pip, says `side.ss23.clover.0` "Race you! ...No,
   don't race me. Follow the arrows! I'll leave arrows!" and runs up the pier and the North Walk at 4 m/s, despawning
   30 m away (`set:ss23.landed`). First person: she runs through the centre of view (actor spawned 2 m ahead-left).
3. **Twelve chalk arrows** (R31), placed by Archwater's `placeIsleStories` pass (`new RNG(40231 * 73 + 7)`, first
   entries of that pass), each the nearest basalt or path-edge cell within 4 m of its anchor, the arrow decal laid on
   the ground (or on a column face within 1 m) and pointing at the next anchor: (−176, −48), (−150, −42), (−130, −40),
   (−100, −46), (−80, −52), (−62, −72), (−60, −100), (−42, −106) (the Hex Maze's south entrance), (−44, −120) (inside),
   (−58, −130) (the west exit), (−100, −112), (−136, −96). **Clover's shortcut**: arrow 8 goes on the top of
   the nearest basalt column within 4 m of (−42, −106) that the Basalt Stairs reach on foot, chalked "SHORTCUT! —C.";
   arrow 9 goes on the top of a column within 4 m of (−44, −120) whose top is 5-7 m lower and that no walkable cell
   leads up to (a 1 m step-down on its south side lets Pip off it). So arrow 9 is reached only by a Leaf Glider glide
   from arrow 8 (ability `glide`, owned since Pinewhistle; 14 m at 6 m/s forward loses about 4.7 m). Arrows 10-12 are
   on the ground as before. The maze-floor route to Clover's Nook stays walkable without the glide (the `arrows` step
   also completes on `ss23.found`, as the table says). The progression solver lists arrow 9 under `glide`. `[E] Look · Chalk arrow`: "A chalk arrow, a bit wobbly.
   Underneath: 'THIS WAY (probably). —C.'" Each looked arrow sets `ss23.a<k>` and increments `ss23.arrows` (counter).
   Arrows are visible from 12 m (FP: a white chalk mark with a little star, 0.8 m, `card` decal, no draw call per arrow:
   one instanced decal mesh).
4. **Clover's Nook** (−150, −90): a 1.2 m basalt column stump (`archwater_stump`) she sits on, with a sandwich (`extra`
   box `#f4e2b0`) and a sketchbook; spot `archwater.clover_nook`, present while `ss-23` is at `arrows` or `find`.
   Talk: `side.ss23.clover.2` "Lost? I'm not lost. I know EXACTLY where I am. I'm here! I just don't know where here is
   yet. It's on the map now." → `give:clover_map_1` (key item "Clover's Map", desc "A wobbly map of Archwater. The sea
   is wobbly too, she says."), `set:ss23.found`. Then she walks to the jetty and "goes home with Nell" (despawn).
5. **Home**: Clover at her LOCKED spot (0.7, 56.0): `[E] Give · Map` → `take:clover_map_1`, `side.ss23.clover.3` "My
   first map! It's a bit wonky. The sea's wonky, though. I'm going to draw every isle. Every single one." →
   `set:ss23.given`, reward.
6. **Ask line** `side.ss23.clover.1` "Take me with you? I'll make a map! A proper one! With a compass rose! I'll draw the
   rose first."
7. **World mark** (within 10 s, for good): from `ss23.done` Clover holds her rolled map at her spot (an `extra` paper
   roll in her left hand, cream `#f4efe2`, 0.3 m); the 12 arrows stay; the map is pinned above her windowsill when
   S-156 builds her room (note in S-156's hand-off list). Journal: open "Clover has gone exploring. Alone. With chalk.
   I trust the chalk." / done "Clover made a map. It is better than mine. I have decided this is because she is younger."
8. Flags registered: `ss23.*` family (`landed`, `a1..a12`, `arrows` counter, `found`, `given`, `done`).
9. Clover's look is LOCKED (cream bunny, flower crown, pink scarf, satchel): the actor reuses `npcDef('clover')`.

#### Assets

`ss23_chalk_arrow`: canvas-drawn decal (white `#f6f3ea` chalk arrow with a five-point star at its tail, rough edges by
noise), 256² canvas texture, no image file. The sandwich and map roll are primitives. No brief needed.

#### Acceptance criteria

- `ss-23` is offered by Clover only after `realm:archwater.visited`; it can be done in one visit; Clover is never shown
  in peril (no enemy spawns within 12 m of Clover's Nook while she is there: a temporary `max 0` zone r 12).
- The 12 arrows are in order along the route, each pointing at the next; following them without Looking still ends
  the `arrows` step on talking to Clover.
- Giving the map pays 35 gems once and the map roll shows in her hand within 10 s, for good.
- Content lint (lines ≤ 220; Clover's LOCKED voice; no `~`/`!!`); quest lint (two-part hints: nudge = the step text,
  answer "Clover's arrows start where the Raft's North Walk meets the shore." / "Clover is sitting on a column west of
  the Hex Maze, by the Pool Steps.").
- Archwater prints untouched (a story-marks post-pass); coverage unchanged or better.
- Invariants: frozen home island (no home pass), frozen realms (post-pass only), one save slot, tone (children never in
  peril), machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (quest lint, flag registry, the story-marks pass is deterministic and adds nothing else to Archwater:
  prints equal with and without it).
- In-page side-story suite (S-110's: `grep -rln "ss-19" js/debug/tests/`): start ss-23 at home, sail, walk the arrows
  by warps, talk to Clover, return, assert gems and the mark.

#### Out of scope

The Raft Choir and the cabins interior (S-131b).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: an arrow anchor in the Hex Maze could land in a wall gap; the pass's cell test uses the post-stump walk grid.
Rollback: revert; the `ss23.*` flags are inert.

<a id="s-131b"></a>

### S-131b · feat: side story 24 The Raft Choir, and the Raft cabins interior

Milestone M16 · Depends on: S-131a · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/side-content.md#ss-24](../bible/side-content.md#ss-24), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-interiors](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-interiors),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-music](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-music), [../bible/cast.md#skipper-rafferty](../bible/cast.md#skipper-rafferty)

#### Why

Second half of INDEX S-131 (R30). Rafferty has a choir and no song; the old shanty is carved on driftwood in the
tide pools, readable only at low tide, which gives the Tide Bell a reason to be rung on a restored isle. The Raft's
cabins open as an interior (R16). Roadmap: M16.

#### What

1. **Interior `raft_cabins`** (R23; kind `interior`, parent `archwater`, grid 48 m, home interior rules of
   [../bible/atlas-home.md#interiors](../bible/atlas-home.md#interiors) with an Archwater palette: ambient `#ffe2c0`,
   lamps `#f2b45a`; no enemies; card "The Raft cabins" / "Everything's a bit damp"). Map (2 m tiles):

   ```
   #w#####w####
   #.O..#.OOO.#
   #....D.....#
   #t...#..O..#
   ##D###.....#
   #....#.....#
   #.O..D..t..#
   #..@.#######
   ###X##
   ```

   Rooms: **the Net Shed** (entry, cols 0-5, rows 4-8: `@` (3,7), `X` (3,8), net rack `O` (2,6), the Lamplighter tide
   chart on the west wall: `[E] Look · Tide chart` "Tide times, a hundred years old, every one marked 'arch lamp
   lit'."); **Rafferty's cabin** (cols 0-5, rows 0-4: hammock `O` (2,1), lamp `t` (1,3), a framed drawing on the north
   wall: `[E] Look · A drawing` "A drawing of the Great Arch with its lamp lit, done in crayon. Signed 'R., aged
   six'."); **the Choir Loft** (cols 5-11, rows 0-7: benches `OOO` (7-9,1), the squeezebox `O` (8,3), lamp (8,6), shanty
   sheets on a stand: before `ss24.done` `[E] Look · Shanty sheets` "Song sheets. Every one is blank except for the
   title: 'A SONG (TO BE FOUND)'."; after, verse 5). Doorways `D` (5,2), (2,4), (5,6). Prints fixture written. The
   main raft door (−162, 2) becomes the portal `archwater:cabins` `[E] Enter · the Raft cabins` (always); return spawn
   1.5 m out on raft 1. Rafferty's schedule gains `[22,'archwater.raft_cabin','sleep']` (spot = the hammock).
2. **Quest** `ss-24` (`js/content/quests/archwater.js`, `n: 124`, owner `rafferty`, `unlock:
   ['flag:beacon:archwater']`, reward `['gems:+35', 'set:ss24.done']`, 20 min). Steps: `ask` "Rafferty's choir needs a
   song" (talk: `side.ss24.rafferty.1` "We've got the voices! Well, I've got A voice. It's loud. Loud counts. Find us a
   song, little 'un!" — keep "(sings)" out of this line; his tic is optional per line); `verses` "Find the five shanty
   verses at low tide ({n}/5)" (`flag:ss24.verses>=5`); `ring` "Ring the verses back to the choir ({n}/5)"
   (`flag:ss24.rung>=5`); `sing` "Hear the Raft Choir at dusk" (`flag:scene:ss24-choir`).
   **The bells** (side-content#ss-24): three ship's bells (code-built `archwater_bell`, ≤ 120 tris each, low `#5a6ad8`
   ribbon, mid `#f2c45a`, high `#ff8fb0`) hang from Rafferty's Mast at 1.4 m, 0.8 m apart; a Swing within 1.6 m rings
   one (`sfx.bell` at 392 / 523 / 659 Hz). Each board shows its phrase as three coloured notches and Field Notes copies
   it on read: v1 low-high-low, v2 mid-mid-high, v3 high-low-mid, v4 low-mid-mid, v5 high-high-low. A pure matcher
   `js/activities/bellphrase.js` (`feed(state, note) → { state, result: 'ok' | 'verse' | 'miss' }`) checks the next
   verse in order (`ss24.rung` + 1); a full phrase plays that verse (`say card side.ss24.verse.<n>`, 4 s) and
   increments `ss24.rung`; a wrong note plays a soft clonk, Rafferty `side.ss24.rafferty.nearly` "(sings) Nearly! From
   the top of that verse, matey!", and only that verse restarts. Unit tests: every phrase in order passes; a wrong
   note mid-phrase resets the phrase, never `ss24.rung`.
3. **Five driftwood boards** in tide pools on the Tidepool Shelf, by Archwater's `placeIsleStories` (appended after
   ss-23's arrows), anchors (20, 140), (50, 150), (−10, 160), (110, 140), (70, 160), each a 1.2 × 0.4 m carved plank
   lying in a pool basin whose floor is below high water. At low water (`tide:archwater` 0): `[E] Read · Driftwood
   verse` shows the verse in the letter frame and sets `ss24.v<n>` (+1 `ss24.verses`). At high water: `[E] Look ·
   Driftwood verse` "Under the water. Come back at low tide." (tide stone `tide-4` beside the Spouter is 30-60 m away).
   Verses verbatim from side-content (ids `side.ss24.verse.1..5`; "The Wide Blue Round").
4. **Scene** `ss24-choir` (25 s, replayable): `start: 'enter:archwater.raft'` with `when: ['quest:ss-24>=3',
   'flag:ss24.rung>=5', 'hour>=18', 'hour<20', 'calm']`: `shot wide at w:landmark:mast`; the seven otters move to
   raft 1 (`pose ... move`); `music { track: 'archwater', layer: 2 }` (the hummed choir layer: the `choir` voice of
   [../bible/art-audio.md#voices](../bible/art-audio.md#voices) singing the track's melody an octave down); `say card`
   verse 5 "So round and round the Wide Blue goes, from the morning to the night; and every isle's a lantern, and
   every lantern's light!"; `say rafferty side.ss24.rafferty.2` "AND EVERY LANTERN'S LIIIIGHT! ...Was I flat? I felt
   flat. I felt GLORIOUS."; `after: ['quest:ss-24.done']`.
5. **World mark** (within 10 s, for good): the five boards hang on Rafferty's Mast (five plank props at 2.0-4.0 m); the
   choir layer plays every dusk 18:00-20:00 on the Raft within 60 m (`archwater_raft` region music layer 2); the Choir
   Loft's sheets show verse 5; a squeezebox wheeze joins the Raft ambience.
6. Journal: open "Otters wish to sing. Otters can sing. One otter cannot. He is the captain." / done "The Raft sings
   every dusk. Rafferty is flat. Nobody minds. I hummed."
7. Hints: `verses` "Some words only show when the sea steps back." / "Ring the tide stone by the Spouter for low water,
   then read the driftwood in the tide pools on the Tidepool Shelf."; `sing` "Choirs sing at dusk." / "Be on the Raft
   between 18:00 and 20:00."

#### Assets

`archwater_driftboard` code-built (a 1.2 × 0.4 × 0.08 m plank `#b8a68a` with a canvas text texture of wavy carved lines,
≤ 40 tris). Raft cabin furniture from S-054's interior kit (`js/props-interior-home.js`) recoloured (`#8a6a48` logs,
`#3f8a88` cloth). No images.

#### Acceptance criteria

- `raft_cabins` compiles, has a prints fixture, enters and leaves ×3 with no leak, ≤ 60 draw calls.
- Each board reads only at low water; the step counts 5 distinct boards.
- The choir scene plays once on entering the Raft region between 18:00 and 20:00 with 5/5 verses, pays 35 gems, and
  the mark appears within 10 s and stays after reload; the choir layer is audible every dusk after.
- Content lint; quest lint.
- Invariants: frozen realms (story marks are a post-pass; the interior is a new realm with its own fixture), one save
  slot, tone, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (interior compile and prints, quest lint, story-marks pass determinism).
- In-page side-story suite: set `beacon:archwater`, ring to low with `G.debug.ring('archwater:tide-4')`, read the five
  boards by warp, `G.debug.tod(18.5)`, enter the Raft, skip the scene; assert flags, gems, the mast boards.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (the interior).

#### Out of scope

Eight Socks and the Moon Pool (S-132).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: a dusk-only step could feel missable; the hint names the hours and night content repeats every day. Rollback:
revert; the portal falls back to the S-120 note.

<a id="s-132"></a>

### S-132 · feat: side stories 25 Eight Socks and 26 The Moon Pool

Milestone M16 · Depends on: S-127b, S-126 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/side-content.md#ss-25](../bible/side-content.md#ss-25), [../bible/side-content.md#ss-26](../bible/side-content.md#ss-26),
[../bible/cast.md#old-gloop](../bible/cast.md#old-gloop), [../bible/cast.md#frostbun](../bible/cast.md#frostbun)

#### Why

Two Archwater stories that tie the isle to home and to the night: Gloop's eighth sock is on Frostbun's ear on the
Peaks, and the otters' moon swims in the Moon Pool at midnight once three stones are set right. Roadmap: INDEX
S-132 (M16).

#### What

1. **ss-25 Eight Socks** (`n: 125`, owner `gloop`, `unlock: ['boss:undertow_grotto:gloop']`, reward `['gems:+30',
   'set:ss25.done']`, 15 min). Steps:
   - `ask` "Old Gloop has only seven socks" · talk at the Moon Pool: `side.ss25.gloop.1` "Seven socks and a dish-cloth.
     It's the cold arm. Always the cold arm. Number eight. Orange stripes. Hand-knitted. Blub." (R22)
   - `clue` "Ask Skipper Rafferty about the sock" · `side.ss25.rafferty.1` "A sock? Orange stripes? A gull had it, matey.
     Flew north. Way north. Singing, I think. Might have been me singing."
   - `find` "Find the orange-striped sock" · from `quest:ss-25>=2` Frostbun wears the sock on **his** left ear (R22): a
     sock `tube` (0.35 m, orange stripes `#f2994a` / `#fff3e0` canvas texture) parented to the Frostbun model at the
     left ear's position (offset measured once from the `boss_frost` GLB's bounding box: top-left of the head,
     ±0.1 m), visible from 30 m. Talk to Frostbun (a priority-70 entry while `quest:ss-25.active`, at his post-bloom
     spot in his arena): `side.ss25.frostbun.1` "It was on a gull. The gull was cold. ...Now I'm cold. Fine. Take it.
     ...It smelled of sea." → `give:sock` (ITEMS `sock` from systems: "Gloop's eighth sock"), the ear sock disappears.
   - `return` "Return the sock to Old Gloop" · `[E] Give · Sock` → `take:sock`, `side.ss25.gloop.2` "Eight! Eight is
     the right number. Everything's the right number now. Even the sea feels warmer."
   - **Mark**: Gloop's arm 8 swaps the grey-blue dish-cloth for the orange-striped sock (model and boss-fight tells,
     R8) within 10 s, for good; Frostbun's ear stays bare until ss-12. Journal: open "An octopus has lost a sock. I have
     lost many socks. It is a universal story." / done "The sock was on a bun. The octopus is complete. I have checked
     my own drawers. Nothing."
2. **Nana Kelp** (R13): new look record `nana_kelp` (`otter` rig, 0.85 m, fur `#8a7a6a` with a silver-grey muzzle
   `#d8d4cc`, a teal knitted shawl `#3f8a88`, a walking stick `#9b7b56` carved like a fish, `acc: ['scarf', 'stick']`),
   spot `archwater.nana_rocker` on raft 10 (−160, 26) in a rocking chair (`O` prop), schedule `[[8,
   'archwater.nana_rocker','sit'],[21,'archwater.nana_rocker','doze']]`. Voice: slow, fond; tic "Mm, pup." (calls
   everyone "pup"; add to the reserved markers). Portrait `portrait_nana_kelp` (realm `archwater`, ≤ 55 KB): house
   preamble + "A tiny, very old otter grandmother with a silver-grey muzzle, a knitted teal shawl and a walking stick
   carved like a fish, twinkly eyes and a gentle knowing smile." + portrait suffix. Idle line: "Mm, pup. I've seen this
   tide come and go a thousand times. It's nice to see it come again."
3. **ss-26 The Moon Pool** (`n: 126`, owner `nana_kelp`, `unlock: ['ability:tide']`, reward `['gems:+35',
   'set:ss26.done']`, 20 min). Steps:
   - `ask` "Nana Kelp misses the moon in the pool" · `side.ss26.kelp.1` "When I was a pup, the moon came down to swim in
     that pool at midnight. Now the water's never the right height." · `side.ss26.kelp.2` "Three stones. My granny knew
     the order. I only remember it rhymed. Something about the middle being low."
   - `stones` "Set the three tide stones round the Moon Pool" · three **moon stones** (R25), `tide_stone` gates with
     `local` flags, appended to Archwater's `placeIsleStories`: A west `ss26.a` (137, −74), B north `ss26.b` (150, −84),
     C east `ss26.c` (162, −71), each the nearest cell within 3 m walkable at low water. Start A 0, B 1, C 0. `[T] Ring ·
     Tide stone` toggles that stone only. The Moon Pool's own water disc (drawn 0.02 m above the realm water where they
     meet) sits at the carved **moon line** (a pale ring `#e2c9a0` on the rim stones at h 0.3) plus `0.25 m × (number
     of stones set wrong) × (B wrong ? −1 : +1)`, tweened 1.5 s; it is exactly on the line only at A 1, B 0, C 1
     (`set:ss26.set` when it is, unset is never needed: the step is done once reached). The stones are reached at low
     water (the tide stone `tide-3` at the grotto stair top is 15 m away).
   - `midnight` "Watch the Moon Pool at midnight" · `when: ['hour>=23.5'] or ['hour<0.5']` (`{ any: [...] }`); `[E] Sit ·
     Pool's edge` on a stone seat at (137, −62) → scene `ss26-moon` (20 s, replayable): `shot look at w:moonPool`; the
     moon's reflection (the `archwater_card_moonpool` shimmer plus a 2 m moon disc `#f6f3d8`) slides to the pool's exact
     centre over 4 s; Marina, Spratt and Nana Kelp float on their backs in the pool (actors); if Gloop is calmed she
     hums (`sfx.hum` + `music layer`); `say nana_kelp side.ss26.kelp.3` "There she is. Hello, moon. ...Thank you,
     little one. Now go to bed. It's very late."; `after: ['quest:ss-26.done']`.
   - **Mark**: every night 23:00-01:00 the Moon Pool shows the moon's reflection at its centre with a ring of 12
     floating lily lights (instanced additive sprites, no point lights) and three otters floating. Journal: open "The
     otters claim the moon swims. The moon is in the sky. I have pointed this out. Nobody listened." / done "I watched.
     The moon swam. I have no further remarks."
4. Hints: ss-25 `find` "Gulls fly north from Archwater. What's north of home that's cold?" / "Frostbun is wearing it on
   his ear, up on the Peaks."; ss-26 `stones` "Something about the middle being low." / "Ring the west stone high, the
   north stone low and the east stone high."; `midnight` "Moons swim at midnight." / "Sit at the pool's edge between
   23:30 and 00:30."
5. Flags: `ss25.*`, `ss26.*` (`ss26.a`, `.b`, `.c` are `toggle` + `rewritable`), `met:nana_kelp`.

#### Assets

`portrait_nana_kelp` (generated). Code-built: the ear sock, the rocking chair (interior kit chair recoloured), the
moon stones (S-122a's stone with a crescent inlay instead of the wave), the lily lights. Nana Kelp's ART BRIEF for
batch 07 (S-137): 0.85 m, ≤ 3,500 tris, 3D "[3D preamble] a tiny very old otter grandmother with a silver-grey muzzle,
teal knitted shawl and a fish-carved walking stick, 0.85 m tall."

#### Acceptance criteria

- ss-25: the sock shows on Frostbun's left ear from step 2, from 30 m in both cameras; giving it back swaps Gloop's arm
  8 within 10 s and in her rematch tells; 30 gems once; Frostbun's LOCKED model and lines are otherwise untouched.
- ss-26: only A 1, B 0, C 1 puts the water on the moon line (unit test of all 8 combinations); the midnight sit works
  only 23:30-00:30; 35 gems once; the nightly mark shows 23:00-01:00 and never by day.
- Every text uses he/him for Frostbun (lint search for "her ear" / "her Summit" in ss-25 text fails the build).
- Content lint: `Mm, pup.` only for `nana_kelp`; `Blub.` only for `gloop`.
- Invariants: frozen home island (the ear sock is a model child, not a world placement; `world-prints.json`
  unchanged), frozen realms, one save slot, tone, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (moon-line truth table, quest lint, flag registry).
- In-page side-story suite: ss-25 end-to-end with warps across realms (Archwater → home Peaks → Archwater); ss-26 with
  `G.debug.give('tide_bell')`, ring the three stones, `G.debug.tod(23.6)`, sit, skip, assert.
- `bun run test:playthrough` (Act I untouched; Frostbun's ear sock appears only with the quest flag).

#### Out of scope

Gloop's Juggling (S-133a); Frostbun's own story (ss-12, S-239).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: parenting a mesh to the LOCKED Frostbun GLB must not change its transform; the sock is a sibling under the same
root with a fixed offset. Rollback: revert.

<a id="s-133a"></a>

### S-133a · feat: Gloop's Juggling and Lagoon Lights

Milestone M16 · Depends on: S-126, S-127b, S-088 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/side-content.md#act-juggling](../bible/side-content.md#act-juggling),
[../bible/side-content.md#act-lagoon-lights](../bible/side-content.md#act-lagoon-lights),
[../bible/side-content.md#activities](../bible/side-content.md#activities), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-poi](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-poi)

#### Why

Half of INDEX S-133 (R30): two repeatable activities, one skill toy (juggling with the boss's own tell idiom) and
one quiet night ritual on Duskwick's lagoon. Roadmap: M16.

#### What

1. **Gloop's Juggling** (`act-juggling`, quest `n: 207`, kind `activity`, owner `gloop`, open `boss:undertow_grotto:gloop`):
   - Post on the Moon Pool rim at (130, −56) (Archwater `placeIsleStories`, appended): `[E] Use · Gloop's Juggling`
     shows the best and the tiers, then Gloop's line `side.act.juggle.gloop.1` "Eight arms, one juggler. I need a
     second. Catch and swing back. Gently. They're knitted." and a 3-2-1 chime.
   - Pip plays inside a 6 m practice circle (a chalk ring decal) on the rim; Gloop in the pool tosses **knitted
     practice urchins** (soft, harmless, no damage ever): the tossing arm lifts and its sock glows 0.8 s before the toss
     (the boss tell idiom); each urchin flies 0.9 s in an arc to a point inside the circle within 3 m of Pip, with a
     cream (not coral) reticle; a Swing that meets it within 2 m sends it back to that arm: **one catch** (`sfx.pop`,
     counter top-centre). An urchin that lands is a **drop**; **3 drops** end the round.
   - Rate: one toss per 1.6 s, 5 % faster every 10 catches (`interval = 1.6 × 0.95^floor(catches/10)`); from 30 catches
     two arms may toss at once (never three; the second at least 0.4 s after the first).
   - Tiers (each once): **50 catches** in one round → a heart petal (`collect.petal['act-juggling']`, the S-072 petal
     beat); **75** → 80 gems; **100** → the sock trophy `give:nook_gift_sock` (gift item, kind `furnishing`; listed in Items under Keepsakes until S-302a puts it on the Nook's trophy stand) and Gloop wears a tiny
     knitted crown for one game day (`set:act.juggle.crownDay=<dayIndex>`). Save: `act.juggle.best` (counter), `act.juggle.t1..t3`.
   - Enemy spawns in Archwater are paused during a round; leaving the circle by 10 m or `[E]` at the post ends it with
     no result.
2. **Lagoon Lights** (`act-lagoon-lights`, `n: 209`, owner `flick`, open `flag:ch8`), Duskwick, nights only:
   - Flick at the float point on Wick Square's north rail (−8, 79) from 21:00 to 03:00: `[E] Talk · Flick` offers it
     with `side.act.lagoon.flick.1` "One for every lamp that ever lit the Wide Blue. We lost count, so it's twelve.
     Twelve is nice." (Flick's tic line on accept: "Can I light it? ...You light it. No, I'll light it.").
   - 12 **float marks** (painted rings on the deck edge over water), placed by Duskwick's `placeIsleStories`
     (`new RNG(40211 * 73 + 7)`, appended): the 12 deck-edge cells next to water nearest (−8, 79), ≥ 2.5 m apart.
     Flick hands Pip one paper lantern float at a time (held in the viewmodel's left hand, a 0.3 m paper lantern on a
     leaf, warm glow); at a lit mark `[E] Place · Lantern float`; the next mark lights.
   - All 12 placed → a 30 s viewing scene `lagoon-lights` (free look: the camera stays Pip's; the floats drift out over
     the lagoon at 0.3 m/s along a spread fan, the Wicklings hum: Duskwick music layer `hum`); repeatable every
     night (`ss`-style replay not needed: it is the activity).
   - Tier (first time only): a **postcard** of the lagoon lights (item `postcard_lagoon`, kind `collectible-view`;
     shown in the Lore tab, and pinned on the Nook's postcard board when that exists). No gems. Save `act.lagoon.done`.
   - Floats are one instanced mesh plus additive glow sprites (no point lights).

#### Assets

| Key | Build / generate | Fallback |
|---|---|---|
| `act_urchin_knit` | code-built knitted urchin: `sphere` r 0.18 with 14 soft `cone` nubs, canvas knit texture in the tossing arm's sock colour | - |
| `act_lagoon_float` | code-built paper lantern on a leaf (`lathe` lantern `#f4e6c6`, leaf `#7fae4b`), glow sprite `#ffcf7a` | - |
| `postcard_lagoon` | generated `card` 1024 × 640 → 512 × 320 webp ≤ 60 KB (core: it shows in the Lore tab anywhere): preamble + "a calm lagoon at night under a starry sky, a stilt village of little lamplit houses on the left, twelve small paper lanterns floating out across the dark water, their reflections glowing, painted like a postcard with a soft white border" | canvas: dark blue with 12 gold dots |

#### Acceptance criteria

- Juggling: a careful bot (swings when an urchin is within 2 m) reaches ≥ 100 catches in a seeded run; a do-nothing bot
  ends after 3 drops; urchins never damage Pip; rate formula unit-tested; tiers pay once each; the crown shows for one
  game day. The petal key `act-juggling` survives a save/load round trip (unit test through `normalize`).
- Lagoon Lights: only 21:00-03:00; 12 marks within the rail band; the scene plays after the 12th float; the postcard
  once; repeating it another night replays the floats with no reward.
- Both activities appear in the journal's Activities list with their tiers and in Completion's activities row (top tier
  counts).
- Invariants: frozen realms (post-pass marks only), one save slot, tone (no fail state, harmless urchins), budgets
  (floats ≤ 2 draw calls), assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (juggling rate and tier logic as a pure module `js/activities/juggling.js`; float-mark placement
  determinism; economy ledger still ≥ 105 %; a save test that `collect.petal['act-juggling'] = true` survives
  `normalize`, i.e. matches S-071's petal pattern `^([a-z0-9_]+:p\d{1,2}|act-[a-z-]+)$`).
- In-page activity suite (S-112's Dash suite or a new `activities` suite: `grep -rln "dash" js/debug/tests/`): run the
  juggling bot to 100; place 12 floats at 22:00 with warps.

#### Out of scope

Dash courses and Rook's race (S-133b).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: swing timing in first person: the urchin's last 0.3 s glints and the reticle reaches into the lower view (the
creatures.md FP rule). Rollback: revert.

<a id="s-133b"></a>

### S-133b · feat: Clover's Dash on Duskwick and Archwater, and Rook's Races part 2

Milestone M16 · Depends on: S-112, S-113, S-127b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/side-content.md#act-dash](../bible/side-content.md#act-dash), [../bible/side-content.md#ss-49](../bible/side-content.md#ss-49),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-poi](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-poi), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-poi](../bible/atlas-isles.md#archwater-poi),
[../bible/cast.md#rook](../bible/cast.md#rook)

#### Why

Second half of INDEX S-133 (R30): two more Dash courses (3 and 4 of 10) and Rook's second race, all data on the
frameworks S-112 and S-113 built. Roadmap: M16.

#### What

1. **Courses** (S-112's framework: start post, numbered ring gates, timer, medals from par P, tiers bronze 15 gems /
   silver 20 gems / gold a ribbon on Clover's windowsill), placed by each land's `placeIsleDash` pass
   (`new RNG(seed * 73 + 3)`), each gate the nearest walkable cell within 3 m of its anchor (R15):
   - **Duskwick** (`duskwick:dash`): start post (−2, 34) → gates (30, −10), (60, −60), (84, −88), (60, −112),
     (0, −120), (−60, −120), (−112, −70), (−110, −30), (−70, 20) → finish at the start. Ribbon colour `#ffcf7a`.
   - **Archwater** (`archwater:dash`): start the Landing Bench (−200, 20) → gates (−150, −30), (−90, −40), (−30, −40),
     (40, −50), (100, −40), (90, 40), (80, 100), (0, 110), (−80, 90) → finish (−196, 24); every gate on a path cell
     walkable at both tides. Ribbon colour `#7fd3d0`.
   - **Par**: each course's in-page test runs the careful bot (S-112's: sprints, rolls on straights, takes the drawn
     line) and records P in ms into the course data (`par: <ms>`), then medals are gold ≤ 1.10 P, silver ≤ 1.30 P,
     bronze ≤ 1.60 P. The PR body lists P for both courses.
   - Save: `collect.dash['dash:duskwick']`, `['dash:archwater']` = `Math.round(seconds * 10)` (tenths of a second,
     1-36000; only a lower value replaces it); medal flags S-112's `act.dash.<realm>.bronze|silver|gold`.
2. **Rook's Races part 2** (ss-49, step `race2`): Rook's spot on Archwater `archwater.rook_start` by the Stair Bench
   (−6, −44) on an empty lamp-stand, 07:00-19:00, from `ss49.race1` won and `realm:archwater.visited`. `[E] Talk ·
   Rook` starts it: 3-2-1; route (atlas) Stair Bench → Arch Bench (112, −60) → the grotto stair top (124, −60) → W3 →
   the Spouter (90, 120); ≈ 380 m; Rook flies a fixed spline (a ghost, no collisions) timed to the careful bot × 1.15
   (S-113's rule); the race ends when Pip touches the finish post at the Spouter's tide post foot. Win: 8 gems once,
   Rook's flag at the finish ("Pip was here first. —R. (grudgingly)"), win line (R15) "You beat me to the Spouter. I was
   busy looking at the arch. ...That's my excuse. It's a good excuse."; lose lines (S-113's cycle) "Ha! Rematch? Go on.
   I'll even pretend to stretch." / "Finders keepers! ...Again?" Rook's start line: "Called it! Race you to the Spouter,
   rival. Loser counts hexagons."
3. Each course appears in the journal's Activities (Clover's Dash 3/10 and 4/10 entries appear as their land is
   visited); Clover's windowsill ribbons count up (drawn when S-156 builds her room; until then the count shows in the
   journal).

#### Assets

None new (S-112's `course_gate`, S-113's Rook rig and flag).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Both courses: start post, gates in order, a skipped gate blinks; P recorded; medal thresholds derived from P; tiers
  pay once; leaving by 30 m ends a run with no result; enemy spawns paused during a run.
- Archwater's course works at both tides; Duskwick's at both tides.
- Rook part 2: Rook's time = bot × 1.15 ± 0.2 s; win pays 8 gems once and plants the flag; losing changes nothing.
- Invariants: frozen realms (post-passes only), one save slot, tone (no fail state), machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (course data validation: gates on walkable cells, order, P present).
- In-page Dash suite (S-112): run the careful bot on both courses at both tides; record P; run Rook part 2 with the bot
  (win) and with a slow bot (lose).

#### Out of scope

Dash on Threemills and Rook part 3 (S-154).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: long courses (≈ 640 m and 760 m) make P large; medals scale with P, so no tier is impossible. Rollback: revert.

### S-134 · feat: side story 3 Nothing Is Refundable, part 1: Granny Mochi's cottage

Milestone M16 · Depends on: S-092, S-054 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/side-content.md#ss-03](../bible/side-content.md#ss-03), [../bible/cast.md#granny-mochi](../bible/cast.md#granny-mochi),
[../bible/atlas-home.md#interiors](../bible/atlas-home.md#interiors), [../bible/atlas-home.md#placePortals](../bible/atlas-home.md#placePortals),
[../bible/atlas-home.md#spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#spots)

#### Why

The yawning-cat cottage opens: Granny Mochi, the very old grandmother of Miso and Kinako, brokers the first letter of a
twenty-year sulk. Part 1 of 2 (part 2 ships with the Kettle stories in S-241). Roadmap: INDEX S-134 (M16).

#### What

1. **Granny Mochi** look `mochi` (`cat` rig, 0.9 m seated / 1.0 m standing, palette from cast.md: fur `#f8f1e4`, ginger
   patches `#f2c79a`, grey muzzle `#c9c2b8`, lilac shawl `#b8a6d0`, silver spectacles `#c0c4cc` (two `torus`es), pink
   yarn `#e88a8a`, body lathe widened × 1.3); voice tic "Mm-hm." and "kitten" (reserve "Mm-hm." in the charter's
   markers, speaker `mochi`); sound cue: knitting-needle clicks within 8 m (`T` ticks 2,400 Hz, v 0.02, 3 Hz) with a
   ball of yarn rolling on her sill as the twin. Portrait `portrait_mochi` (core, `assets/portraits/mochi.webp`, ≤ 60 KB)
   from cast.md's prompt.
2. **Spots**: `home:spot-mochi-step` appended to `placeSpots` (atlas-home row 6, append-only:
   `near(cottage 4 door + 1.5 m out, 0, 2)`), sunny afternoons 13:00-17:00 dozing on her step; otherwise indoors at the
   window seat. Before S-174 schedules, use the time-window switch S-115 built for Dormy (`grep -rn "dormy" js/`).
3. **Interior `cottage_mochi`** (R23) from atlas-home's cottage template (house-sized, 7 × 6 tiles: `#w###w#` /
   `#.....#` ×3 / `#..@..#` / `###X###`), card "Granny Mochi's Cottage" / "Sunny spot taken": window seat under (1, 0)
   with a sunbeam card; knitting basket (1, 2); letter desk (4, 1) holding the twins' letters (`[E] Look · Letter
   desk` "Two stacks of letters. One stack says 'MISO'. One says 'KINAKO'. Neither has been opened."); on the east wall
   a painting of two kittens (`[E] Look · A painting` "Two kittens painting one shop sign. One has paint on her nose.
   One has paint on everything else."). Kit from `js/props-interior-home.js` (accent `#f2b45a`). Prints fixture.
4. **Door and meeting** (atlas-home#placePortals row 5): the first knock keeps the shipped line "Knock knock!
   ...Nobody answers. A cat yawns at you through the window." (LOCKED, `DOOR_LINES[3]`). From ss-03's `refused` step,
   a knock opens the door: Mochi `side.ss03.mochi.0` "Mm-hm. You'll be the one carrying boxes for my grandchildren.
   Come in, kitten. Mind the wool." → `set:met:mochi`; from then `[E] Enter · Granny Mochi's Cottage` replaces Knock
   (`home:portal-cottage-4`, needs `met:mochi`).
5. **Quest `ss-03`** (`n: 103`, owner `miso`, `unlock: ['flag:ch8', 'met:kinako']`), steps 1-3 now, 4-5 in S-241:

   | Step | Text | Done | Lines |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `box` | "Take Kinako's 'defective' sword back to Miso" | `item:kinako_box` | at Kinako's Bazaar: `side.ss03.kinako.1` "Return this to my sister!! It's DEFECTIVE!! Not nearly shiny enough!! ...Don't tell her I said 'sister'." → `give:kinako_box` |
   | `refused` | "Miso won't take it. Ask Granny Mochi" | `met:mochi` | Miso `side.ss03.miso.1` "Mrrow~ Nothing is refundable. Especially not to him~ He can keep his box." then the knock above |
   | `letter` | "Carry Granny Mochi's letter to Kinako" | `flag:ss03.letter` | Mochi `side.ss03.mochi.1` "Twenty years over one opening day. Cats. ...I've raised a lot of cats, Pip, and every one of them sulks." · `side.ss03.mochi.2` "Take him this. Don't read it. ...You can read it a little." → `give:mochi_letter`; at the Bazaar `[E] Give · Letter` → `take:mochi_letter`, `take:kinako_box`, `side.ss03.kinako.2` "...She kept the sign? The one we painted? ...She KEPT it?!" → `set:ss03.letter`, `gems:+15` |

   After step 3 the journal shows "To be continued ✿" (step 4 waits for `ss41.done`, Kettle Isle). Reading the letter
   (`[E] Read` on it in the Items tab): "Kinako. Your sister kept the sign. The whole half of it. She dusts it on
   Sundays. Stop sulking and come to tea. — Gran. P.S. Bring biscuits." (≤ 600 characters, letter frame).
   Journal open: "Two cats, one sulk, twenty years. The literature on cat sulks is vast and unread."
6. **Mochi's lines** otherwise: idle at the window "Mm-hm. Sunny spot's taken, kitten. It's always taken. By me."; the
   bazaar service line from S-092 moves to her once she is met: "Mm-hm. My grandson's at {land} today, kitten. The
   other one." (`{land}` filled from Kinako's dock schedule, S-092).
7. Items: `kinako_box` (delivery, "A long box. It rattles 'defectively'."), `mochi_letter` (delivery, readable).

#### Assets

`portrait_mochi` (generated, core). Code-built Mochi from the `cat` rig. Cottage furniture from the interior kit. ART
BRIEF `npc_mochi` for batch 07 (cast.md verbatim).

#### Acceptance criteria

- The first knock at cottage 4 always shows the LOCKED line; Mochi is met only through ss-03; afterwards the portal
  works and `cottage_mochi` enters and leaves cleanly (prints fixture, ≤ 60 calls).
- ss-03 steps 1-3 complete with 15 gems once; the quest then shows "To be continued ✿" and is not counted as done.
- `~` only in Miso's lines; `!!` only in Kinako's; "Mm-hm." only in Mochi's.
- `world-prints.json` unchanged; the `placeSpots` append changes no earlier spot (world test on seeds 1-40).
- Invariants: frozen home island, one save slot, tone, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (world test for the spot append, interior compile, quest lint, content lint).
- In-page home stories suite (S-115's: `grep -rln "dormy" js/debug/tests/`): set `ch8` and `met:kinako`, do steps 1-3
  with warps between home and Duskwick, assert flags, gems, the portal.
- `bun run test:playthrough`.

#### Out of scope

Steps 4-5 and the joined sign (S-241); Miso's back room (M27).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the shipped knock line must stay first; the door handler checks `seen:door.cottage4` before swapping. Rollback:
revert; the door returns to the shipped knock.

<a id="s-135"></a>

### S-135 · feat: side story 5 A Bun for Every Isle: Hazel's bakery and buns

Milestone M16 · Depends on: S-054, S-035, S-078 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/side-content.md#ss-05](../bible/side-content.md#ss-05), [../bible/cast.md#hazel-thimble](../bible/cast.md#hazel-thimble),
[../bible/systems.md#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools), [../bible/atlas-home.md#interiors](../bible/atlas-home.md#interiors),
[../bible/atlas-home.md#spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#spots)

#### Why

The cinnamon-bun cottage opens: Hazel, who fed Pip as a baby, sells buns (a cheaper half-heart heal) and wants one bun
with something from every isle in it. The delivery list grows as lands ship; this step ships Duskwick, Pinewhistle and
Archwater. Roadmap: INDEX S-135 (M16).

#### What

1. **Hazel Thimble** look `hazel` (`rodent` rig with `spines: true`: a half-`sphere` shell over the back with 24 small
   `cone`s, `tail: none`; palette spines `#8a6a4a` tipped `#e8d6b8`, face `#f2dcc0`, rose apron `#e88a8a`, white kerchief
   `#fff8e6`, red oven mitt `#c2563f`; `extra` tray with three `torus` buns `#c98b4b`), 0.8 m. Tic: baking measures; calls
   Pip "sprout" (reserve "sprout" for `hazel` in the charter's nickname list). Sound cue: an oven-timer *ding* every 45 s
   by day within 12 m, with a puff of flour from the window. Portrait `portrait_hazel` (core) from cast.md's prompt.
2. **Bun item** `bun` (systems.md): consumable, stack 5, 10 gems, heals half a heart (1 HP). The `potion` action drinks a
   potion; with none it eats a bun ("Mmm. Cinnamon."); with neither, the shipped line "No potions! Miso sells them."
   HUD shows the bun count next to the potion count when > 0 (no overlap: `G.debug.hud.audit()`).
3. **Bun stall** at `home:spot-hazel-stall` (`placeSpots` append: `near((−19, 9), 1, 4)`), 07:00-11:00:
   `[E] Shop · Hazel's buns` (one row: bun 10 gems; max carry 5: "Your pockets are full of buns, sprout."). Outside
   those hours Hazel is in the bakery. Before S-174, the time-window switch S-115 built.
4. **Interior `cottage_hazel`** (R23; atlas-home cottage 3 "Hazel's Bakery" / "Mind the flour"): brick oven (3, 0) with a
   `glow` flicker; counter across (1..5, 2) as a solid row with a gap at (5, 2); flour sacks; Hazel behind the counter;
   `[E] Shop · Hazel` (the same bun row); the **bun rack** on the west wall shows one bun per isle delivered (10 slots).
   Prints fixture.
5. **Door and meeting**: the first knock keeps the LOCKED line ("Mmm, it smells like cinnamon buns in there. The door is
   locked, sadly." `DOOR_LINES[2]`). After `wennaAwake`, a knock between 06:00 and 11:00 opens it: "Buns aren't ready.
   ...Fine, they're ready. Come in before they're gone." → `met:hazel`; then `[E] Enter · Hazel's Bakery`
   (`home:portal-cottage-3`).
6. **Quest `ss-05`** (`n: 105`, owner `hazel`, `unlock: ['flag:lanternGot']`, 35 min across the game). Steps:
   - `meet` "Visit the cinnamon-bun cottage" (`met:hazel`): `side.ss05.hazel.1` "Come in, come in, mind the flour! A
     bun's half a heart, sprout. Ten gems. A cuddle's free." · `side.ss05.hazel.2` "I'd like to bake one bun with every
     isle in it. For the festival. One day. When everyone's home."
   - `deliver` "Deliver a bun to someone on every isle ({n}/10)" (parallel; `give:bun_parcel` when the step starts and
     again, free, each time one is delivered: key item "Hazel's bun parcel", stack 1). Recipients this step (the rest
     hide as "Somewhere past the fog..." until their land ships, side-content rule 5): `[E] Give · Bun` →
     `take:bun_parcel`, `gems:+3`, the ingredient key item, `set:ss05.<land>`:

     | Land | Recipient | Their line | Ingredient (key item) |
     |---|---|---|---|
     | Duskwick | Granny Wick | "Hm. Hm. A bun from Hazel? She always did bake for the lamp-keepers. Take her some of my moth-bean flour, dear." | `ing_mothbean` "moth-bean flour" |
     | Pinewhistle | Elder Nutmeg | "A bun! For safekeeping! ...No, for eating. For eating. Here, take Hazel a jar of pine-nut butter." | `ing_pinenut` "pine-nut butter" |
     | Archwater | Skipper Rafferty | "(sings) A bun, a bun, from over the sea... Ta, matey! Take Hazel a pinch of our sea salt. Best on the Wide Blue." | `ing_seasalt` "sea salt from the Raft's pans" |

   - `bake` "Bring the ingredients to Hazel": waits for 9 ingredients and the Driftholm delivery (later lands); shows
     "To be continued ✿" while nothing deliverable remains.
7. **World mark** (per delivery, within 10 s): Hazel's outside windowsill (a 10-slot sill prop on cottage 3, placed with
   the stall by the same `placeSpots` entry's record) shows one small bun per delivered isle, each tinted for its land
   (Duskwick `#ffcf7a` glaze, Pinewhistle `#c98b4b` nut, Archwater `#e2c9a0` salt crystals); the indoor rack mirrors it.
8. Journal: open "Hazel wants a bun with every isle in it. Scientifically, that is a very large bun."
9. **Economy**: buns are a gem sink with no cap; `tests/economy.test.js` gains the 3-gem deliveries (30 total) and the
   end reward (30) in the side-story sum (still 1,855 overall) and treats buns as an optional consumable sink.

#### Assets

`portrait_hazel` (generated, core). Code-built Hazel, bun (`torus` `#c98b4b` with an icing swirl `#fff3e0`), bun icon
cell `bun` in the items atlas (kind `icon`: "a round cinnamon bun with a white icing swirl", 128²). ART BRIEF `npc_hazel`
for batch 07 (cast.md verbatim).

#### Acceptance criteria

- The LOCKED knock line shows first; Hazel can be met only in the morning window after `wennaAwake`; the bakery
  portal and interior work (prints, ≤ 60 calls, no leak).
- Buns: 10 gems each, carry 5, heal 1 HP; the potion button falls back to a bun; the HUD audit passes at 1080p and
  390 × 844.
- Three deliveries possible now, each 3 gems once and an ingredient; the sill shows each within 10 s; deliveries for
  unbuilt lands are hidden.
- Economy test passes; content lint ("sprout" only for Hazel; `(sings)` only for Rafferty; "For safekeeping!" only for
  Nutmeg; "Hm. Hm." only for Wick).
- `world-prints.json` unchanged; spot appends move no earlier spot.
- Invariants: frozen home island, one save slot (buns in `S.items`), tone, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (items, potion-action fallback unit test, economy, world test, quest lint).
- In-page home stories suite: knock at 08:00, buy 5 buns, try a 6th, eat one at 2 HP missing, deliver to the three
  recipients via warps, check the sill.
- `bun run test:playthrough`; `node tests/inpage.mjs wp8` (HUD layouts; shard names per the CI matrix).

#### Out of scope

Deliveries on later lands (each land's side-story step adds its row); the Every-Isle Bun (after Driftholm).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the potion action change touches a LOCKED control; the fallback runs only when potions are 0, so the shipped path
is unchanged (test:playthrough). Rollback: revert; buns in saves are inert.

<a id="s-136"></a>

### S-136 · feat: side story 6 The Duck Regatta with the Quackenbushes

Milestone M16 · Depends on: S-112, S-068 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/side-content.md#ss-06](../bible/side-content.md#ss-06), [../bible/side-content.md#act-regatta](../bible/side-content.md#act-regatta),
[../bible/cast.md#the-quackenbushes](../bible/cast.md#the-quackenbushes), [../bible/atlas-home.md#courses](../bible/atlas-home.md#courses),
[../bible/atlas-home.md#spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#spots), [../bible/side-content.md#plushies](../bible/side-content.md#plushies)

#### Why

The ducks have been jealous of Ribbit's croak since the parade (LOCKED). They get an event of their own: a herding
race along the Shores shallows, started by Ribbit's croak. Roadmap: INDEX S-136 (M16).

#### What

1. **Five ducks** (R12, `bird` rig from S-068 with `beak: 'flat'`, scaled per member): `duck_dabble` Admiral Dabble
   0.70 m with a folded paper admiral's hat `#f4f1ea` with a red stripe `#c2563f`; `duck_dilly` 0.60 m pink ribbon
   `#f59aa8`; `duck_dally` 0.60 m blue ribbon `#8fb8ff`; `duck_quilliam` 0.62 m purple bow tie `#6b4a8c`; `duck_bobbin`
   0.45 m yellow duckling `#f6d860`; white `#f7f4ec`, bills and feet `#f29b38`. Spots `home:spot-ducks-1..5` (append to
   `placeSpots`: five points on a circle r 2.4 m round the village pond (−13, 25), on the water; they bob ±0.03 m).
   Group portrait `portrait_ducks` (core) from cast.md's prompt. Voice: they finish one sentence between them; their
   practice croak is "Qroak." (reserve it for the `ducks` group). Sound cue: a quack chorus within 15 m every 20-40 s,
   with V-shaped ripples behind them.
2. **Quest `ss-06`** (`n: 106`, owner `ducks` (Admiral Dabble speaks), `unlock: ['ending']`, reward `['gems:+25',
   'set:ss06.done']`, 15 min). Steps: `meet` "Meet the Quackenbushes at the village pond" (talk to Admiral Dabble:
   `side.ss06.dabble.1` "Quack. The Mayor has a croak. We have a regatta. Well, we will. Once you help." then Bobbin
   `side.ss06.bobbin.1` "I'm the fastest! ...I'm the smallest. Same thing, nearly."); `race` "Herd the ducks through the
   Regatta course" (finish one run); `starter` "Ask Mayor Ribbit to be the starter" (Ribbit, priority 70 while active:
   `side.ss06.ribbit.1` "A starter's croak? For the ducks? ...It would be my civic duty. CROAK. Was that loud enough? It
   was, wasn't it." → `set:ss06.starter`). Done line `side.ss06.dabble.2` "A croak to start a duck race. Ridiculous.
   Wonderful. Same time next year." Group line on talk after: "We have..." / "...been practising..." / "...a croak." /
   "Qroak." / "Quack." (one box, the five phrases joined).
3. **The course** (R27; atlas-home `home:course-regatta`, appended to `placeCourses` row 11, `new RNG(7151 * 107 + 47)`):
   a regatta post at the jetty root (−14, 126) (`near` the root, 2-4 m west of the jetty, dry sand); **8 buoy gates**
   (S-112's `course_gate` scaled 0.75: 1.8 m wide painted arches with duck flags) in walkable shallows (`h` −0.4 to
   0.2) along the beach westward, 8-12 m apart, the chain chosen by the pass (gate k + 1 is the valid shallows cell
   8-12 m from gate k with the smallest x, preferring z within ±6 m of gate k), and a finish post at the islet-1 bridge
   foot. The ducks' guide line (a painted rope of floats, decorative) runs 6 m further out to sea. If the shallows band
   cannot fit 8 gates on seed 7151, use 6 (side-content's count) and say so in the PR body.
4. **Rules** (`js/activities/regatta.js`, pure where possible): `[E] Use · Regatta post` shows best and tiers; the five
   ducks flap from the pond to the start line (a 1.0 s feather-puff move, they do not path across the village); Admiral
   Dabble quacks the start, or after `ss06.starter` Ribbit does: a Ribbit scene actor stands on the bank by the post for
   the run and croaks "Three, two, one, CROAK!" (his plaza NPC hides during the run). Ducks follow Pip when within 4 m,
   keeping a loose V 1.5-3 m behind (follow point behind Pip, separation 0.8 m, top speed 4.0 m/s); a **Hop** makes every
   duck within 4 m flap 2 m forward; a duck more than 10 m from Pip flaps to 3 m behind him in 1.0 s (no duck is ever
   lost). A gate counts when all five ducks have passed through it; the next gate glows. The run ends at the finish
   post with all five through the last gate, or by leaving 30 m / `[E]` at the post (no result). Ducks walk on sand and
   swim on water cells (they ignore walkability). Enemy spawns paused (the Shores have Snippy Crabs).
5. **Tiers** (each once): **under 120 s** 40 gems; **under 90 s** the Quackenbushes give Miso their pattern and the
   duck plushie `plush:duck` goes on sale at 100 gems (R32: desc "One duck. Stands for all five. They argued about
   which.", got "A Quackenbush plushie! Quack-purr~", stock rule S-045); **under 70 s** the gold duck `give:nook_gift_duck` (gift item, kind `furnishing`; listed in Items under Keepsakes until S-302a puts it on the Nook's trophy stand);
   Pip is "an honorary Quackenbush" and the ducks quack at him when he passes within 6 m of the pond (once per 30 s). Save
   `act.regatta.best` (ms), `act.regatta.t1..t3`. Line on start `side.act.regatta.dabble.1` "All five through every
   gate, mind. We're a family. We finish together. Mostly at once."
6. **World mark of ss-06** (within 10 s, for good): six painted regatta buoys (red, yellow, white) bob on the village
   pond; Ribbit croaks every regatta start. Journal: open "The ducks wish to race. Ducks do not race. Ducks bob. I stand
   corrected (by a duck)." / done "Ribbit starts the regatta now. The ducks have forgiven his croak. Mostly."
7. **Par check**: the regatta's in-page bot (walks the gate line, hops when a duck lags 3 m) must finish in ≤ 60 s so
   the 70 s trophy tier is reachable; record its time in the PR body.

#### Assets

`portrait_ducks` (generated, core, ≤ 60 KB). Code-built ducks (`bird` rig), buoys (`cyl` + `cone`, `#c2563f` /
`#f2c94c` / `#f7f4ec`), the paper hat (`cone` folded). ART BRIEF `npc_duck` for batch 07 (cast.md verbatim).

#### Acceptance criteria

- `world-prints.json` unchanged; `placeSpots` and `placeCourses` appends move no earlier item (world test seeds 1-40);
  all gates in the shallows band, 8-12 m apart; the start post and finish reachable on foot.
- The bot finishes ≤ 60 s; ducks never get stuck (no duck > 10 m from Pip for more than 1.2 s during a run).
- Tiers pay once; the plushie appears in Miso's stock after tier 2 and the bought-out rule.
- Ribbit croaks the start only after `ss06.starter`; LOCKED Ribbit lines and spot are untouched otherwise.
- "Qroak." only for the ducks; content lint.
- Invariants: frozen home island, one save slot, tone (no fail state, LOCKED "the ducks are jealous" kept), assets,
  machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (world test SLOW group for the appends; regatta pure logic: gate counting, catch-up; quest lint; economy).
- In-page activity suite: run the regatta bot (time, all tiers with a forced fast clock for tier checks), then talk to
  Ribbit and assert the starter croak on the next run.
- `bun run test:playthrough` (Ribbit's plaza behaviour unchanged).

#### Out of scope

The Duck Pond Dock and Bandstand (Village Works, S-157).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: herding feel; the catch-up rule guarantees completion. Rollback: revert; the passes are append-only.

### S-138 · feat: Threemills downs recipe: farmland, hedgerows, millpond (hidden)

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-117 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.threemills.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-card](../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-card),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-recipe](../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-recipe),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-regions](../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-regions),
[../bible/systems.md#realm-registry](../bible/systems.md#realm-registry), [../bible/art-audio.md#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes)

#### Why

M17's first brick: the farm isle west-south-west of home, rolling chalk downs cut into fields by hedgerows (walls you
cannot hop: the land's routing), a millpond that low water drains, and Tumble Hill with its three mills. It reuses
S-117's tide machinery (a second land with a tide). Roadmap: INDEX S-138 (M17 Threemills; the Tower unlocks);
vision item **Threemills; the Tower unlocks**.

#### What

1. **Registry** (copy the `archwater` entry): `threemills: { id: 'threemills', name: 'Threemills', kind: 'overworld',
   size: 576, seed: 40237, recipe: 'downs', palette: 'threemills', music: 'threemills', ambience: 'threemills_farm',
   hidden: true, open: 'flag:ch8', arrive: 'jetty', bundle: 'threemills', enemies: 'threemills', dash: true, benches: 6,
   rootgate: true, tide: { flag: 'tide:threemills', low: -1.4, high: 0, absent: 'high' }, views: [...] }`. Spawns
   `jetty` (204, −18) facing 267° (Middle Mill dead ahead), `granary` (0, 44) facing 180°, `rootgate` (94, −144) facing
   67°. Register `tide:threemills` (toggle, rewritable).
2. **Recipe `js/realms/recipes/downs.js`** (a `RealmWorld` subclass like `basalt.js`; every stage on its own
   `new RNG(40237 * k + c)`), exactly atlas-isles#threemills-recipe items 1-10:
   - Outline: shore radius `226 + 14·fbm(θ; 4 cycles)`; north coast (bearings 300°-60°) low chalk cliffs 3-5 m (slope >
     1.0); south and east beaches easing to h 1 at the shore.
   - Downs: `h = 4 + 6·fbm(x/90, z/90; 3 octaves)` inland, easing to 1 m at the beaches.
   - Tumble Hill: `+30·(1 − (d/88)²)²` at (0, −8) (summit ≈ 35); three spurs flattened (disc r 7, blend 10 m): Tansy
     (−32, 10) h 27, Thrift (30, 14) h 26, Middle Mill (0, −8) h 35; the Granary pad (0, 30) h 24.8 r 8 and the Hoist
     House pad (12, 34) r 4; the cellar door apron (0, 36) to (0, 44) a ramp down to h 23.6 (the spawn `granary`).
   - Millpond: ellipse (−70, 120) rx 62 rz 40, floor −1.6 in the middle third, −0.6 near the rim; a 10 m channel from
     (−70, 160) to the south coast (−70, 226) at floor −1.6; Heron Island (−60, 112) r 6 top h 1.0; marsh h 0.2-0.6
     round the west and south rims. At low water (`waterY` −1.4) the whole pond floor is walkable (`h ≥ −1.8`).
   - Boardwalks (bridge decks 2 m wide, top h 1.4): **B1** Old Sluice House (−6, 118) → along the south edge →
     junction (−60, 150); **B2** junction → the Marsh Lamp ring platform (deck r 4 round (−104, 136), h 1.4); **B3**
     junction → north-west through the reeds → the reed island (−150, 120) (island r 6, h 0.8). The Old Sluice House pad
     (−6, 118) 10 × 8 m at h 1.2 on the pond's east outlet.
   - Fields and hedgerows (stream `new RNG(40237 * 7 + 19)`): a field grid of 55 m cells rotated 12°, jittered ±8 m,
     clipped to land outside Tumble Hill's 60 m core, Haybury's box (`x ≥ 118`, `|z| < 110`) and the pond's rx + 8 m;
     70 % of cell edges carry a **hedgerow**: a collider rail 1.4 m tall, 1.2 m thick (Pip's hop apex is 0.85 m: never
     hoppable), with **≥ 2 stiles** per hedge (2 m gaps with a 0.4 m step deck) and a **field gate** (a 3 m gap) wherever
     a path crosses. Store `W.hedges` (segments with stile and gate gaps) and `W.fields` (polygon, area, ground type).
     Ground types by field: 45 % wheat, 20 % barley, 15 % clover pasture, 20 % fallow (splat slots `meadow` wheat,
     `forest` orchard floor where orchards are, `dirt` furrow for fallow, `grass` pasture).
   - Orchards: eight blocks (≈ 50 × 40 m each) in Orchard Rows on the north fields, reserved as `W.orchards` (S-139a
     fills them with rows of trees 8 m apart along 12°); the Grandmother Apple disc (−40, −140) r 14 reserved.
   - Haybury: box centre (150, 10) reserved for S-139a's cottages (`W.reserved`), Festival Green disc (140, 30) r 12 flat,
     the Pie Shop pad (148, 14), the Weathercock Barn pad (160, −20) 14 × 10 m at h 5.
   - The jetty: deck from the shore (206, −20) 26 m east to (232, −24), 3 m wide, top h 1.4; bollard at the end.
   - Caves (dark zones, `W.dark`): **the Cider Cave** mouth (−146, −66) cut 8 m deep and 4 m wide into a chalk bank
     (floor at the local h); **the north chalk bank** door niche (−20, −200) cut 2 m into the cliff (S-144 puts a Hollow
     door there).
   - Paths (2.4 m, splat slot `snow` = `threemills_chalk`, the white chalky farm track of art-audio), stamped through
     hedges as field gates: **R1** jetty → Festival
     Green (140, 30) → (100, 40) → (60, 40) → a switchback up Tumble Hill's south face → the Granary (0, 30); **R5** the
     summit lane Tansy (−32, 10) → Middle Mill (0, −8) → Thrift (30, 14) → the Granary; **R2** Green → (150, −60) →
     (110, −120) → (40, −140) → the Grandmother Apple (−40, −140); **R3** (−70, 0) → (−50, 60) → the Old Sluice House;
     **R4 the Long Lane** (hedge-lined both sides) (−120, −60) → (−150, 0) → (−140, 90) → the reed island; **R6**
     Grandmother Apple → (−80, −90) → (−120, −60).
   - Sightlines: a 6 m tree-free corridor from each orchard block's centre toward Middle Mill (scatter reservation).
3. **Regions** (rule exactly the atlas): `haybury` if `x ≥ 118` and `|z| < 110` (town, `max 0`); else `tumblehill` if
   within 92 m of (0, 0); else by bearing `orchard` [285°, 135°), `millpond` [135°, 285°). Cards: **Haybury** · "Pies
   cooling", **Tumble Hill** · "Three mills, one hill", **Orchard Rows** · "Apples, always apples", **Millpond Marsh** ·
   "Boots optional". Entry points: jetty spawn, (60, 40), (110, −120), (−50, 60). Fog per art-audio (R11):
   `haybury [32, 240, #f6ecd0, .10]`, `tumblehill [36, 262, #f4efd8, .12]`, `orchard [28, 225, #eef0d0, .18]`,
   `millpond [24, 200, #dfeedd, .30]`. Enemies: every region `[]`, `max 0` this step.
4. **Palette** `W.palette.threemills` (art-audio row): accents `#7fae4b #e7c45a #c2563f #a7d3f0`; sky day `#5aaef2` /
   `#e2f2fb`, gold `#ffe2a0`, dusk `#7484cc` / `#ffc890`, night shipped; water deep `#2f86b8`, shallow `#a7d3f0`; terrain
   keys `threemills_pasture`, `threemills_orchard`, `threemills_wheat`, `threemills_pondsand`, `threemills_chalk`,
   `threemills_furrow`, `threemills_flint`, cliff = home `cliff_hd` (fallback: home layers tinted with the accents until
   S-140). A wheat colour uniform `uWheat` (0 green `#8fbf4a`, 1 gold `#e7c45a`) is declared now and stays 0.
5. **Views**: (1) town: jetty (204, −18) yaw 267 pitch 2; (2) landmark: (60, 40) yaw 300 pitch 8 (the three mills);
   (3) densest: (−70, −100) yaw 60 pitch 0 (orchard rows); (4) widest: Middle Mill gallery (0, −8) at eye height 49.9
   yaw 200 pitch −12.
6. **Debug**: `?tide=low` and `G.debug.tide()` already generic (S-117).

#### Assets

None (fallback tints). S-139a/b build props, S-140 paints.

#### Acceptance criteria

- `generateRealm('threemills')` is deterministic; `W.genMs` ≤ 1,200 ms; reachable land 148,000-172,000 m².
- In both tide states `W.unreachable` is empty; the three spawns and four entry points are reachable; every field is
  reachable through stiles or gates (BFS); no hedge segment has fewer than two stiles; every path crossing has a gate.
- At low water the pond floor and Heron Island are walkable; at high water Heron Island is not reachable on foot.
- Region samples: (150, 10) `haybury`; (0, 0) `tumblehill`; (−40, −140) `orchard`; (−70, 120) `millpond`.
- Prints `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/threemills.json` with `low` / `high` walk and reach checksums; home prints
  untouched; Threemills hidden.
- Invariants: frozen home island, frozen realms, hidden until whole, budgets (genMs), machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/realm-threemills.test.js` (new, the Archwater test's shape): prints, genMs, area, per-state reach,
  hedge rules (stiles, gates, collider height 1.4), regions, path heights.
- `bun test`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (Threemills added; four views both cameras; both tides).

#### Out of scope

Props and landmarks (S-139a, S-139b), art and music (S-140), people (S-141), enemies (S-142), finds (S-144).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: hedges can wall off a field; the stile rule and the BFS test catch it. Rollback: revert; hidden.

<a id="s-139a"></a>

### S-139a · feat: Threemills prop kit: hedgerows, wheat, orchards, haystacks and Haybury's cottages

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-138, S-082 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.threemills.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-kit](../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-kit),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-threemills-others](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-threemills-others),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-interiors](../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-interiors)

#### Why

Half of INDEX S-139 (split, R30): the instanced kit that makes the downs read as farmland (hedges, wheat squares,
orchard rows) and Haybury as a village. The landmarks follow in S-139b.

#### What

1. **Prop builders** (`js/props.js` or Pinewhistle's realm prop module), code-built toon, ink hull where shipped props
   have one:
   - `threemills_hedgerow`: 4 m segment 1.4 × 1.2 m, leaves `#5f8f3a` with hawthorn flowers `#f4efe2` (vertex dots),
     ≤ 160 tris, LOD to a box at 40 m; laid along every `W.hedges` segment (the colliders are the recipe's).
   - `threemills_stile` (two-step wooden stile, ≤ 120) at every stile gap; `threemills_gate` (five-bar gate, ≤ 140)
     swung open at every field gate.
   - `threemills_wheat` and `threemills_barley`: deco card clusters 1 m (wheat tinted by `uWheat`, barley `#d9c98a`
     green-tinted `#a8c46a` while dim), scattered at 0.6 per m² in wheat and barley fields (instanced, distance-culled
     at 60 m), sway in wind.
   - `threemills_haystack` (domed 3 m `#e7c45a`, ≤ 200), `threemills_bale` (≤ 60), `threemills_cart` (≤ 500),
     `threemills_scarecrow_pole` (2.2 m cross with a straw tuft, ≤ 100), `threemills_reed` (card cluster, millpond rims
     and marsh), `threemills_boardwalk_post` (along B1-B3), `threemills_lilypad` (pond), `threemills_milestone` (0.4 m,
     ≤ 60; placed as curios in S-144), apple tree (reuse `tree_round`, leaves `#7fae4b`, an apple cluster of 12 small
     `sphere`s `#c2563f`), round tree (reused), fence (reused), grass card, sunflower (reused flower card, `#f2c94c`).
   - **Orchards**: each `W.orchards` block filled with apple trees in rows 8 m apart along 12°, 7 m apart in the row,
     jitter ±0.6 m, keeping the sightline corridors clear.
   - **Haybury**: eight `threemills_cottage`s (3.5 m ridge, thatch `#e2c38a`, walls `#f4efe2`, door `#c2563f`, flower
     boxes; ≤ 1,000 tris) at (156, 30), (168, 4), (138, −8), (174, 40), (126, 52), (180, −36), (186, 18), (132, 22),
     doors facing the Green; `threemills_pie_shop` at (148, 14) (striped awning `#e7c45a` / `#c2563f`, an open counter of
     pies, ≤ 1,600); kitchen gardens (fence rows + cabbage spheres) at (130, −10) and (176, 56); the Festival Green
     (140, 30) with bunting strings between four posts (canvas bunting, the hedge card later). Door interactables with
     no text yet (S-141 adds the knock lines).
   - Shared kit types registered (no placements yet): `kit_bench`, `kit_ruin_pillar`, `kit_ruin_lampstump`,
     `kit_ruin_ring`, `kit_log_niche`.
   - Instanced types ≤ **24** exactly as atlas-isles#threemills-kit lists (unit test counts `W.scatterTypes`).
2. **Scatter tables**: `tumblehill` grass 6, sunflower 2, haystack 1; `orchard` grass 6, flower 2 (plus the orchard
   rows); `millpond` reed 6 on h < 0.8, grass 4, lily pads on the pond surface; `haybury` none (hand-placed). Density
   as home (0.30 deco, 0.032 props per m²).
3. **Material programs**: thatch, hedge, wheat (uniform lerp), reed, millpond tint (5 of the 8; S-139b adds chalk
   plaster, cupola glass, iron).
4. Refresh `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/threemills.json`.

#### Assets

Code-built only. ART BRIEFs pasted from atlas-isles#brief-threemills-others (`threemills_cottage`, `threemills_pie_shop`,
the small props) with commented-out GLB slots (`prop_threemills_hedgerow` 1.4, `prop_threemills_haystack` 3.0,
`prop_threemills_cottage` 3.5, `prop_threemills_apple_tree` 6.0).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Hedge visuals cover every hedge collider; stiles and gates sit in every gap; no prop blocks a path, stile or gate.
- ≤ 24 instanced types; ≤ 12 material programs; the four views within budget in both cameras (orchard view: ≤ 200 /
  300 calls, ≤ 1.3 M tris).
- Prints refreshed; home untouched; hidden.
- Invariants: frozen home island, frozen realms, hidden until whole, budgets, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/realm-threemills.test.js` (type count, material count, gap furniture, path clearance, prints).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (numbers in the PR body).

#### Out of scope

The mills, Granary, barn, Grandmother Apple, Marsh Lamp and Old Sluice House (S-139b).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: wheat cards are the triangle risk at the orchard and hill views; cap by distance cull (60 m) and density. Rollback:
revert; hidden.

<a id="s-139b"></a>

### S-139b · feat: Threemills landmarks: Tansy, Thrift and Middle Mill, the Granary, the barn, the Grandmother Apple and the Marsh Lamp

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-139a · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.threemills.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-landmarks](../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-landmarks),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-threemills-mill](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-threemills-mill),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-threemills-granary](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-threemills-granary),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-threemills-others](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-threemills-others), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-beacon](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-beacon)

#### Why

Second half of S-139 (R30): the landmarks a first-person player steers by, the sealed Granary with its hay avalanche and
dark sun-lamp, and the Old Sluice House that the chain needs.

#### What

1. **`threemills_mill`** (one model, three skins; sails a separate mesh, static until `beacon:threemills`): tapering tower
   base 7 m across, boat-shaped conical cap, four lattice sails on a hub, a door and two windows. **Middle Mill** (0, −8)
   ground 35: 22 m, plaster `#f2efe6`, trim `#a7d3f0`, sails 18 m; a **cap gallery** ring deck at 14 m (h 49) reached
   by an **inside stair**: the tower is hollow (ring collider with a door gap facing south), a spiral of 1.0 m bridge
   decks climbing 0.3 m per step round the inside wall to a hatch onto the gallery; gallery rail ring collider r 4.6.
   **Tansy Mill** (−32, 10) ground 27: 18 m, ochre `#e7c45a` + `#7a5a3e`, cream sails 16 m, no gallery. **Thrift Mill**
   (30, 14) ground 26: 18 m, barn red `#c2563f` + `#f4efe2`, cream sails 16 m, a cap gallery ring deck at 12 m (h 38)
   with no stair (Hook only, S-144's hookbloom). Shingles `#5e4a34`, sail frames `#8a6a48`, cloth `#f4ead0`. ≤ 4,000 tris
   each (sails ≤ 600). Brakes: a red brake bar on each cap (dim).
2. **The Granary** (0, 30) on its pad (h 24.8): round stone granary on staddle stones, 10 m across, 12 m tall, conical
   roof `#5e4a34`, a glass **cupola** 3.5 m holding the **sun-lamp** (`kit_beacon` 1.6× inside the glass, dark: glass
   `#6b6478`); stone `#d9cdb4`. Its south **cellar door** (0, 36) carries the sealed portal `threemills:granary` →
   `granary_vaults` spawn `entry` (`needs: 'flag:tm:door'`, R6; S-145 builds the realm). **The hay avalanche**: a 6 m
   heap of hay (`#e7c45a`, lumpy lathe) with a box collider over the door until `tm:door` (S-148a animates its lift).
   **The Hoist House** (12, 34): 5 × 4 × 6 m timber house with a 7 m crane arm over the heap (arm a separate mesh; swing
   and lift animation hooks exported for S-148a), a locked door. ≤ 3,600 tris together.
3. **The Weathercock Barn** (160, −20) on its pad: a red gambrel barn 16 m `#c2563f` with white trim `#f4efe2`, a loft
   door, and a copper weathercock `#b87a4a` (patina `#6fa08a`) 3 m on the ridge as a separate mesh turning to the
   realm's wind direction (the shipped wind vector, eased 0.5 rad/s). ≤ 2,800 tris.
4. **The Grandmother Apple** (−40, −140) ground 8: 18 m tall, 24 m across, gnarled trunk `#6e5236`, leaves `#7fae4b` /
   `#9fc45a`, apples `#c2563f`, a bird box; the lowest bough rests on a stone crutch (collider); a **high bough** deck
   2 × 6 m at h 24 (for S-144's hookbloom and chest-9) and a **low bough** at (−44, −132) h 2.4 (ss-28's hat later).
   ≤ 6,000 tris, LOD ≤ 700 beyond 48 m.
5. **The Marsh Lamp** (−104, 136) in the pond: an open iron-frame tower on four stilts `#4a4a52` (rust `#8a5a3c`), a
   stone lamp room `#d9cdb4` on top with a dark `kit_beacon` (it stays dark: it is a ruin until `mainEnding`), joined to
   S-138's ring platform. 15 m; ≤ 2,200 tris.
6. **The Old Sluice House** (−6, 118) on its pad: an 8 × 6 m stone house with an open doorway on its west side, a small
   side waterwheel (static until `tm:sluice`) over the outlet, three iron sluice wheels mounted on the inside north wall
   at (−9, 116.2), (−6, 116.2), (−3, 116.2) (meshes and interactable stubs, no prompt yet; R29), and chalk drawings
   above them (canvas decals: two sails, three sails, one sail). Its interior footprint is `W.dark` (Lantern zone). ≤
   2,000 tris.
7. **Landmark records** `W.landmarks`: `mills` (0, −8, top 57), `barn` (160, −20, top 24), `apple` (−40, −140, top 26),
   `marshlamp` (−104, 136, top 15). Sightline tests: jetty → Middle Mill top; (110, −120) → the Apple's top; (−50, 60)
   → the Marsh Lamp's top; (60, 40) → the mills.
8. Material programs added: chalk plaster, cupola glass, iron (total 8 ≤ 12). Refresh prints.

#### Assets

Code-built only. ART BRIEFs pasted verbatim from atlas-isles (`threemills_mill`, `threemills_granary`,
`threemills_barn`, `threemills_grandmother_apple`, `threemills_marsh_lamp`, `threemills_sluice_house`) with commented-out
GLB slots and heights (22, 15.5, 19, 18, 15, 6).

#### Acceptance criteria

- The Middle Mill stair is walkable from the door to the gallery (BFS over decks); the gallery is at h 49 ± 0.3; Thrift's
  gallery is unreachable on foot.
- The cellar door is blocked by the hay heap; the portal is sealed (`tm:door`).
- The four sightlines hold; landmark fog cap ≤ 0.45.
- Budgets at the four views in both cameras; ≤ 24 types; ≤ 12 material programs.
- Hidden; prints refreshed; home untouched.
- Invariants: frozen home island, frozen realms, hidden until whole, budgets, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/realm-threemills.test.js` (stair reachability, galleries, sightlines, sealed portal, prints).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (both cameras; the widest view from the gallery).

#### Out of scope

Sails turning and wheat going gold (S-148b); the hoist and sluice interactions (S-148a); textures (S-140).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the inside stair is a narrow FP space; keep the tower's inner radius ≥ 2.4 m and the step decks 1.0 m wide.
Rollback: revert; hidden.
