### S-140 · art: Threemills look: terrain layers, cards, loading card, map art and music

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-138, S-029b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.threemills.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/art-audio.md#threemills](../bible/art-audio.md#threemills), [../bible/art-audio.md#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data),
[../bible/art-audio.md#ambience](../bible/art-audio.md#ambience), [../bible/art-audio.md#bundles](../bible/art-audio.md#bundles),
[../bible/art-audio.md#loading-cards](../bible/art-audio.md#loading-cards)

#### Why

Threemills' painted look and its sound: a patient accordion air while the mills are still, a jig when they turn.

#### What

1. **Terrain** (kind `tile`, 1024² → `_hd` 1024² + `_lo` 512² webp q 82, `python3 -I tools/make_seamless.py <in> <out>
   --blend 64`): `threemills_pasture`, `threemills_orchard`, `threemills_wheat`, `threemills_pondsand`,
   `threemills_chalk`, `threemills_furrow`, `threemills_flint`, subjects verbatim from art-audio#threemills (each ends
   with its "Palette: …"). The cliff slot stays home `cliff_hd` (no new file). hd ≤ 110 KB, lo ≤ 40 KB.
2. **Cards** (kind `card`, white keyed): `threemills_card_ground` (sheet-items N = 4, 1024 × 512: `poppy`,
   `wheatstalk`, `cornflower`, `cowparsley`; `tools/slice_sheet.py`), `threemills_card_chaff` (256² 4-frame strip,
   `tools/process_strip.py`), `threemills_card_hedge` (1024 × 256 alpha), `threemills_card_apples` (512² alpha),
   `threemills_card_reeds` (512² alpha). Each ≤ 90 KB (the bundle row's 5 × 90).
3. **Loading** `load_threemills` (1600 × 900 + 960 × 540 `_lo`, ≤ 200 KB) and **map** `map_threemills` (1024², ≤ 180
   KB), subjects verbatim. The sailing card for Threemills is `load_threemills`.
4. **Music** (`js/realms/threemills.js`, the realm module): register `threemills` from art-audio#track-data (D, 6/8,
   112 bpm; `lead2: 'fiddle'`; `layers: { lead2: 1 }`, `startLayer: 0`) and on entry `G.audio.layer(S.flags
   ['beacon:threemills'] ? 1 : 0)`; dim = the fiddle and the mill creak off (art-audio "dim land" row). Register the
   dungeon variant `granary` (`from: 'depths'`, per track-data) and `warden_honksworth` (`{ from: 'warden', voices: {
   lead: 'fiddle' } }`) if not present.
5. **Ambience** `threemills_farm` (Haybury, Tumble Hill, Orchard Rows) and `threemills_marsh` (Millpond Marsh) with the
   exact data rows of art-audio#ambience (`dim: { ambMill: 0 }` while `!flag:beacon:threemills`).
6. **Bundle** `threemills` ≤ 2.5 MB (art-audio's row totals ≈ 2,490 KB: terrain 1,050, cards 450, portraits 5 × 55,
   beast cards 4 × 35, loading 260, map 180, sketches 3 × 45). This step's share must leave ≥ 690 KB for S-141, S-142,
   S-144 and S-147 (portraits, cards, sketches); the PR prints the bundle total from `node tools/build-pages.mjs`.
7. Every prompt and command appended to `docs/art/prompts.md`.

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Processing | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| 7 terrain keys | tile · 1024 + 512 webp | make_seamless, webp q 82 | home layer tinted `#7fae4b #e7c45a #c2563f #a7d3f0` |
| 5 cards | card · as listed | white→alpha, slice / strip, webp | tinted quads |
| `load_threemills` | loading · 1600 × 900 + 960 × 540 | webp q 80 | loading colour + region text |
| `map_threemills` | map · 1024² | webp q 80 | `buildMapCanvas(W)` |

#### Acceptance criteria

- Every key in `assets/manifest.js` with `realm: 'threemills'` within its size limit (`tests/assets.test.js`);
  `?noassets=all` renders Threemills; the track validates (bars sum, chord types, voices); dim at layer 0.
- Bundle ≤ 2.5 MB with ≥ 690 KB left; core precache change ≤ 20 KB; `bun run test:pwa` passes.
- Invariants: hidden until whole, precache, assets, IP, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (assets test, track validator); `node tools/build-pages.mjs`; `bun run test:pwa`; `node tests/inpage.mjs
  realm`; four view screenshots saved to `test-results/` and linked in the PR body.

#### Out of scope

Portraits (S-141, S-147), bestiary cards (S-142, S-147), sketches (S-144).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the bundle is 10 KB under its cap at full plan; generate at the listed sizes and drop quality to 75 where a file is
over its line. Rollback: revert; fallbacks remain.

<a id="s-141"></a>

### S-141 · feat: Haybury's people: the Hayburys, Goody Gander and Pennywhistle

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-139b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.threemills.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/cast.md#the-hayburys](../bible/cast.md#the-hayburys), [../bible/cast.md#goody-gander](../bible/cast.md#goody-gander),
[../bible/cast.md#pennywhistle](../bible/cast.md#pennywhistle), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-poi](../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-poi),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-interiors](../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-interiors),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-chain](../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-chain), [../bible/story.md#ch9-11](../bible/story.md#ch9-11)

#### Why

Threemills' people and the start of its chain: a field-mouse family rationing pies, a patient goose miller, and a
scarecrow who woke a century ago and wants a crow friend. The `mq-threemills` quest is written as data.

#### What

1. **Look records** (`rodent` rig from S-099, `bird` rig from S-068, one one-off case):

   | id | Rig · h | Palette and accessories | Spot (atlas wins, R21) |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `haybury_poppy` (Ma) | rodent · 0.75 | fur `#c9a27a`, ears `#f4b3b0` round large, gingham apron `#c2563f`/`#ffffff`, headscarf `#e7c45a`, pie dish `extra` | `threemills.pie_counter` (148, 14) |
   | `haybury_barley` (Pa) | rodent · 0.75 | blue dungarees `#5d7fa8`, straw hat `#e7c45a`, pitchfork `extra`, wheat stalk | `threemills.kitchen_garden` (130, −10) |
   | `haybury_oat`, `_rye`, `_millet`, `_spelt`, `_bran`, `_wheatley` (kits) | rodent · 0.35-0.45 | smocks `#7fae4b`, `#a7d3f0`, `#e7c45a`, `#f59aa8`, `#c9b6ec`, `#ff8f7a` | six spots round the Festival Green (140, 30), r 6, 60° apart |
   | `gander` (Goody Gander) | bird `beak: 'flat'` · 1.4 | grey-brown `#a59d92`, white face band `#f4f1ea`, bill `#f29b38`, flour apron `#f3ead2`, green cap `#7fae4b`, flour sack `#e2c9a0` | `threemills.middle_door` (0, −2); until `tm:step1` at the Pie Shop queue (152, 16) |
   | `pennywhistle` | one-off scarecrow · 1.8 | burlap face `#d9c193` (canvas weave), stitched smile `#5a4a3a` (`torus` arc), button eyes `#3a2a20`, straw `#e7c45a` (cone tufts), coat `#7fae4b` with patches `#c2563f` / `#a7d3f0`, floppy hat `#8a6a4a`, a brass button heart `cyl` `#f2b45a` glowing `#ffcf7a` (`glow: 0.6`) | `threemills.pennywhistle_field` (100, 60): on his pole by day, steps down when talked to |

   Pennywhistle is never shot in dark or eerie light: by night (21:00-05:00) he dozes sitting at the pole's foot with
   his button glowing softly. Sound cues: the Hayburys' cart creak and chatter in town; Pennywhistle's three-note
   whistle every 30 s within 20 m with straw wisps blowing off him; Gander's flour-sack thump.
2. **Dialogue** (`js/content/dialogue/threemills.js`, text `js/text/en/threemills.js`, ids `threemills.npc.<id>.*`):
   - **Chain step 1** (`when: ['!flag:tm:step1']`, priority 80, on Ma Poppy or Gander): Ma 1 "Mind the pies. One slice
     each, and no looking sad at the window. The mills stopped and the wheat won't turn gold, so the pies are rationed."
     · Ma 2 "The sun-lamp on the Granary went out and the sails stopped the same night. Her Honkship hasn't come down off
     the hill in days. Flaps the sails by hand, she does. At night!" · Gander 3 "There, there, Poppy. ...The Granary
     door's buried in hay. The hoist can lift it, but the hoist runs off the Old Sluice, and the sluice is stuck shut." ·
     Gander 4 "And the Hoist House key went off with the vole gang, up in Orchard Rows. There, there. They don't mean
     anything by it. They just like shiny things." → `set:tm:step1`, `met:haybury_poppy`, `met:gander`.
   - Ma Poppy after (priority 50): before the beacon "Mind the pies. Off you go, little one. Key first, sluice second, or
     sluice first. I'm a pie person, not a sluice person." · after `beacon:threemills` "Mind the pies! There are SO MANY
     pies. Take one. Take two."
   - Pa Barley (30): before "Right as rain. Well. Right as no rain. The wheat wants the sun-lamp, not rain." · after
     "Right as rain! Gold as butter! Look at it!"
   - The kits (30, one box for all six): before "(six tiny giggles, one after another, each a little higher)" · after
     "(six tiny cheers, and one hiccup)".
   - Gander at Middle Mill (50): before "There, there. The sails will turn again. They always have. ...They always have,
     haven't they?" · after "There, there, sister. ...She's asleep on her feet. Let her be."
   - Pennywhistle (40): before "Fwee-fwoo-fwee. Good morning! Pardon my straw. I'd wave, but the crows might see. Not
     that there are any crows. There never are." · after "Fwee-fwoo-fwee! The wheat's gold! I've guarded it for a
     hundred years and it has never looked so pleased with itself."
   - Reserve markers in the charter: "Fwee-fwoo-fwee." (pennywhistle), "There, there." (gander), "Mind the pies."
     (haybury_poppy), "Right as rain." (haybury_barley).
3. **Haybury doors**: the six knock lines (before / after `beacon:threemills`) verbatim from
   [../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-interiors](../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-interiors) on the cottages at
   (156, 30), (168, 4), (138, −8), (174, 40), (126, 52), (180, −36) (ids `threemills.door.<n>.0/1`); the other two
   cottages are decor (no prompt).
4. **Quest data** `mq-threemills` (`js/content/quests/threemills.js`; `start: 'realm:threemills.visited'` until
   S-148b): `n: 11`, title "Three Mills, One Hill", owner `haybury_poppy`, steps `arrive` (s0 "Sail to Threemills") ·
   `people` (s1 "Find out why the mills stopped", `flag:tm:step1`) · `key_sluice` (s2 "Win back the Hoist Key and open
   the Old Sluice ({n}/2)", parallel `['flag:tm:key', 'flag:tm:sluice']`) · `hoist` (s3 "Lift the hay off the Granary
   door", `flag:tm:door`) · `depths` (s4 "Explore the Granary Vaults", `item:hook`) · `warden` (s5 "Calm the Warden of
   Tumble Hill", `boss:granary_vaults:honksworth`) · `kindle` (s6 "Kindle the Granary's sun-lamp",
   `flag:beacon:threemills`). Journal open (Hoot): "Threemills has three mills and no wind in them. Ma Haybury blames a
   lamp. I blame nobody. It is more restful." Done: "The sails turn. The wheat is gold. There were pies. I am not able
   to say how many." Short labels ≤ 32 characters. `draft: true` until S-148a adds hints.
5. **Flags**: `tm:step1`, `tm:key`, `tm:sluice`, `tm:door` (story), `met:*` for the new NPCs.
6. **Portraits** (realm `threemills`, ≤ 55 KB each): `portrait_haybury_poppy`, `portrait_haybury_barley`,
   `portrait_gander`, `portrait_pennywhistle` (cast.md prompts verbatim; Pennywhistle's in bright daylight).

#### Assets

Four portraits (generated). Code-built NPCs. ART BRIEFs `npc_haybury`, `npc_gander`, `npc_pennywhistle` (cast.md)
pasted in the PR body; manifest slots commented out (0.75, 1.4, 1.8).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Thirteen people stand on walkable cells at their spots (both tides); none within 2.5 m of a door.
- Step-1 dialogue sets `tm:step1` and the `met:` flags; every other line shows by its condition.
- Pennywhistle reads as a friendly daytime scarecrow (button glow, no dark lighting); the Scarecrowlet (S-142) is visibly
  a different shape (half his height, rounder).
- POI count with people ≥ 128 once S-144's finds are in (asserted in S-144); content lint (markers by speaker).
- Portraits ≤ 55 KB; Threemills bundle still ≤ 2.5 MB.
- Prints refreshed (NPC spots are realm data); hidden.
- Invariants: frozen realms, hidden until whole, tone, assets, IP, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (looks, quest lint, content lint, spots both tides).
- In-page people suite (S-120's): talk to Ma Poppy, assert four boxes and flags; knock on each door.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

The vole den, sluice and hoist (S-148a); the Harvest Festival (S-152).

Risk: six kits at the Green add draw calls; they share one instanced kit mesh with six tints. Rollback: revert; hidden.

### S-142 · feat: Threemills enemies: Field Vole gang, Scarecrowlet and Woolly Ram

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-042, S-139b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.threemills.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/creatures.md#enemy-field-vole](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-field-vole),
[../bible/creatures.md#enemy-scarecrowlet](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-scarecrowlet),
[../bible/creatures.md#enemy-woolly-ram](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-woolly-ram), [../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables](../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables)

#### Why

Threemills' species: a swarm, a turret and a charger. The Woolly Ram teaches the land's geometry: hedges stop a charge.

#### What

1. **Species data** `js/realms/threemills/enemies.js` (S-121's shape):

   | id | name | archetype | hp | r | emerge | gems | poof | params |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `field_vole` | Field Vole gang | `swarm` | 4 × 1 | 0.22 (member) | `bush` | 1 (per group) | `0xecd9b8` | `{ members: 4, orbitR: 2.5, dartWind: 0.60, dartSpeed: 6, dartT: 0.4, memberHp: 1, flying: false, tier: 1 }` |
   | `scarecrowlet` | Scarecrowlet | `turret` | 4 | 0.40 | `still` | 1 | `0xe7c45a` | `{ turn: 90, aim: 0.80, lock: 0.30, shot: 10, burst: 1, reload: 1.2, range: 13, tier: 1 }` |
   | `woolly_ram` | Woolly Ram | `charger` | 5 | 0.52 | `ground` | 1 | `0xf4eee0` | `{ windup: 0.70, speed: 7.5, dur: 0.9, reach: 7.0, guard: false, bonkOpen: 1.4, tier: 1 }` |

   Lore / tip verbatim from creatures.md: voles "The Field Vole gang has a leader. I do not know which one. Neither do
   they." / "Only the shiny one is about to jump. Hit that one!"; Scarecrowlet "Scarecrowlets are scarecrows still in
   school." / "It points before it throws. Then it has to reload."; Ram "The Woolly Ram is mostly wool. Inside, there is
   a bit more wool." / "Let it run into a hedge. It gets stuck!". The darting vole stands up, squeaks and shines 0.60 s
   (rim flash `#fff3a0`). The Scarecrowlet points one stick arm while its 0.80 s stripe draws and throws a seed puff.
   The Ram paws, head down, wool puffing 0.70 s (stripe locks 0.25 s before); **hedges, walls and fences** stop a
   charge: hedge colliders carry `bonk: true`, and a ram stopped by one is snagged 1.4 s (gold).
2. **Rigs** (`enemyDef`, ≤ 1,200 tris, ≤ 6 materials): voles: four 0.25 m capsule-mice `#a8805a` with cream bellies
   `#ecd9b8`, pink round ears `#e8a0a0`, a wheat stalk `#e7c45a` in each mouth (one instanced rig, 4 members); the
   Scarecrowlet: sack head `#d8c08a` with button eyes, red felt hat `#c2563f`, straw tufts, a green patch `#7fae4b`, on a
   post (1.1 m total; half Pennywhistle's height and rounder); the Ram: a cloud of white wool spheres `#f4eee0`, dark
   round face `#5a4a44`, curled horns (`torus` segments `#c9a87a`), four short legs `#3b3540`.
3. **Spawn tables** (creatures.md is the definition): `tumblehill [['woolly_ram', 3], ['field_vole', 2]], max 5`;
   `orchard [['field_vole', 3]], max 5`; `millpond [['field_vole', 3], ['woolly_ram', 2]], max 5`; `haybury [] max 0`;
   dark zones (Cider Cave, Sluice cellar) `[['dimling', 1]], max 2`. Rams spawn only on fields and downs (not marsh
   cells, not boardwalks).
4. **Placed Scarecrowlets** via `placeIsleTurrets` (`new RNG(40237 * 71 + 25)`), nearest valid cell within 8 m (valid as
   S-121, plus "not inside a wheat field's inner 3 m"): Orchard Rows (6) (20, −150), (60, −130), (−20, −130), (−100, −80),
   (−60, −160), (100, −100); Tumble Hill (4) (−45, −55), (55, −45), (−65, 35), (25, −70).
5. **Cards** `card_field_vole`, `card_scarecrowlet`, `card_woolly_ram` (384², ≤ 35 KB to fit the bundle row, realm
   `threemills`), descriptions verbatim from creatures.md in the card pattern.
6. Wind-up sounds with a pitch rise: vole squeak (`T({f: 1800, f1: 2600, d: 0.3, v: 0.05})`), scarecrow creak, ram
   paw-and-bleat (`T({f: 260, f1: 340, d: 0.7, v: 0.1, vib: [6, 20]})`).

#### Assets

Code rigs with ART BRIEFs from creatures.md (manifest slots commented out: 0.25, 1.1, 1.0); three generated cards.

#### Acceptance criteria

- Each species: telegraph ≥ 0.55 s before damage (vole 0.60, Scarecrowlet 0.80, Ram 0.70), 1 HP per hit, poof and
  bestiary count; the swarm holds one token and counts one poof per group.
- A ram charging into a hedge stops at it and shows the 1.4 s gold snag; a ram never charges through a hedge.
- 10 placed Scarecrowlets at the anchors; no spawn in Haybury or within 12 m of it; no rolled spawn on water.
- Bundle ≤ 2.5 MB; prints refreshed; hidden.
- Invariants: frozen realms, hidden until whole, budgets, assets, tone, IP, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (species params in range; spawn tables equal creatures.md; turret anchors).
- In-page species tests (S-121's suite): spawn each on Threemills, measure lead, poof; ram into a hedge.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

The vole den waves (S-148a); dungeon waves (S-145, S-146).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: hedge bonks need the hedge collider tag; tagged in this PR on `W.hedges` colliders only. Rollback: revert; hidden.

### S-143a · feat: the Thistle Hook grapples to hookblooms; three isle finds reopen

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-048, S-138, S-123, S-037b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none (the tool is found in
`granary_vaults`, S-146; tests grant it) · Bible: [../bible/systems.md#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools),
[../bible/systems.md#tool-hook](../bible/systems.md#tool-hook), [../bible/systems.md#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates),
[../bible/systems.md#brief-tools](../bible/systems.md#brief-tools), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#isle-totals](../bible/atlas-isles.md#isle-totals)

#### Why

Tool 4 of 10: aim at a giant purple thistle and be pulled to it. Half of INDEX S-143 (R30); the home hook finds are
S-143b. Pacing rule 5: the Hook reopens Duskwick chest-6, Pinewhistle chest-11 and Archwater chest-10.

#### What

1. **Item and ability**: `ITEMS.hook = { name: 'Thistle Hook', kind: 'tool', icon: 'icons_tools:hook', desc: 'Aim at a
   big purple hookbloom and press {tool}. Whoosh!', stack: 1, beat: true }`; `ABILITIES.hook`. Tool ring slot 3.
   Item-get line: "You got the Thistle Hook! Aim at a big purple hookbloom and press {tool}. Whoosh!"
2. **Hookbloom gate** (`js/gates.js`; extend the look-only type S-123 / S-086 / S-101 registered): record `{ type:
   'hookbloom', id, x, z, y (head height), pad: { x, z, h, w: 1.2, d: 1.2 } }`; the pad is a bridge deck under the head
   where Pip lands facing the bloom. Look `gate_hookbloom` (code-built: stalk `cyl` `#5f8f4a` 4-8 m, a 1.2 m spiky head
   `#8c5ad8` with tips `#c9a0ff`, a small plank pad `#9b7a52`; sways 2°; ≤ 700 tris).
3. **Targeting** (`js/hook.js`): a bloom is a target when (a) Pip owns `hook`, (b) the bloom's head is within **14 m** of
   Pip's eye, (c) within **25°** of the view centre (FP: the crosshair; TP: the camera's centre ray), (d) a clear line of
   sight: a heightfield ray plus a collider segment test from the eye to the head is free (the bloom's own stalk
   excluded). The nearest valid bloom to the view centre wins. The target shows a **lilac ring** (`#c9a0ff` additive
   ring sprite) and the pill `[T] Hook · Hookbloom`; entering range plays a bright chime (`T({f: 1568, d: 0.15, v:
   0.06})`, once per target acquisition: the FP cue for a bloom out of view is the chime from its position).
4. **The pull**: on the tool tap, `sfx.hookFire`, the vine line (a 1-draw tube from the held hook to the head, `#5f8f4a`)
   shoots in 0.12 s, `sfx.hookAttach`, then Pip is pulled along the straight segment eye → pad at **14 m/s** through
   a generic player data hook `player.mods.pull = { to: [x, y, z], speed: 14, done }` (add `pull` to the S-048 mod set:
   gravity off, input ignored, collisions as a capsule sweep; if the sweep is blocked mid-pull, stop and drop: no damage),
   reel ticks at 14 Hz (`sfx.hookReel`); on arrival Pip stands on the pad facing the bloom; cooldown 0.5 s. During a pull
   enemy contact is ignored (no hits taken or given). Swing cancels nothing (input locked for the pull).
5. **Held model** `tool_hook` (code-built: a 0.4 m wooden handle `#9b7a52` topped with a purple thistle head `#8c5ad8` /
   `#c9a0ff` and a coiled green vine `#5f8f4a`; ≤ 500 tris), viewmodel left hand; on the belt in TP; the head flies on
   the line when used.
6. **Pads for the isle blooms already placed** (additive data on existing gate records; positions unchanged):
   - Duskwick `chest-6`: the Gull Stack bloom (hooked from the plateau rim (148, −100)): pad on the stack top beside the
     chest.
   - Pinewhistle `chest-11`: the Lookout Pine platform rim bloom (110, 70) at 20 m: pad = the platform's edge.
   - Archwater `chest-10`: `archwater:hook-1` on the arch's west shoulder (140, −70) head h 22.5 with a pad shelf 1.6 ×
     1.6 m at (141, −70) h 21.6, hooked from the west-leg ledge (132, −73) h 14 (11.7 m, in range); `archwater:hook-2`
     on the keystone (150, −70) head h 27.5 with its pad on the crown deck at (148, −70) h 26, hooked from hook-1's pad
     (10.5 m).
   Each pad is a realm-module deck (like S-127a's drained-pool decks), so prints stay byte-identical.
7. **Solver**: the hook model (14 m line with line of sight, pad landing) opens the three chests; dungeon reach for `h`
   tiles is 7 tiles (dungeons.md#reach) and lands on the tile in front of the bloom.
8. **Icon** `hook` cell in Tools I (kind `icon`: "a purple thistle head `#8a5a9c` on a coil of twine"), packed with
   `tools/pack_atlas.py`.

#### Assets

| Key | Build | Fallback |
|---|---|---|
| `tool_hook` | code-built; brief from systems.md#brief-tools (image "a wooden grappling tool topped with a big purple thistle flower head, a coiled green vine rope, isolated, transparent background"; 3D "low-poly toon grappling hook made of a thistle flower on a wooden handle with vine rope, 500 tris"); slot commented out, height 0.6 | box handle + spiky icosphere |
| `gate_hookbloom` | code-built; brief from systems.md#brief-gates (700 tris) | cylinder + spiky icosphere + disc |
| `icons_tools` cell `hook` | generated, packed | canvas glyph |

#### Acceptance criteria

- With `G.debug.give('hook')`: a bloom 12 m away in the crosshair shows the ring and pill; at 15 m, at 30° off centre or
  behind a wall it does not; the pull takes `distance / 14 + 0.12 s` ± 0.05 and lands Pip on the pad, in both cameras.
- A blocked sweep stops the pull safely (no damage, Pip on walkable ground within 0.5 s).
- Duskwick chest-6, Pinewhistle chest-11 and Archwater chest-10 are reachable with the Hook; the solver report lists
  them under `hook`; prints of the three realms are unchanged.
- Invariants: frozen realms (pads are additive module decks), one save slot, budgets (the line is one draw), tone,
  assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/hook.test.js` (new, pure: target picking by range, cone and LOS on a synthetic heightfield; pull timing).
- `bun test` (solver, prints of duskwick, pinewhistle and archwater unchanged).
- In-page tools suite: give the hook, warp to Archwater's west-leg ledge, aim at hook-1 (set the camera yaw/pitch), tap,
  assert arrival on the pad; then hook-2 to the crown deck; repeat in TP.

#### Out of scope

Home hook finds (S-143b); dungeon hookblooms (S-146); Threemills' blooms (S-144).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the capsule sweep must not tunnel through thin arch geometry; the arch legs are the only colliders and the line of
sight test runs first. Rollback: revert; the blooms fall back to look-only.

<a id="s-143b"></a>

### S-143b · feat: three home hook finds: the Stairfoot route, the Cap Ledge and the Gull's Nest

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-143a, S-102 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-home.md#hook-finds](../bible/atlas-home.md#hook-finds), [../bible/atlas-home.md#passes](../bible/atlas-home.md#passes),
[../bible/atlas-home.md#starstair](../bible/atlas-home.md#starstair)

#### Why

Pacing rule 5 for the Hook at home: the Cairn's needle gets a route to an old stone platform (the Starstair's foot, inert
until chapter 18), Grumble's Crown gets a ledge with the best view of the Woods, and a gull's stash sits on the
lighthouse roof. Second half of INDEX S-143 (R30).

#### What

1. **Post-pass `placeHookFinds`** (atlas-home row 14, `new RNG(7151 * 127 + 61)`, guarded, after row 13), writing only
   `W.hookFinds`, with solids and decks through `attachPost(W)`:
   - `home:stairfoot-route`: **the Stairfoot** (a 4 × 3 m Lamplighter stone platform on the summit's east side, centre
     4.4 m from the Cairn's centre (100, −48) on bearing 60°, deck top = summit h + 0.1 (seed 7151 ≈ Y 23.4), a broken
     stair stub of three steps rising toward bearing 48°, a spiral carved in the floor, `glow` 0.2); **the First Ledge**
     (a 2.5 × 2.0 m deck on the needle's south-east face, ≤ 12 m line with sight from H2); hookbloom **H1** beside the
     First Ledge, ≤ 13 m line from a reachable Peaks plateau cell (seed 7151 ≈ (110, −32), Y 13); hookbloom **H2** on the
     Stairfoot's rim. A 20-gem pouch on the First Ledge (`S.chests['home:hook1']`). The Stairfoot is inert: `[E] Look ·
     Old steps` "Old stone steps climb three steps into the air and stop. Someone was going somewhere."
   - `home:cap-ledge`: on Grumble's Crown (10, −131): hookbloom **C1** on a shelf fungus half-way up the stem; **the Stem
     Shelf** deck 2.2 × 1.6 m at Y = base + 0.45 H (≈ 11.5) on the stem's south-west side, with C1's ground hook point
     (≤ 12 m line) outside the arena's 5 m ring (elevated decks are exempt from the ring rule); hookbloom **C2** on the
     cap's rim; **the Cap Ledge**: a 2.4 × 1.8 m moss deck on the cap rim's top at r = cap radius − 0.6 m, same bearing,
     Y = base + 0.72 H, ≤ 10 m line from the Stem Shelf with sight past the rim. It holds the anchor of Sleeping Acorn
     `home:a03` (drawn by S-161) and is a free view point.
   - `home:gulls-nest`: hookbloom **G1** on the lamp-room roof's shoulder, ≤ 6 m line from Pinch's Lookout (S-102's
     gallery deck); **the Gull's Nest**: a woven nest deck 1.6 × 1.6 m on the coral cone's south-west shoulder, Y ≈ 16.2;
     a 25-gem pouch (`S.chests['home:hook2']`) with the line "Shiny things. A gull's whole collection. The gull looks
     proud."
2. **Solver**: the three decks are unreachable on foot and reachable with the hook model (rule 9d of atlas-home 1.1);
   the report lists `hook → home: stairfoot (20 gems), cap-ledge (view, a03 later), gulls-nest (25 gems)`.
3. **Completion**: the two pouches count in home's chests row.

#### Assets

Code-built: `stairfoot` (pale stone `#cfc8bc` / `#a8a096`, spiral inlay `#8fd8ff`, frost `#f6faff`, ≤ 900 tris), the
ledge decks (rock `ico` slabs), the nest (`torus` of twig `cyl`s `#9b7a52`), the shelf fungus (`lathe` `#e8d6b8`).
ART BRIEF `stairfoot` pasted verbatim from atlas-home with a commented-out slot (height 1.6).

#### Acceptance criteria

- `world-prints.json` byte-identical; `placeHookFinds: false` vs true equal on prints, walk, reach, unreachable for
  seeds 1-40; on 7151 the decks are unreachable on foot and reachable by the hook model; each hook line ≤ its stated
  length with sight.
- The pouches pay once; the Stairfoot Look line shows; nothing else on home moves.
- In-page: from the plateau cell, H1 → First Ledge → H2 → Stairfoot; C1 → Stem Shelf → C2 → Cap Ledge; Lookout → G1 →
  nest, in both cameras.
- Invariants: frozen home island, one save slot, budgets, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/world.test.js` (SLOW group) and `bun test` (solver, completion).
- In-page tools suite with `G.debug.give('hook')` on home, scripted aims.
- `bun run test:playthrough`.

#### Out of scope

The ivy route and the climb portal (S-264); the flights (S-212); acorn a03 (S-161).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: the First Ledge must not be reachable by a hop from the plateau; the pass proves on-foot unreachability. Rollback:
revert; append-only pass.
