   cart` "A hay cart has rolled into the hedge gap. Something glassy glints beyond it.") that S-233 replaces with
   thornglass; c3 lies under a flat carved stone `threemills:block-star` in the middle of the Ring Stones (`kit_ruin_ring`)
   (`[E] Look · Carved stone` "A flat stone carved with a moon lies over something. It feels like it's waiting for
   night.") that S-208 turns into a star door.
4. **POI count**: assert ≥ 128 with S-141's 13 people (finds 50 + sites ≈ 43 + curios 22 + gates 4 + quest props 2 +
   people 13); print both numbers (with and without people) in the PR body.
5. **Solver and pacing rule 5**: the Tide Bell now opens Threemills c11, a10 and (with low water) c10, completing the
   Tide Bell's three isle reopenings; the Hook opens c8, c9; the Glider opens c10; Lantern c6, c7.
6. **Sketches** `sketch_threemills_1..3` (kind `sketch`, 512 × 288 ≤ 45 KB, realm `threemills`): v1 "the view from a
   windmill's gallery over a patchwork of small fields, hedges, an orchard and a reedy pond, two other windmills nearby";
   v2 "rows of apple trees on a gentle ridge, one huge old apple tree among them"; v3 "an old iron lamp tower on stilts in
   a reedy millpond at dusk, a boardwalk leading to it".
7. **Completion wiring**: the `threemills` land table (as S-123).

**Prize chests** (CANON §10.4; rows already in `js/content/prizes.js`, opened by S-086a's chest path): this land's
three prize chests give their item instead of 30 gems: `c5` Haybury Rocking Chair, `c9` Poppy Red trim, `c8` "Three Mills" sticker. Add one unit test that each listed chest resolves to its
prize, and keep `STATS` chest counts unchanged.

#### Assets

`sketch_threemills_1..3` (generated). Kit reuse; blockers code-built (cart from S-139a, carved stone slab with a canvas
moon decal).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Counts 14 / 4 / 10 / 3 / 1 / 2 / 3 / 6 / 1; anchors within radius; per-state reach as the gate table says.
- Coverage passes in both states with fill ≤ 12 %; POI ≥ 128.
- The solver passes; the report lists Threemills finds by tool.
- Bundle ≤ 2.5 MB; prints refreshed; hidden.
- Invariants: frozen home island, frozen realms, hidden until whole, budgets, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/realm-threemills.test.js tests/realm-coverage.test.js`; `bun test`.
- In-page finds check: open c1, read Lamp Log 8 at the Stone Stile, ring `threemills:tide-1` and walk to c11, hook to c8.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

The vole den and sluice behaviour (S-148a); Hollows (S-149); star doors (S-208); thornglass (S-233); digging (S-181).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: farmland fill; the field-feature threshold is the knob. Rollback: revert; hidden.

<a id="s-145"></a>

### S-145 · feat: the Granary Vaults rooms 1-6: hay-bale blocks and gear bridges

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-143a, S-053, S-142 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.granary_vaults.hidden: true` ·
Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#d4](../bible/dungeons.md#d4), [../bible/dungeons.md#entities](../bible/dungeons.md#entities),
[../bible/dungeons.md#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules)

#### Why

Dungeon 4, first half: hay bales that fill pits for good, gear-bridges turned by cranks and ridden on their hub wheels.

#### What

1. **Data** `js/realms/dungeons/granary_vaults.js` (R1; music variant `granary`): `id: 'granary_vaults', name: 'The
   Granary Vaults', kind: 'dungeon', tile: 2, palette: 'granary', music: 'granary', light: { ambient: 0.6, torches: true,
   sun: false, tod: 0.5 }, parent: 'threemills', door: 'granary'`; composite map `granary L1` verbatim and whole; rooms
   r1-r12 boxes; this step fills r1-r6. Door (5,16) r6 → r7 `cond: 'sealed'` (S-124's condition).
2. **Hay bales** (every `b` in d4): push like a block; pushed onto a `_` tile it drops in (0.4 s, `sfx.landHeavy`, a
   straw puff) and **fills it**: that tile becomes floor at 0 for good (`S.dungeons.granary_vaults.open['fill:c,r']`),
   the bale is used up. A reset lever `r` lifts every bale of its room back to its start, including filled ones (the
   pits reopen; 0.6 s fade). Skin `int_bale` (1.9 m rounded hay cube `#e7c45a` with two twine bands `#7a5232`).
3. **Gear-bridges** `G` with `len` arms: the pit tiles round the hub hold arms pointing `NS` or `EW`; arm tiles are a
   bridge deck in the current state and a pit otherwise; turning takes 1.0 s (`sfx.gearCrank`), blocked while Pip
   stands on an **arm**. A crank `y` (`[E] Use · Crank` or a sword hit) turns its listed hub 90°. The hub carries a
   wheel: standing on `G`, `[E] Use · Wheel` turns the bridge **with Pip riding** (Pip rotates with the hub; camera yaw
   follows). Skins `int_gear_bridge` (oak hub 1.8 m with a 16-tooth iron rim, plank arms 2 m wide), `int_crank`.
   Hubs: `r3:G1` (25,4) len 2 starts `NS`, crank `r3:y1` (22,1); `r6:G1` (5,13) len 1 starts `EW`, crank `r6:y1` (8,10);
   `r10:G1` is S-146's.
4. **Rooms r1-r6** exactly as dungeons.md: r1 The Barn Door (`X` (0,4) → Threemills spawn `granary` (0, 44), R6; sign
   "Granary Vaults. Bales go where you push them. Mind the gears."; crates and a barrel); r2 The Bale Room (bale `r2:b1`
   (13,3) E ×4 onto hold plate `r2:P1` (17,3) holds door (20,4) open; map `&` (15,1); lever `r2:r1` (15,7)); r3 The Gear
   Hall (ceiling 7; crank → `EW`, walk out to the hub, wheel to `NS`, north to gem chest `r3:c1` (25,1), south to the
   landing (25,7), door (25,8) `always`); r4 The Vole Hall (wave Field Vole gang ×5 as one swarm of 5 members at (23,10),
   (27,10), (22,13), (28,13), (25,14); `clear` → small key `r4:k1` (25,12); door (20,12) west `always`); r5 The
   Millstone Room (bales `r5:b1` (13,11) S ×2 fills (13,13); `r5:b2` (17,11) W ×4 then S ×3 fills (13,14); Chime Stone `%`
   (15,15); lever `r5:r1` (19,9); locked door (10,12) uses `r4:k1`); r6 The Flour Loft (wave Woolly Ram ×2 at (3,10),
   (7,11): charges stop at walls (bonk); crank → `NS`, walk the arm to the hub, wheel to `EW`, gem chest `r6:c2` (3,13)
   from the west arm, wheel back to `NS`, door (5,16) sealed this step).
5. **Realm** `granary_vaults` (`kind: 'dungeon'`, parent `threemills`, size 98, hidden, bundle `threemills`, tod 0.5,
   `needsOnEntry: ['lantern']`); name card "The Granary Vaults" / "under Tumble Hill"; the Threemills portal (S-139b)
   targets it, still needing `tm:door`. Dust-shaft cards `card_dust_shaft` in r3 and r5.
6. **Solver**: state adds bale fills and gear states; replay the notes r1 → r6 to the sealed door; every chest/key of
   r1-r6 reachable; no soft-lock (levers cover every bale).
7. **Hints** 1 "Bridges on gears like to be turned from the middle, too." / "Use the crank to swing the bridge round,
   walk to the middle, then use the wheel there. The bridge turns with you on it." · 2 "Hay is heavier than it looks.
   Pits are shallower than they look." / "Push both bales into the same column of the pit in the Millstone Room. Two
   bales make a path across."
8. Prints `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/granary_vaults.json`.

#### Assets

| Key | Build / generate | Fallback |
|---|---|---|
| `tex_granary` (`realm: threemills`) | tile: "<style line> Seamless texture of an old granary wall, warm terracotta bricks with oak beams and a dusting of flour and straw, tileable"; make_seamless, 1024/512 webp | `tex_dungeon_brick` |
| `card_dust_shaft` | card 256 × 1024 alpha: "a shaft of warm light with floating dust motes" | none |
| `int_bale`, `int_gear_bridge`, `int_crank` | code-built; ART BRIEF `int_bale` from dungeons.md d4 verbatim | kit block tinted; plank deck; lever |

#### Acceptance criteria

- Map equals dungeons.md; compiler and solver pass for r1-r6 with door (5,16) `sealed`.
- A bale fills a pit for good; the lever restores it; the crank will not turn with Pip on an arm; riding the wheel works
  in both cameras.
- State survives leave and re-enter; dungeon budgets ≤ 120 calls / 150 k tris; genMs ≤ 150 ms.
- Hidden.
- Invariants: frozen realms, hidden until whole, one save slot, budgets, tone, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/dungeon-granary.test.js` (new).
- In-page dungeon suite: push r2's bale, crank and ride r3, fill r5's column; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

Rooms 7-12 and the Hook chest (S-146); Lady Honksworth (S-147).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: riding a rotating deck: rotate Pip's position about the hub each frame and clamp to the arm tile. Rollback: revert.

<a id="s-146"></a>

### S-146 · feat: the Granary Vaults rooms 7-12: the Thistle Hook and the way to the Warden

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-145 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.granary_vaults.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/dungeons.md#d4](../bible/dungeons.md#d4), [../bible/systems.md#tool-hook](../bible/systems.md#tool-hook)

#### Why

The second half of dungeon 4: the Thistle Hook is found and used at once over pits, a crank turns a bridge in the next
room through a visible chain, and the mill floor waits for Lady Honksworth.

#### What

1. Unseal (5,16). Fill r7-r12 exactly as dungeons.md:
   - r7 The Hook Store: treasure `$` (3,19) = the **Thistle Hook** (`give:hook`, `ability:hook`, S-143a's item-get);
     a 2-tile pit (cols 6-7); hook `r7:h1` (9,18) from the west floor, land (8,18); door (10,20) `always`.
   - r8 The Sack Hall (ceiling 7): a 6-wide pit with an island; hook `r8:h2` (16,20) from (12,20), land (15,20); small key
     `r8:k1` (15,19); hook `r8:h1` (11,18) to return, land (12,18); gem chest `r8:c3` (19,17); doors east (20,20)
     `always`, south locked (15,24) uses `r8:k1`.
   - r9 The Sail Room (ceiling 7): wave Scarecrowlet ×3 at (23,19), (27,21), (23,22) (placed turrets); a digit-6 loft in
     the NE: hook `r9:h1` (28,17) from below, land (28,18); gem chest `r9:c4` (29,17); boss door `B` (25,24).
   - r10 The Grain Chute (ceiling 7): `r10:G1` (5,28) len 3 starts `NS`; its crank is `r11:y1` (16,28) in the next room;
     an iron chain runs along the shared wall at waist height between them (R7 visible cue), turning when cranked; the
     Root Key chest `K` (1,28) is opened from the arm tile (2,28) in the `EW` state.
   - r11 The Chaff Hall: wave Field Vole gang ×4 (one swarm, 4 members) at (13,26), (17,26), (12,29), (18,29); crank
     `r11:y1`; door (10,28) west `always`.
   - r12 Lady Honksworth's Mill Floor (ceiling 12; arena 15 × 15 tiles, R9): the **east** wall (col 36) is the gear wall
     (`int_gear_wall`: three wooden gears 4, 3 and 2 m across in relief that turn 30°/s for 2 s when hit); boss spawn `M`
     (28,30) holds this step an inert placeholder (a goose-shaped heap of hay that snores: `[E] Look · Snoring hay` "A
     very large heap of hay is snoring. It is wearing a bonnet."); the sun-lamp beacon `!` (28,37) (isle `threemills`,
     kindle needs `boss:granary_vaults:honksworth`); `X` (28,40) → spawn `granary` (R6).
2. **Hookbloom entity** `h` (dungeons.md: a big purple thistle on a 2.4 m post; target within 7 tiles in a straight line
   with nothing taller than 1.2 m between; Pip lands on the tile in front of the bloom facing it), using S-143a's hook.
3. **Ledger**: `r4:k1` → L (10,12); `r8:k1` → L (15,24); `K` → B (25,24); gem chests r3:c1, r6:c2, r8:c3, r9:c4 = 160
   gems; map r2, Chime Stone r5, Hook r7.
4. **Solver** full replay of the notes (the boss step skipped with `bossCalmed` until S-147); every chest.
5. **Hint 3**: "The chain on the wall goes somewhere." / "The crank in the Chaff Hall turns the bridge in the Grain
   Chute next door. Turn it, then walk across to the Root Key."
6. Refresh prints.

#### Assets

`int_hookbloom` (the dungeon skin of `gate_hookbloom`: 2.4 m post), `int_gear_wall`, the wall chain (`tube` links
`#5b5e66`): code-built; briefs from dungeons.md d4.

#### Acceptance criteria

- No `sealed` doors; the full solver passes (boss door, exit, every chest, the Hook); the notes replay.
- Hooking in r7-r9 works in both cameras; the r11 crank turns r10's bridge with the chain moving.
- Budgets per room view (r12 included); hidden; prints refreshed.
- Invariants: frozen realms, hidden until whole, one save slot, budgets, tone, assets, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/dungeon-granary.test.js`.
- In-page dungeon suite: open `$`, hook across r7 and r8, crank r11, open `K`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

Lady Honksworth (S-147); the overworld kindle (S-148b).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: a hook landing tile occupied by a block; d4 has none in r7-r9. Rollback: revert.

<a id="s-146b"></a>

### S-146b · feat: The Granary Vaults grow the Thresher Loft, the Brake Room and the Miller's Cupboard: the gears turn by themselves (depth rule R14)

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-146, S-060b, S-104b (shared annex pieces and the latch) · Size: M (200-500; most of it map data and entity tables) · Save: none · Flag: the dungeon realm stays `hidden: true` · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#d4-depth](../bible/dungeons.md#d4-depth), [../bible/dungeons.md#depth-common](../bible/dungeons.md#depth-common), [../bible/dungeons.md#depth-counts](../bible/dungeons.md#depth-counts), [../bible/dungeons.md#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules), [../bible/dungeons.md#d4](../bible/dungeons.md#d4)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md R14, CANON §11.1 A2; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after
S-146 and before S-147, which now depends on it.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M17 · Threemills; the Tower unlocks**. Every story dungeon had about one simple beat per room and almost no room where two
mechanics meet, so The Granary Vaults played in well under its budget and taught one idea without building on it. Rule R14
asks for ≥ 1.6 beats per counted room, ≥ 4 compound rooms, one mid-dungeon state flip that sends the player back
through rooms already solved, and a last room before the boss that uses everything. This step builds
The Granary Vaults's annex, flip, latch and synthesis from the bible, so the dungeon meets the rule before its boss lands.

#### What
1. **Annex levels.** Copy the map blocks of [dungeons.md#d4-depth](../bible/dungeons.md#d4-depth) verbatim
   into the dungeon's data module (`js/realms/dungeons/<id>.js`, the file its room steps created): `granary LA` (`at` [39,0]): r13 The Thresher Loft, r14 The Brake Room; `granary LB` (`at` [39,10]): r15 The Miller's Cupboard; extent 60 × 41, realm grid 136 m. Room
   table rows, entity rows and the room-by-room conditions exactly as the bible gives them. Annex levels join the §5
   rooms only through stair warps.
2. **Stairs.** r13 `^`(1,7) ↔ r9 (29,18) on the loft (Hook); r15 `^`(1,7) ↔ r6 (2,10), sealed until the flip.
3. **Tile edits to the §5 map** (apply to the bible's map block and to the data in this PR; the map test compares
   against the edited block): r9 (29,18) `6` → `^`; r9 (23,18) `.` → `b` (`r9:b1`); r9 (23,20) `.` → `P` (`r9:P1`); r6 (2,10) `.` → `^`. Update the bible's room table, keys-and-chests ledger and solver notes to
   match, in the same PR.
4. **The flip.** **the Mill Brake** `r14:s1` (19,1), flag `gv.brake`, line `dg.gv.flip` "The brake lets go. Far off, the old gears start to turn by themselves.". From then on: **Q = r8**: hookbloom `r8:h2` rides the sack-line back and forth over the pit from (14,20) to (18,20) at 1 m/s; fire the Hook when it passes in range (a timed hook). **P = r6**: `G1` (5,13) turns by itself, `NS` ↔ `EW` every 4 s; the crank `r6:y1` (8,10) now freezes it for 10 s in its current position; the stair (2,10) unseals. Saved as `S.dungeons[<id>].state.flip = true` (v8
   container, no bump); the dungeon compiler applies the listed entity-state changes when it builds Q and P; the first
   entry into each after the flip plays the 0.4 s result cue (R7).
5. **The latch.** `B` (25,24) opens on `key:K && latch:r15:s1 && clear:r9`. Hitting the latch plays the toast `dg.latch` and a positional clunk at `B`.
6. **Synthesis in the room before the boss** (R14 item 4): r9: push the new bale `r9:b1` (23,18) south ×2 onto the new hold plate `r9:P1` (23,20) to lower the loft's hookbloom into reach; hook up to the loft and its stair; the Scarecrowlet wave is `clear` for `B`.
7. **Ask the Tree** gains two hint pairs for this dungeon (systems#hints): "The Sail Room's loft has a way down to the old threshers." / "Push the new bale onto the square plate in the Sail Room, hook up to the loft and take the stair; at the end, pull the Mill Brake." · "The Flour Loft's gear won't sit still now." /
   "Wait until the Flour Loft's gear-bridge points the way you need, crank to freeze it, cross, and take the stair on the north side." (text ids `dg.granary.hint.4`, `.5`).
8. **Solver and depth test.** The flip is one more state bit in the lock-and-key graph (S-049); the solver proves the
   whole path (§5 rooms → annex → flip → Q and P re-solved → latch → `B`), every chest, and no soft-lock whether the
   player flips early or late. Extend `tests/dungeon-depth.test.js` (S-060b) with this dungeon's row: 15 rooms, 13 counted, ≥ 21 beats (planned 24), ≥ 4 compound rooms (planned 7); the test prints the per-room table
   for the PR body.

#### Assets
- The annex rooms reuse this dungeon's kit, textures and palette (no new generated images; the annex rooms are within
  the realm bundle already budgeted).
- **`int_mill_brake`** (code-built, ≤ 120 tris; the flip prop): a tall wooden brake lever with an iron brake shoe and a straw-wrapped handle. ART BRIEF: silhouette as described;
  size 1.6 m; palette `#8a6a3a` `#5a5a5a` `#e7c45a`; toon materials; 120 / 500 tris (code / painted GLB); pose static, it
  turns or swings once when used; image prompt "<style line> a tall wooden brake lever with an iron brake shoe and a straw-wrapped handle, cosy storybook dungeon prop, three-quarter
  view"; 3D prompt "stylised low-poly a tall wooden brake lever with an iron brake shoe and a straw-wrapped handle, hand-painted texture, game-ready, under 500
  triangles, 1.6 m". Manifest key `int_mill_brake` (commented-out GLB slot). Fallback: the code-built model.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (this dungeon's prints are refreshed in this PR, inside its own build milestone,
  before the land is unhidden), *Hidden until whole*, *One save slot* (no bump), *Budgets* (R11: every annex room view
  ≤ 120 draw calls and ≤ 150 k triangles), *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the annex blocks and the edited §5 block; data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves the full path, every chest, the flip in both orders, and no soft-lock; the depth test row passes
  the thresholds above.
- In page: hitting the flip changes Q and P exactly as listed; `B` stays shut until the latch (or d1's plate) and the
  Root Key; the hints show in Ask the Tree.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/dungeon-depth.test.js` and the
  dungeon's solver test.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `granary_depth`: `G.debug.realm('<id>')`,
  play the intended path from the annex stair with `G.debug.dungeon.solve('<id>', { from: 'annex' })`, assert the flip's
  changes in Q and P, the latch, and `B`).
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (prints refreshed, budgets).

#### Out of scope
- The boss (S-147); any new chest, key or reward (the 48-chest ledger is fixed); the land overworld.

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the flip makes a room unsolvable from the side the player returns by; the solver run in both flip orders is the
guard, and the bible's fallback (§5A.1) adds a block and plate. Rollback: revert the PR; the §5 rooms work alone
(stair warps to a missing annex show as sealed hatches) and the realm stays hidden.

<a id="s-147"></a>

### S-147 · feat: Lady Honksworth, the exhausted goose who flapped the mill sails by hand

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-146, S-045, S-146b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.granary_vaults.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/creatures.md#boss-honksworth](../bible/creatures.md#boss-honksworth), [../bible/creatures.md#boss-def](../bible/creatures.md#boss-def),
[../bible/creatures.md#hp-formula](../bible/creatures.md#hp-formula), [../bible/cast.md#lady-honksworth](../bible/cast.md#lady-honksworth)

#### Why

The Warden of Tumble Hill: a very proud goose who flapped the sails by hand so nobody went hungry, exhausted and
therefore extremely loud. Her own charges open her up when she hits the gear wall; the Hook makes it certain.

#### What

1. **Def** `js/boss/honksworth.js`: `id: 'threemills.honksworth', key: 'granary_vaults:honksworth', name: 'Lady
   Honksworth', title: 'Her Honkship, Warden of Tumble Hill', kind: 'bout', model: 'boss_honksworth', height: 3.2, r:
   1.3, keepOut: 2.0, room: 3.0, talkAt: 2.8, hp: { base: 74, perCalmed: 8, group: 'act2Wardens' }, enrageAt: 0.5, cool:
   [1.6, 2.6]`, arena `{ realm: 'granary_vaults', room: 'r12', gearWall: 'east', seal: 'vines', wallBonk: true }`.
   Phases P1 `honk 35, charge 40, feathers 25`; P2 `honk 25, charge 35, feathers 20, doublehonk 20`. Moves:

   | Move | Tell | Shape | Wind | Active | Opening |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `honk` | rears back, beak open, a ring of light at her feet, a long inhale (`sfx.honkRing`) | ring 7 m/s to r 12, height 0.3 (hop it) | 0.7 | ≈ 1.7 | - |
   | `charge` | lowers her neck, wings out; the stripe locks 0.4 s before she goes | stripe from her to the far wall along her line to Pip | 0.8 | 9 m/s | into the **gear wall**: bonk, stunned **1.5 s gold** (hay drops cosmetically, the gears turn); into any other wall **0.8 s gold** |
   | `feathers` | fluffs up, rustle | fan × 5 (× 7 in P2), 60° | 0.6 | 8 m/s | - |
   | `doublehonk` (P2) | two inhales | two rings 0.6 s apart (two threats) | 0.7 | - | - |

   P2 at 50 %: `speedK` 1.4, cool 1.2-2.0 s. Grammar as S-105 (≥ 0.55 s, ≤ 2 threats, shrug, 1 HP, no contact damage).
2. **Arena props** (boss-def, not map glyphs, R9): four **rafter hookblooms** with pad decks 2 × 2 m at h 5.0 at tiles
   (23,27), (33,27), (23,37), (33,37) (blooms 0.5 m above each pad); four static **hay bales** at the corners (22,26),
   (34,26), (22,38), (34,38) (colliders, cover against feather fans). **The Hook trick**: if Pip drops from a pad and lands
   within 1.5 m of her during a `charge` wind-up, she flinches and charges the gear wall regardless of where Pip was (a
   guaranteed 1.5 s bonk).
3. **Model** `boss_honksworth` (R8, cast.md look): `bird` rig `beak: 'flat'` scaled 4.6 with a `tube` S-neck (node `head`
   bends in moves), feathers `#f7f4ec`, wing tips `#cfc4b0`, bill and feet `#f29b38`, **straw bonnet** `#e7c45a` (`cyl` +
   `torus` brim) with a **poppy-red ribbon** `#c2563f`, a pearl necklace `#f4f1ea` (instanced small spheres), ruffled
   feather tufts while exhausted. 3.2 m; ≤ 6,000 tris. Animations by transform: strut, rear and honk (neck × 1.3), charge,
   bonk wobble with the bonnet askew, feather fluff, sit-down dizzy.
4. **Intro and handover**: name card "Lady Honksworth" / "Her Honkship, Warden of Tumble Hill". Handover (with her tic):
   "HONK! ...Honk. Oh, goodness. Do excuse me." / "The mills stopped, so I flapped the sails myself. Day and night.
   Someone had to. One cannot have a hungry Haybury." / "Light the sun-lamp, dear child. And do address me as Your
   Honkship. I have earned it this week." Reward: the `!` tile becomes `[E] Kindle · Beacon`. `S.bosses
   ['granary_vaults:honksworth']`.
5. **After**: from the first exit after the handover she stands on the summit lane by Middle Mill
   (`threemills.honk_lane`, nearest path cell to (10, −6)) by day; Talk "Your Honkship. Yes. Thank you. Carry on."; `[E]
   Wake · Lady Honksworth` there loads r12 (S-045 flow). Festival-night hosting comes with ss-27 (S-152).
6. **Rematch** 113 HP, enraged from 75 %, 60 gems once. **Plushie** `granary_vaults:honksworth` (R32), 100 gems, desc "Felt
   bonnet. Very loud expression.", got "A Lady Honksworth plushie! Honk-tastic~". **Dream** data `{ id:
   'dream_honksworth', twist: 'goslings' }`.
7. **Bestiary** `card_boss_honksworth` (384², ≤ 35 KB, realm `threemills`): "a very large, very proud white goose wearing a
   straw bonnet with a poppy-red ribbon and a string of pearls, mid-honk, in a granary full of golden hay and wooden
   gears"; Hoot "Lady Honksworth outranks me. She has told me so." · Clover "Hop the honk! Then make her charge the big
   gear." **Portrait** `portrait_honksworth` (threemills, ≤ 55 KB, cast.md prompt).
8. **Music** `warden_honksworth` (S-140); the `granary` calm layer at the handover.
9. **Balance test** (S-045 bots) at HP 74 and 90: careful bot ≥ 90 % wins in 60-120 s losing ≤ 3 hearts; never-dodging
   ≤ 50 %.
10. Reserve "HONK" (capitalised, as her tic) and "Your Honkship" (said to her by anyone) in the charter's markers.

#### Assets

`boss_honksworth` (code-built; ART BRIEF from cast.md (batch 08) pasted; slot `// boss_honksworth: { src:
'assets/realms/threemills/models/boss_honksworth.glb', height: 3.2 },`), `portrait_honksworth`, `card_boss_honksworth`.

#### Acceptance criteria

- HP 74 / 82 / 90 by Wardens calmed (Gloop counts as calmed); every move's telegraph ≥ 0.55 s and matching; ≤ 2 threats; 1 HP per hit.
- A charge into the gear wall gives 1.5 s gold and turns the gears; elsewhere 0.8 s; the Hook drop forces a gear-wall
  charge.
- Dizzy resets as S-126; handover, after spot, Wake, rematch (113 HP, 60 gems once), plushie stock.
- Balance test passes; content lint.
- Invariants: hidden until whole, one save slot, budgets, tone, assets, IP, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (def, HP, balance suite).
- In-page boss suite: each move by name, the gear-wall bonk, the Hook drop, dizzy reset, `killBoss`, after spot, Wake.

#### Out of scope

The kindle and the sails (S-148b); ss-27 and the Harvest Cut (M18).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: a charge "to the far wall" across a 30 m room at 9 m/s lasts ≈ 3 s: the stripe is drawn for the whole length
before she moves, so it stays one threat. Rollback: revert; hidden.

<a id="s-148a"></a>

### S-148a · feat: Threemills' chain on the ground: the vole gang, the Old Sluice and the hoist

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-141, S-142, S-144, S-146 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.threemills.hidden: true` ·
Bible: [../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-chain](../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-chain),
[../bible/story.md#ch9-11](../bible/story.md#ch9-11), [../bible/systems.md#hints](../bible/systems.md#hints)

#### Why

First half of INDEX S-148 (R30): Threemills' "people" steps become playable. Win the Hoist Key back from the vole
gang, open the Old Sluice by its creaking wheels in the dark, and lift the hay off the Granary door; Lady Honksworth
storms past to her sun-lamp (story.md's line).

#### What

1. **Step 2, the vole den** (`threemills:vole-den` (−20, −100), Orchard Rows): a hollow under a hedge with a stolen-goods
   pile (buttons, a thimble, one very shiny key: a glint sprite). Before `tm:step1`: `[E] Look · Stolen-goods pile` "A
   heap of shiny things under the hedge. Something squeaks." After `tm:step1`, Pip coming within 8 m starts three
   scripted waves of one Field Vole gang each (4 members), the next 1.5 s after the previous poofs (region spawns pause
   for the fight; tokens as usual). After the third: the key hops out of the pile: `[E] Pick up · Hoist Key` →
   `give:hoist_key` (key item "Hoist House key", desc "A big iron key with a little windmill on the bow."), `set:tm:key`.
   Leaving mid-fight resets the waves (nothing lost).
2. **Step 3, the Old Sluice** (R29): the house's interior is a dark zone (walking in without the lit Lantern: "Too dark to
   see. A light would help."). With the Lantern, the three wheels on the north wall show the chalk drawings above them
   (two sails west, three middle, one east). A sword hit turns a wheel a quarter with a creak at its pitch: **one sail =
   low (C3), two = mid (G3), three = high (C4)** (`T` creaks with pitch). Hitting them in rising order (east, west,
   middle) opens the sluice: water gushes through the outlet (a flow card), the side waterwheel turns for good,
   `sfx.gearCrank`, `set:tm:sluice`. A wrong wheel: a soft "clunk" and the sequence restarts (no reset of anything else).
3. **Step 4, the hoist** (12, 34): the Hoist House door: without the key `[E] Look · Hoist House` "Locked. A note: 'Key's on
   the hook. —G.' The hook is empty."; with it `[E] Unlock · Hoist House` (opens for good, `gate:threemills:hoist`).
   Inside, `[E] Use · Hoist`: without `tm:sluice` "The hoist creaks but won't turn. No water in the sluice."; with it the
   crane arm swings over the heap and lifts the hay clear of the cellar door in 3 s (S-139b's animation hooks; the heap's
   collider turns inactive) → `set:tm:door`; the portal becomes `[E] Enter · the Granary Vaults`.
4. **Honksworth storms past** (scene `tm-honk-past`, 8 s, replayable, right after `tm:door`): `shot two at
   w:landmark:granary`; a Honksworth actor lands on the Granary roof with a thump; `say honksworth
   threemills.scene.honkpast.01` "HONK. If ANYONE is going near my sun-lamp, it is ME." (story.md's line with her tic);
   she flaps down past the cellar door and despawns inside. `after: []`.
5. **Her Honkship at night** (dim state): from `tm:step1` until `boss:granary_vaults:honksworth`, between 21:00 and
   05:00, a Honksworth actor stands on Middle Mill's gallery flapping its sails by hand (the sails turn 0.1 rad/s while
   she flaps); unhittable, unlockable; `[E] Talk · Lady Honksworth` "HONK. ...Not now, child. Sails." She is gone by day
   (napping in the Vaults: the arena placeholder).
6. **Hints** (remove `draft`): s0 "Nell can sail you anywhere on the chart." / "Use the mooring post on Nell's Jetty and
   pick Threemills." · s1 "The pie shop knows everything that happens in Haybury." / "Talk to Ma Haybury at the Pie
   Shop counter." · s2 (names the first missing part) "Voles like shiny things; sluices like being turned in order." /
   "The vole gang's den is under a hedge in Orchard Rows. Calm them and take the key." · "Light the Old Sluice House with
   the Lantern and hit the wheels from one sail to three." · s3 "The hoist runs off the sluice." / "Unlock the Hoist
   House beside the Granary and use the hoist." · s4 "Bales fill pits; gears turn bridges." / "Push hay into pits to
   cross them, and turn bridges with the cranks and the wheel in the middle." · s5 "Make her charge the big gear." / "Hop
   the honk rings, then stand in front of the gear wall so her charge hits it." · s6 "The sun-lamp is waiting." / "Walk
   to the lamp at the south end of the mill floor and press {interact}."
7. Flags: `gate:threemills:hoist`; items `hoist_key`.
8. **Mote's trace** (one row, S-090b's runner; story.md#motes-traces): append to `placeIsleStories`
   (`new RNG(40237 * 73 + 7)`) the record `threemills:mote-trace` at `near((−8, 44), 0, 6)` on the Granary's south
   apron, beside the hay heap over the cellar door: a `decal_scorch_spiral` and a `prop_scrap_lamp`.

#### Assets

Code-built: the stolen-goods pile (spheres and a ring), the hoist key model (`#5b5e66` with a windmill bow), the flow
card (reuse `fx_caustics` tinted), creak sounds. None generated.

#### Acceptance criteria

- Steps 2 and 3 complete in either order; step 4 needs both; the key and the opened sluice, door and hay stay after a
  reload; nothing is taken back (the key item stays).
- The sluice accepts only east, west, middle; wrong hits only restart the sequence.
- The night Honksworth is never hittable and leaves by day; the storm-past scene plays once (replayable).
- The objective, compass and two-part hints work for each step.
- Invariants: frozen realms (no placement changes), hidden until whole, one save slot, tone, machine.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (quest lint, sluice order as a pure function, flag registry).
- In-page chain suite: steps 1-4 by warps with `G.debug.give('lantern')`; assert flags, portal, scene.

#### Out of scope

The kindle, unhiding, the Tower beat and the chapter test (S-148b).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: scripted vole waves could exceed the region cap; they replace the region's rolls while active. Rollback: revert.

<a id="s-148c"></a>

### S-148c · feat: Follow the Honks: trail Lady Honksworth across the hedgerows to the vault entrance

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-148a, S-091b · Size: M (about 420 lines) · Save: none · Flag:
`REALMS.threemills.hidden: true` · Bible: [../bible/story.md#set-pieces](../bible/story.md#set-pieces),
[../bible/story.md#ch9-11](../bible/story.md#ch9-11), [../bible/cast.md#lady-honksworth](../bible/cast.md#lady-honksworth),
[../bible/charter.md#first-person](../bible/charter.md#first-person)

Added in review (see [Index changes](#index-changes)); **filed between S-148a and S-148b** (INDEX §1.8), while
Threemills is still hidden. It ships `js/npc-guide.js`, which S-152a then reuses.

#### Why

Part of **Threemills; the Tower unlocks**. Threemills' main-path set piece (15-25 minutes, no fail): at dusk the
exhausted goose sets off across the hedgerows to check on her vault, and Pip follows. She walks ahead (the
first-person guide rule), stopping at six hedge gaps where something is in the way; at the end she shows where her
vault is and why nobody else may lift the hay.

#### What

1. **Guide module** `js/npc-guide.js`: build it here exactly as S-152a's item 1 specifies (`makeGuide`, `guideStep`,
   `release`, `aheadOk`, the `npc.setGuide` runtime, cleared on a realm swap and resumed from a counter flag). S-152a
   then only uses it (its item 1 becomes "reuse `js/npc-guide.js` from S-148c").
2. **Data** (`mq-threemills`, step `hoist`): `before: [{ id: 'honks', until: 'flag:tm:honksFollowed', minutes: 20,
   text: 'qt.tm.honks', short: 'qt.tm.honks.short', target: 'npc:honksworth', hint: ['ht.tm.honks.n',
   'ht.tm.honks.a'] }]`. Journal "Follow Lady Honksworth across the hedgerows ({n}/6)." · compass "Follow Her
   Honkship" · nudge "She goes out at dusk. Geese are very punctual." · answer "From 17:00, wait by Middle Mill's door;
   when she sets off, follow her and clear whatever stops her at each hedge."
3. **The start** (from `tm:sluice` and `tm:key`, at 17:00-21:00, or at once with the Starsong's *Dusk*): Honksworth
   lands at Middle Mill's door: "HONK. You. Hood. I am going to look at my vault. You may come. Do not touch anything.
   Do not touch ME." She walks the route at `speed: 1.4, lead: 8` with `stops: [0..5]`.
4. **Route** (`placeIsleStories`, `new RNG(40237 * 73 + 7)`, appended rows `threemills:honks-1..6`): the F27 walk-grid
   path from Middle Mill's door to the Granary's cellar door (0, 36) is routed through the hedgerows (path cost × 0.5
   on hedge-adjacent cells); six stops at 1/7 … 6/7 of its length, each nudged ≤ 4 m to a hedge gap. Stop beats, one
   each, with the verbs the player already has (gates, stile and rope from S-139a's kit; voles from S-142):
   - 1 and 4: a field gate jammed shut: `[E] Use · Gate latch` does nothing; one Swing at the latch knocks it open.
   - 2 and 5: a Field Vole pair (S-142's gang species) pops out of the hedge: poof both (combat, shipped numbers).
   - 3: a stile too high for a goose: Pip hops it and pulls the hidden rope on the far side (`[E] Pull · Rope`), and a
     gate swings for her.
   - 6: the hay heap's edge: Honksworth stops, and Pip does nothing: she speaks (below).
   After each beat she says one line (`tm.honks.<n>`) and walks on: 1 "Hmph. Adequate." 2 "Voles. Everywhere. Like
   gossip with teeth." 3 "I can fly over it. I simply choose not to. Dignity." 4 "Hmph. Adequate again. Do not let it
   go to your hood." 5 "HONK. Thank you. That was not a thank you. It was a honk."
5. **The vault** (stop 6, scene `tm-honks-vault`, 20 s, replayable): `shot two at npc:honksworth`; she stands by the
   hay heap over the cellar door: "Under that hay is my vault. In my vault is my lamp. It went dark, and the sails
   stopped, and I have flapped them by hand for nine days." / "Nobody lifts that hay but ME. ...Unless the hoist does.
   The hoist is allowed. The hoist is staff." She flaps up to Middle Mill's gallery (her S-148a night spot).
   `after: ['set:tm:honksFollowed']`. Then the step's own text ("Lift the hay off the Granary door") shows.
6. **Resume**: leaving Threemills or Continue mid-walk resumes at the last released stop (`tm:honksStop` counter); dizzy
   (a vole pair) wakes Pip at the last stop with progress kept. **No fail.**
7. **Chapter test** (S-148b's `ch-threemills` spec): `G.debug.tod(17.5)`, `G.debug.tm.followHonks()` (walks the route
   with the real beats); expect `tm:honksFollowed`. **Debug:** `G.debug.tm.followHonks()`, `G.debug.guide.state()`.
8. **Tests** `tests/npc-guide.test.js` (pure): waits beyond `lead`; stops hold until released; speeds up when Pip is
   within `near`; `done` at the last point; `aheadOk` true for a scripted walk.

#### Assets

None new (Honksworth's model from S-147; gates and stile from S-139a's hedgerow kit, or a `box` gate and a two-step stile if the kit lacks them; the rope is a `tube`).

#### Acceptance criteria

- From `tm:key` and `tm:sluice`, at dusk, Honksworth leads six stops ahead of Pip (in view: `aheadOk` true ≥ 90 % of
  the walk in the in-page test); each stop's beat releases her; the vault scene plays and sets `tm:honksFollowed`.
- Leaving and returning resumes at the last stop; dizzy keeps progress.
- `js/npc-guide.js` passes its unit tests and the shipped Bloom parade follower is untouched (test:playthrough green).
- Invariants: *Hidden until whole*, *Frozen realms*, *Tone* (no fail; enemies poof; Pip never speaks), *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

```sh
bun test tests/npc-guide.test.js tests/quests.test.js tests/content.test.js && bun test
free -h && node tests/inpage.mjs threemills
bun run test:playthrough
```

#### Out of scope

ss-27's festival (S-152a, which reuses the guide module). The beacon and unhide (S-148b).

#### Risk & rollback

A new pure module plus data. Rollback: revert; S-152a then builds the guide module as originally written.

<a id="s-148b"></a>

### S-148b · feat: Threemills' beacon: the sails turn; Threemills opens; the Tower unlocks

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-148a, S-148c, S-147, S-095, S-128, S-030 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: unhides `threemills` · Bible:
[../bible/story.md#kindle](../bible/story.md#kindle), [../bible/story.md#beacon-3](../bible/story.md#beacon-3),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-beacon](../bible/atlas-isles.md#threemills-beacon), [../bible/systems.md#beacons](../bible/systems.md#beacons),
[../bible/atlas-home.md#spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#spots), [../bible/story.md#act-II-reactions](../bible/story.md#act-II-reactions)

#### Why

The payoff and the end of the three-isle loop: the sails turn and the wheat goes gold; if this is the third beacon,
Granny Wick says the old lamp can wake and the Tower door unlocks (`towerOpen`). Roadmap: INDEX S-148 (M17); vision
item **Threemills; the Tower unlocks**.

#### What

1. **Kindle scene** `kindle-threemills` (30 s, replayable; S-127b's shape): pov kindle at r12's `!`, `flag {
   'beacon:threemills': 'next' }`, fade to spawn `granary` (0, 44), `shot crane from w:landmark:granary at
   w:landmark:mills dur 3.0`, `emit rhythm.threemills`, `wait 2.5`, Honksworth actor on the summit lane: `say honksworth
   threemills.kindle.1` "The sails! THE SAILS! I am not crying. Geese do not cry. It is the wind." · `.2` "You may call me
   Your Honkship. Once. Out of gratitude. Do not let it go to your hood." · `sfx.crown`, crown card and toast. `after:
   ['quest:mq-threemills.done', 'set:visit:threemills']`.
2. **`rhythm.threemills`** (within 10 s, for good): t 0 the cupola lights gold (glass `#fff1c4`) and throws a soft 40 m
   beam straight up (additive cylinder, 1 s); t 0-5 s the three mills release their brakes (the brake bars swing off)
   and the sails spin up 0 → 0.5 rad/s, each mill a third of a turn out of phase; t 0-8 s the wheat goes from `#8fbf4a`
   to `#e7c45a` spreading outward from Tumble Hill at 30 m/s (`uWheat` per instance by distance from (0, −8):
   `clamp((t·30 − d) / 20, 0, 1)`), done by 8 s everywhere in view; Haybury's chimneys smoke (puff cards); music layer 1
   (the jig, fiddle in); the mill creak joins the ambience; the positional landmark sound: the mills' creak-and-whoosh in
   a three-beat pattern audible 250 m. On entry with the flag set: built in the restored state.
3. **Home**: crown lantern (S-095), horizon card `d` night windows (bearing 245°), the distant `beaconLit` bell on the next
   arrival; **plaza visitor** (R26) Ma Poppy Haybury at `home:spot-visit-threemills` (bearing 240°, r 12-15) 08:00-20:00
   with a pie basket, lines by day index: "Mind the pies! I brought one for your Tree. She can't eat it, so I'll have
   to." · "Pies are not rationed any more, and I want everyone to know." · "Your village is lovely. Your pies are...
   absent. I'll fix that."
4. **Beacon 3** (story.md#beacon-3), on the kindle scene's end when `beacons == 3` (whichever isle is third):
   toast "✿ Three lights on the water. Granny Wick will want to know."; Granny Wick's next Talk plays scene
   `beacon3-wick` (20 s, replayable): Wick "Three lights. That's enough to wake the old lamp." · "The Tower door opened by
   itself this morning. Ticking's stopped too. Something up there is waiting for you, dear." → `set:towerOpen`. The Tower
   door's lock state clears (its lock plate drops, the door stands ajar, its frame glows `#ffd27a`); compass "The
   Lamplighters' Tower". **Until the Tower realm ships (S-162)**, Granny Wick stands at the open door at all hours with
   `[E] Talk · Granny Wick` "Hm. Hm. Not yet, dear. Let me find my good candle first. The Tower's waited a hundred years;
   it can wait while I look." and the objective reads "The Lamplighters' Tower (to be continued ✿)". (S-162/S-165
   remove this hold.) The `b3` reaction lines of story.md#act-II-reactions become every villager's top line (Shellby "He
   was such a bright little thing. Ask me again another time, dear. I'm not ready." · Ribbit "Three lights! I've written a
   speech. It's eleven pages. You may be busy." · Miso "Three beacons! Purr-fect. I'm stocking lamp oil, just in case~"
   · Clover "Three! Three lights! I'm counting them every night. My mum says that's not a hobby." · Hoot "My star count is
   down another seven. I've checked my spectacles. They are fine." · Digby "The roots are humming louder. Three hums now.
   I'd call that a chord." · Nell "Three lights out there. The sea feels less big.").
5. **II.M on Threemills**: if Threemills is the second isle, S-128's scene plays at the granary cupola anchor
   (`w:beacon.rim` = the cupola's top ring, h 24.8 + 15.5); verify with `iim-pine-three`.
6. **Unhide**: `REALMS.threemills.hidden = false`; chart; `mq-threemills.start = 'flag:ch8'`; chapter banner; the
   Completion page shows the Threemills group.
7. **Chapter tests**: `ch-threemills` (start = `ch-archwater` end; the isle script as `ch-archwater`'s with Threemills
   anchors: talk Ma Poppy, scene.skip, the vole den (`killBoss`-style `G.debug.actors.clear()` after warping in, then
   pick up the key), the sluice wheels in order, the Hoist House unlock and hoist, `scene.skip`, enter, `give hook`,
   `killBoss granary_vaults:honksworth`, kindle, `scene.skip`, sail to Duskwick, talk Granny Wick, `scene.skip`).
   Expect `beacon:threemills: 3`, `towerOpen: true`, `moteNamed: true`, `lanterns: 3`, `quests { 'mq-threemills':
   'done' }`, `items { hook: 1, glider: 1, tide_bell: 1 }`. And `iim-pine-three` (start = `ch-pinewhistle` end; Threemills
   second): expect `moteNamed` and the scene's anchor realm `threemills`.

#### Assets

None new (beam cylinder, smoke puffs from `fx_atlas`).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Within 10 s of kindling: cupola lit, sails turning in phase offsets, wheat gold in view by 8 s, jig playing; all stay.
- Crown lanterns +1; horizon card d lit at night; Ma Poppy at the plaza 08:00-20:00.
- At `beacons == 3` (any order) the toast plays and Wick's scene sets `towerOpen` exactly once; Wick holds the door until
  S-162; no Tower realm is enterable yet.
- `REALMS.threemills.hidden === false`; `ch-threemills` and `iim-pine-three` pass; the chain check passes.
- Invariants: frozen home island, frozen realms (Threemills frozen from now on), one save slot, budgets, tone, IP,
  machine; release state: no act card ahead of content (`act2` waits for S-165).

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (registry, quest lint, chain check).
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch-threemills`, `node tests/chapter.mjs iim-pine-three`, plus `ch-archwater`, `iim-pine-arch`,
  `iim-arch-pine`.
- In-page: wheat spread timing (sampled colours at 2, 5, 8 s at 3 distances); home visitor.
- `bun run test:smoke`, `bun run test:playthrough`, `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

The six-order test (S-150); the Tower (M19); Threemills side stories (M18).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: `towerOpen` before the Tower exists: the Granny Wick hold keeps the game releasable. Rollback: revert; `towerOpen`
in saves is harmless (the hold reads it).

<a id="s-149"></a>

### S-149 · feat: Threemills' three Hollows

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-144, S-073, S-145, S-107 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [../bible/dungeons.md#h11](../bible/dungeons.md#h11),
[../bible/dungeons.md#h12](../bible/dungeons.md#h12), [../bible/dungeons.md#h13](../bible/dungeons.md#h13)

#### Why

Three one-room puzzles on Threemills built from the Vaults' mechanics: hookblooms, a gear-bridge and hay bales; two
petals and a gem bag.

#### What

1. Realms `hollow_threemills_1..3` (kind `hollow`, grid 48 m, parent `threemills`), maps verbatim; doors from S-144:
   (−20, −200) → `_1`, (−70, −30) → `_2`, (96, 104) → `_3`; toast names; solved door lamps.
2. **H11 The Thistle Hollow** (Hook, petal): hook the bloom (5,4) from (5,8), land on the island (5,5); hook (1,1) from the
   island, land (2,2), take the petal (1,2); the stair warp (2,1) ↔ (1,9) returns Pip. Hint "Thistles on islands." /
   "Hook the thistle on the middle island, then the one in the corner. The little stair takes you back."
3. **H12 The Millwheel Hollow** (no tool, gem bag): gear-bridge (5,5) len 2 starts `EW`; crank (8,8). Crank to `NS`, walk to
   the hub, use the wheel (`EW`), walk east to the alcove column (col 8) and north to the bag (8,1). Hint "Turn it, then
   ride it." / "Crank the bridge round, walk to the middle, turn the wheel, then walk east and north to the bag."
4. **H13 The Haystack Hollow** (no tool, petal): bales (2,6), (5,6), (8,6); a 2-row pit (rows 3-4); (5,6) N ×2 fills
   (5,4); (8,6) W ×3 then N ×3 fills (5,3); lever (1,9). Hint "Two bales in one column." / "Fill the middle column of the
   pit with two bales and walk across."
5. Prizes: H11 `collect.petal['hollow_threemills_1:p1']`, H13 `collect.petal['hollow_threemills_3:p1']`, H12 60 gems;
   `collect.hollow['hollow_threemills_1']` … `['hollow_threemills_3']` (realm ids, as S-071's `normalize` requires).
6. Solver per Hollow (H11 `needsOnEntry: ['hook']`; the lever covers H13); the progression solver lists H11 under `hook`.

#### Assets

None new (S-145/S-146 skins).

#### Acceptance criteria

Maps equal dungeons.md; compile, solve, prints; prizes once; Completion's Threemills Hollows row 3/3; realm suite
clean; invariants (frozen realms, one save slot, budgets, tone, machine).

#### Verification plan

`bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/hollows.test.js`; the in-page Hollow suite solving each; `node tests/inpage.mjs
realm`.

#### Out of scope

The Rootdeep's Hollows (S-186).

#### Risk & rollback

Low. Rollback: revert; doors fall back to the Look line.

<a id="s-149b"></a>

### S-149b · feat: Threemills's Hollows get their second stage, and The Millwheel Hollow becomes a Deep Hollow

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-149, S-090b · Size: M (200-500; mostly map data) · Save: none · Flag: the land's Hollow realms keep the land's hidden flag · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [../bible/dungeons.md#h12](../bible/dungeons.md#h12), [../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs](../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs), [../bible/story.md#motes-traces](../bible/story.md#motes-traces)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md §7 and R15, CANON §11.1 A3; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after S-149.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M17 · Threemills; the Tower unlocks**. Every Hollow was a one-step room that played in 2-3 minutes and all 24 shared one
shape. Now every Hollow has two stages with the prize behind the second, and one Hollow per land is a three-room
**Deep Hollow** (15-20 min) that ends in a reading nook with a Lamp Log and a note from the Lamp Thief, so the
optional bucket of CANON §11 is real play and each land has one hidden place worth remembering.

#### What
1. **The reliquary** (if S-093b has not added it yet, this step does; later steps reuse it): entity `reliquary: { when:
   [...] }` on a Hollow's prize tile; a code-built cage of four curved roots `#6f5a44` with gold buds `#ffe9a8`, 1.0 m,
   ≤ 120 tris; it opens in 1.0 s with `chime_up` when its condition holds; its state lives in
   `S.dungeons['<hollow>'].stage` until the prize is taken, then is dropped (the shipped Hollow rule). The solver proves
   stage 1, then stage 2, then the prize.
2. **Stage 2 for H11 The Thistle Hollow, H13 The Haystack Hollow** (copy each Hollow's **Stage 2** line from [dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows)):
   - **H11 The Thistle Hollow** (`hollow_threemills_1`): crank `y` (9,8) on the south floor winds the cage open; turn it before hooking; condition `crank`.
   - **H13 The Haystack Hollow** (`hollow_threemills_3`): the cage hangs 3 m up on a sack rope; hook the hookbloom `h` (9,1) on its pulley to lower it; condition `hooked`.
3. **The Deep Hollow H12 The Millwheel Hollow** (`hollow_threemills_2`): replace its one-room data with the 31 × 11 composite map
   from [dungeons.md#h12](../bible/dungeons.md#h12) (copy, never paraphrase; realm grid 78 m). Room 1 is the old
   puzzle (stage 1) with a door (10,1) where its prize stood. **Room 2 The Sail Loft**: hook the hookbloom (16,3) from the west floor and land on the island (15,3)-(16,3); walk the gear-bridge (hub (15,6), length 5, starts `NS`) to its hub, turn the hub wheel to `EW`, walk east; exit door (20,9). **Room 3 The Reading
   Nook**: the prize on its pedestal (25,1); **Lamp Log 11** on a page pinned to stone (23,3) (`Read · Lamp Log`;
   it moves here from its atlas slot in Threemills: edit that land's finds data so the slot is skipped, update the
   atlas row and run `node tools/realm-stats.mjs`; land and game totals unchanged); a scrap jam-jar lamp (`prop_scrap_lamp`
   from S-090b, or its fallback) holding a folded note (27,3), `[E] Read · Note`: "Wheels go round and come back. I would like to come back too, one day. — M" (text id
   `lore.hollow.threemills`, ≤ 220 characters; it never names him, the charter spoiler rule; reading it adds the Lore
   entry "A note in a Hollow" and sets `trace:hollow:threemills`; if story.md's traces define this land's note, its
   text wins); and its own exit `X` (25,10) back to the spiral door.
4. **Hints** (Ask the Tree): one pair per stage-2 line (nudge = the condition's object, plain = the line), and for the
   Deep Hollow's room 2: "Hook to the island, then turn the bridge under your feet." / "Hook the thistle on the island, walk down the gear-bridge to its middle, turn the wheel and walk east.".
5. `STATS` is regenerated (`node tools/realm-stats.mjs`): same Hollow ids, same prizes, the log's new position.

#### Assets
- `int_reliquary` (code-built as above; ART BRIEF: a small cage of four curved roots with gold buds over a stone
  pedestal; 1.0 m; palette `#6f5a44` `#8a5a3c` `#ffe9a8`; toon bark; 120 / 500 tris; it opens like a flower; image prompt
  "<style line> a small cage of four curved tree roots with tiny gold buds over a mossy stone pedestal, cosy"; 3D prompt
  "stylised low-poly root cage over a stone pedestal, hand-painted, under 500 triangles, 1.0 m"). Fallback: four bent
  cylinders. The Deep Hollow reuses the land's Hollow kit and the scrap lamp; no new images.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the edited Hollow blocks (16 `+` and 8 `*` still counted across the Hollows);
  data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves each Hollow's two stages and the Deep Hollow's three rooms in order, with no soft-lock, using only
  the tools listed in the Hollow table.
- In page: a bot solves each stage-2 Hollow and the Deep Hollow; reading the note sets `trace:hollow:threemills`; the
  Lamp Log counts once; prizes and `collect.hollow` keys are unchanged.
- Content lint (≤ 220 characters, no spoiler name). Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (Hollow realms only; the land's
  overworld prints are untouched except the log slot, refreshed in this PR before the land is unhidden or, if it is
  already unhidden, by a finds-data change that the prints test lists), *One save slot*, *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/stats.test.js tests/content.test.js`.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `hollows_threemills`).

#### Out of scope
- New prizes or Hollows; the land's other finds.

#### Risk & rollback
Data changes inside Hollow realms plus one log slot. Revert the PR: the one-stage Hollows and the log's atlas slot come
back.

<a id="s-150"></a>

### S-150 · test: all six Act II isle orders reach the Tower

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-148b, S-108, S-030 · Size: S · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/systems.md#test-harness](../bible/systems.md#test-harness), [../bible/story.md#II.M](../bible/story.md#II.M),
[../bible/story.md#beacon-3](../bible/story.md#beacon-3), [../bible/systems.md#abilities](../bible/systems.md#abilities)

#### Why

CANON §12 rule 7: plot is keyed to counts, so every isle order must play the same beats. This proves it for all six
orders and keeps it proven in CI.

#### What

1. **Shared isle scripts**: move each isle's compressed steps (from `ch-pinewhistle`, `ch-archwater`, `ch-threemills`)
   into `tests/chapters/isle-pinewhistle.steps.json`, `isle-archwater.steps.json`, `isle-threemills.steps.json`; add an
   `include` verb to `tests/chapter.mjs` (`{ "do": "include", "file": "isle-archwater.steps.json" }`) if it lacks one; the
   three existing chapter specs use the includes (no behaviour change).
2. **Six specs** `act2-order-pat`, `-pta`, `-apt`, `-atp`, `-tpa`, `-tap` (p Pinewhistle, a Archwater, t Threemills), each
   starting from the `ch8` end fixture and running the three includes in its order, then `sail duskwick`, `talk
   granny_wick`, `scene.skip`. After each isle an `assert` step checks the count beats:
   - after isle 1: `flag:beacon:<isle1>=1`, `lanterns>=1`, `flag:beat:beacon1`, `!flag:moteNamed`;
   - after isle 2: `flag:beacon:<isle2>=2`, `flag:moteNamed`, `flag:scene:iim-mote-named`;
   - after isle 3 and Wick: `flag:beacon:<isle3>=3`, `flag:towerOpen`.
   Expect at the end: `lanterns: 3`, all three `mq-*` done, items `glider`, `tide_bell`, `hook`; and from
   `G.debug.scene.log`: `iim-mote-named` played **exactly once**, in the realm of isle 2 (add `scene.log` to the S-034
   debug hooks if missing: an array of `{ id, realm, at }`).
3. **CI**: six matrix jobs `chapter:act2-order-*` (each ≤ 15 min); locally run one at a time.
4. **Solver**: a `bun test` case runs `progress-graph` from the `ch8` fixture six times with the isle order forced (edges
   of the other two isles disabled until their turn) and asserts each isle's critical path needs only `lantern`, the
   sword and its own tool (pacing rule 6) in every order.

#### Assets

None.

#### Acceptance criteria

All six order specs pass locally and in CI; II.M fires once per run at the second isle; `towerOpen` at the third; the
solver order test passes; the existing chapter specs still pass after the include refactor.

#### Verification plan

`bun test`; `node tests/chapter.mjs act2-order-pat` … `act2-order-tap` (one at a time, `free -h` ≥ 6 GB first);
`node tests/chapter.mjs ch-pinewhistle` / `ch-archwater` / `ch-threemills`.

#### Out of scope

The six Stair Stone orders (S-256).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: CI time; each spec warps and uses `killBoss`, so a run is a few minutes. Rollback: revert (tests only).

<a id="s-151"></a>

### S-151 · perf: checkpoint 4: four isles, save size and swap soak; close Threemills

Milestone M17 · Depends on: S-148b, S-149, S-150 (and every M17 step), S-001, S-146b, S-149b · Size: S · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/charter.md#budgets](../bible/charter.md#budgets), [../bible/charter.md#budgets-over](../bible/charter.md#budgets-over),
[../bible/charter.md#close-out-perf](../bible/charter.md#close-out-perf), [../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone](../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone),
[../bible/systems.md#realm-swap](../bible/systems.md#realm-swap)

#### Why

Performance checkpoint 4 of 8: with home and four isles, their dungeons, Hollows and interiors, prove the budgets and
that swapping realms leaks nothing; then close M17 (vision item **Threemills; the Tower unlocks**).

#### What

1. **Swap soak** (`js/debug/tests/soak.js`, new in-page suite `soak`, CI job too): from home, cycle 10 times through
   `home → duskwick → pinewhistle → whistlehollow → pinewhistle → archwater → undertow_grotto → archwater →
   threemills → granary_vaults → threemills → home` (plus one entry into each interior and Hollow on cycle 1) using
   `G.debug.realm`; after each full cycle record `renderer.info.memory` (geometries, textures), `renderer.info.programs
   .length`, `performance.memory.usedJSHeapSize` (Chromium) and each swap's time split (teardown, generate, build, warm).
   Pass: geometries and textures back to the cycle-1 baseline ± 2; programs not growing after cycle 1; JS heap growth
   ≤ 10 MB from cycle 2 to cycle 10; generation ≤ 1.2 s per overworld, ≤ 150 ms per dungeon, < 100 ms back to a resident
   parent. Run it under a memory cap (SwiftShader runs reach 4-5 GB): `systemd-run --user --scope -p MemoryMax=7G node
   tests/inpage.mjs soak`.
2. **Views**: every shipped realm's 4 fixed views in both cameras (`G.debug.perf.views(realm)`): main ≤ 200 calls, total
   ≤ 300, ≤ 1.3 M tris; locally the main-pass GPU median and p90. Fix what fails in the charter's
   [order](../bible/charter.md#budgets-over) (in this PR if under ~500 lines, else file the next step naming the view).
3. **Save size**: the `act2-order-pat` end fixture's JSON size (target well under the 80 KB cap at 100 %; record it).
4. **Precache**: core MB and the `duskwick`, `pinewhistle`, `archwater`, `threemills` bundles (`node
   tools/build-pages.mjs`); each bundle ≤ 2.5 MB, core ≤ 30 MB.
5. **Close M17** (charter close-out steps 1-10): the suite in order (chapters `ch8`, `ch-pinewhistle`, `ch-archwater`,
   `ch-threemills`, `iim-*`, `act2-order-*`); `docs/DESIGN.md` gains the **Act II isles table** (land · recipe · size ·
   seed · regions · landmark · tool · dungeon · Warden · chests / keepsakes / acorns / vistas / Hollows / benches · hours
   built) and the §14 performance table's worst view; `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` gains the hook (`player.mods.pull`), hay
   bales, gear-bridges, the soak suite; `docs/roadmap/progress.md` entry (template + the checkpoint numbers); bible edits
   for M17's reconciliations (R6, R9, R17, R21, R26, R29, R30, R32), and the Hollow save-key fix: in
   `docs/roadmap/bible/dungeons.md#hollows` the save line reads `collect.hollow['hollow_<land>_<n>']` (the Hollow's
   realm id; petals `collect.petal['hollow_<land>_<n>:p<k>']`), never `'<realm>:<n>'`, matching S-071's `normalize`
   and the keys S-129 and S-149 write. Add a `tests/docs.test.js` or content-lint check that no step or bible file
   writes `collect.hollow['<land>:` (a regex over `docs/roadmap/**/*.md`).
6. No brief batch (batch 08 is S-159).

#### Assets

None.

#### Acceptance criteria

The soak passes the leak and time thresholds; every view of every shipped realm is within budget in both cameras (or a
named follow-up step is filed for an over-budget view, with the numbers); save size and precache recorded; all M17
steps stacked or merged; the close-out records exist; `bun test tests/docs.test.js` passes.

#### Verification plan

`free -h` (≥ 6 GB), then one at a time: `bun test`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs` + `bun run test:pwa`; `bun run
test:smoke`; `bun run test:opening`; `bun run test:playthrough`; every CI in-page suite including `realm` and `soak`;
every chapter spec. Numbers in the PR body and `progress.md`.

#### Out of scope

Checkpoint 5 (S-196).

#### Risk & rollback

Risk: a leak found here may need a fix bigger than this PR; file it as the next step and keep M17 open until it lands.
Rollback: revert (measurement and docs).
